# NeuralOps — Architecture Image Generation Prompts

Paper-grade image-generation prompts for all **220 canonical registered modules** (142 attentions + 78 compression engines) of the NeuralOps framework (`Codes/framework/attentions/`, `Codes/framework/compression_engine/`).

Every prompt set was generated directly from the module's source code — class names, tensor shapes, formulas, and mechanism descriptions reflect the real implementation, not generic placeholders. Canonical module names/aliases follow `scratch/image_prompts/manifest.json` (alias-deduplicated registry resolution; counts reproducible via `scripts/count_modules.py`).

Each module carries at least five self-contained prompts:
1. **Base Architecture Diagram** — full schematic with data flow and shape annotations
2. **Mathematical Core** — key formulas as large LaTeX with per-term visual annotation
3. **Tensor Flow & Shape Trace** — step-by-step shape-annotated pipeline
4. **Unique Mechanism Close-up** — zoom on the module's distinguishing idea
5. **System Context & Complexity** — placement in a transformer layer + complexity inset

Flagship modules (ASVD, Linformer, Mamba, MinimalSVD, Nyström, Performer, SVD-Linear, AASVD, FLAP, Hybrid-SVD, SliceGPT, SparseGPT, Subspace Projection, Swift-SVD) additionally include **Paper Comparison Figure** and **Attention/KV- or Spectrum-Visualization** prompts.

All prompts share one style block (flat vector, #60A5FA/#A78BFA palette, engineering grid, 16:9) so figures generated across the set look consistent in the paper.

---

## Module: adaptive_span (attention; aliases: adaptive_span, adaptive_span_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Adaptive Span Attention (Sukhbaatar 2019) for a transformer layer, left-to-right data flow. Input token row [B, N, D] enters Q/K/V projection blocks drawn as rounded rectangles, each a LoRALinear (frozen base W plus a thin parallel purple LoRA branch A,B of rank r). Heads split via reshape into [B, H, N, d]. A trapezoidal "adaptive span mask" panel over the score matrix [B, H, N, N] shows a learnable span length [w, b] per head: each head's attention window narrows to its learned span w with a soft ramp, drawn as shrinking shaded bands per head row. Score formula label \(S_{ij} = Q_i K_j^\top / \sqrt{d}\), causal tril mask and padding mask icons feeding masked_fill nodes at −10000, softmax, then weighted sum over V and output projection back to [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Single centered panel, large legible LaTeX equations for adaptive-span attention with each term visually annotated: \(S_{ij} = \frac{Q_i K_j^\top}{\sqrt{d}}\); span masking \(S_{ij} = -\infty\) for \(j < i - w_h\); adaptive span window \(w_h\) learned per head h with soft ramp \(R(z)\); softmax \(P = \mathrm{softmax}(S)\); output \(O = P V\). Around each equation, small graphics: Q and K as small blue heatmap grids with a √d divider, the span mask as a lower-triangular banded matrix with purple shaded windows of varying width per head row, −10000 masked cells as light gray hatched squares, V values grid, and a small LoRA branch diagram \(y = W x + B(A x)\) with rank r annotation. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Horizontal pipeline diagram tracing exact tensor shapes from the code: hidden_states [B, N, D] → q_proj/k_proj/v_proj (LoRALinear) → view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; K and V use separate num_kv_heads (GQA) [B, H_kv, N_k, d]; scores matmul → [B, H, N_q, N_k] labeled ×scale; two masked_fill nodes labeled "padding mask" and "causal tril mask" at −10000; position ids arange(N)→unsqueeze→expand [B, N] feeding a RoPE node; softmax → [B, H, N_q, N_k]; matmul with V → [B, H, N_q, d] → transpose back → merge heads → out proj [B, N, D]. Each arrow annotated with its shape in small mono font; the score cube drawn as the large memory block. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Zoomed cutaway of the per-head adaptive span selection: a vertical stack of H head rows, each showing a triangular causal score matrix where a purple window of learnable width w_h slides along the diagonal; a small per-head span-length bar chart on the right showing learned spans of different sizes; a soft ramp function R(z) curve transitioning inside-zero to outside-zero over a narrow band; an annotation "spans are learned parameters, initialized to max_span and contracted during training." Include a tiny inset showing cost dropping from O(N²) to O(N·w) as w ≪ N. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** One transformer encoder/decoder block shown as a vertical stack (LN → attention → residual add → LN → MLP → residual), with the standard multi-head self-attention block replaced by a highlighted "Adaptive Span Attention" block containing mini Q/K/V LoRA projections, span-mask cube, softmax. Four small architecture badges underneath: DistilBERT (flat q_lin/k_lin/v_lin), RoBERTa/BERT (nested self-attn), GPT-2 (causal, fused Conv1D), Qwen/LLaMA (GQA + partial RoPE). Bottom-right inset: two curves over sequence length N — quadratic red-dashed curve for full attention O(N²) versus linear-in-w blue curve O(N·w) for adaptive span, w ≪ N. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: adaptive_sparse (attention; aliases: adaptive_sparse, adaptive_sparse_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Adaptive Sparse Attention, left-to-right: input [B, N, D] → LoRALinear Q/K/V projections (frozen blue base, thin purple LoRA branch rank r) → head reshape to [B, H, N, d] with GQA grouping (H query heads share H_kv KV heads, drawn as grouped wires) → score matmul [B, H, N_q, N_k] ×scale → a two-stage sparsity panel: (a) dynamic top-k selector keeping the k largest scores per query row, (b) threshold gate drawn as a small dial producing a per-head threshold tensor [1, H, 1, 1]; RoPE rotary node on Q/K; padding and causal tril masks as two gray hatched mask icons into masked_fill(−10000); sparse softmax (zeros outside kept entries drawn as white cells) → weighted sum over V → merge heads → output projection [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel for adaptive sparse attention: \(S = \frac{QK^\top}{\sqrt{d}}\); sparsity \(P_{ij} = 0\) if \(S_{ij} < \tau_h\) or \(S_{ij} \notin \mathrm{TopK}_k\); \(\tau_h\) a learned threshold broadcast per head shown as tensor [1, H, 1, 1] next to a [B, H, N, N] score cube; softmax over surviving entries; \(O = PV\). Annotate: top-k selector drawn as each matrix row sorted with k purple kept cells and gray dropped cells; a sparsity_ratio parameter dial controlling k; RoPE rotation glyph \(q' = R_\theta q\); LoRA equation \(y = Wx + B(Ax)\) rank r. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape-annotated pipeline: [B, N, D] → q_proj → view(B, N_q, H, d) → transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N_q, d]; k_proj/v_proj → [B, H_kv, N_k, d] (GQA note); scores [B, H, N_q, N_k]; threshold broadcast [1, H, 1, 1] → [B, H, N_q, N_k]; masked_fill nodes (padding, causal) at −10000; topk mask same shape as boolean; softmax → sparse [B, H, N_q, N_k] drawn mostly white; @V → [B, H, N_q, d] → back-transpose → [B, N, D]. A side gauge shows nonzeros fraction = sparsity_ratio. Mono-font shape labels on every arrow; score cube highlighted as the memory hotspot, with a scissors icon where top-k cuts it. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the dual adaptive sparsity selector: left, a score matrix row being sorted with the k largest entries kept (purple) and the rest grayed; right, a learned per-head threshold τ_h dial ([1, H, 1, 1]) applied as a horizontal cutoff line across the row's values; below, the union of both criteria drawn as a Venn diagram (top-k ∩ above-threshold) producing the final sparse pattern. Inset: three example sparsity patterns for sparsity_ratio 0.1/0.3/0.5. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer block stack with the attention block swapped for highlighted "Adaptive Sparse Attention" (LoRA Q/K/V, top-k + threshold mask, RoPE). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2 causal, Qwen/LLaMA GQA+RoPE. Inset complexity chart: dense O(N²) red dashed vs adaptive sparse O(N·k) blue with k ≪ N; a small bar showing KV memory saving from GQA grouping (H → H_kv). ## Module: adaptive_svd (attention; aliases: adaptive_svd, adaptive_svd_attention, adaptive_svd_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Adaptive SVD Attention with residual adapters. Left-to-right: input [B, N, D] → three factorized projection blocks labeled AdaptiveSVDLinear: inside each, a frozen blue rank-r SVD path drawn as two thin matrices U [out, r] and V [r, in] (weights W = U·V reconstructed), plus a trainable purple residual branch with lora_A [res, in], lora_B [out, res], the two paths summing at a ⊕ node. Head split → [B, H, N, D] with GQA (H_kv KV heads); RoPE node on Q/K; scores [B, H, N_q, N_k] ×scale; causal tril and padding masks (−10000); softmax; @V; merge; output factorized projection → [B, N, D]. Callout: "rank chosen by cumulative singular-energy threshold". Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: SVD \(W \approx U_r V_r\) with \(U_r \in \mathbb{R}^{d_{out}\times r}\), \(V_r \in \mathbb{R}^{r\times d_{in}}\); energy-based rank selection \(\min r\) s.t. \(\sum_{i\le r}\sigma_i^2 / \sum_i \sigma_i^2 \ge\) energy_threshold; weight assembly \(W = U V + B A\) drawn as three grids joining at a plus; forward \(y = xW^\top\); attention \(S = QK^\top/\sqrt{d}\), \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(S)V\). Each matrix drawn as a small grid: U and V thin blue strips, lora_A/lora_B thin purple strips, frozen vs trainable marked with a lock vs flame icon. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace pipeline: [B, N, D] → AdaptiveSVDLinear (U: [out, svd_rank], V: [svd_rank, in]; residual lora_B [out, residual_rank] × lora_A [residual_rank, in]) → [B, N, D] → view(B, N_q, H, D).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N_q, D]; K,V via num_kv_heads → [B, H_kv, N_k, D]; scores [B, H, N_q, N_k] × scale; masked_fill causal −10000; softmax; @V → [B, H, N_q, D] → merge → factorized out-proj → [B, N, D]. Weight-reconstruction node shown once: W = matmul(U, V) then W += matmul(lora_B, lora_A), annotated "rebuilt each forward; LoRA params carry lora_A/lora_B names for optimizer filters." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of energy-thresholded rank selection: a descending singular value spectrum bar chart σ₁…σₙ with cumulative-energy curve rising to the energy_threshold horizontal line; the cut rank r marked where the curve crosses, σ-tail drawn fading to gray; beside it, the resulting U/V thin factor strips versus the original full W grid, with parameter count ratio labeled (e.g., r(d_in+d_out) vs d_in·d_out). A small trainable-residual adapter slides in as a purple parallelogram next to the frozen blue base. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with all four attention projections replaced by highlighted AdaptiveSVDLinear blocks (each showing U·V+BA); residual MLP left uncompressed, grayed. Badges: DistilBERT flat MHA, RoBERTa/BERT nested, GPT-2 Conv1D fused c_attn (weight splitting note), Qwen/LLaMA GQA+partial RoPE. Inset: parameter count vs rank r curve and reconstruction error vs r (relative Frobenius ‖W−UV‖/‖W‖) decreasing. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: adasplash (attention; aliases: adasplash, adasplash_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of AdaSplash Attention: adaptive splash radius with α-entmax sparse activations. Left-to-right: [B, N, D] → AdaSplashLinear Q/K/V (frozen base + purple LoRA residual, rank r) → heads [B, H, N, d], GQA wiring, RoPE on Q/K → splash-radius local attention panel: a diagonal band of width splash_radius per head over the [B, H, N_q, N_k] score matrix (blue band with soft purple edges), plus a global token-routing side path where a few "landmark" tokens connect everywhere; α-entmax block labeled with iteration count n_iter (fixed-point loop drawn as a circular arrow) producing exactly-zero entries (white cells) unlike softmax; NaN-guard node (nan_to_num) and valid-mask masked_fill(−10000); output @V → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: scores \(S = QK^\top/\sqrt{d}\); α-entmax \(P = \mathrm{entmax}_\alpha(S)\) with \(P = 0\) entries shown as white cells in a small matrix (sparse by construction, α shown on a slider between 1=softmax and 2=sparsemax); local mask \(S_{ij}=-\infty\) for \(|i-j| > w\) with splash radius w per head; fixed-point iteration glyph \(p^{(t+1)} = T_\alpha(S p^{(t)})\) × n_iter; routing weights over global tokens; \(O = PV\). Include softmax-vs-entmax mini-plot: softmax curve all-positive vs entmax curve hitting exact zero. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape pipeline: [B, N, D] → AdaSplashLinear → view/transpose → [B, H, N_q, d] (K,V [B, H_kv, N_k, d]); scores [B, H, N_q, N_k]; valid_mask build → masked_fill(−10000) and masked_fill(0) nodes; float upcast node → softmax/entmax in fp32 → nan_to_num → cast back (numerical-safety path drawn as a small shield icon); p.masked_fill(~valid, 0); min-threshold computation via scaled.masked_fill(+10000) → splash radius band mask; @V → [B, H, N_q, d] → [B, N, D]. Annotate dtype transitions fp16→fp32→fp16 on the entmax loop. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the "splash" pattern: a 2-D token grid where a query token (purple dot) attends with high weight inside an elliptical splash radius (blue gradient-free shaded ellipse) and to a handful of routed global tokens (thin arrows); three radius sizes shown for different heads; beside it the α-entmax fixed-point loop as a circular diagram with n_iter marked and exact-zero probabilities drawn as white cells appearing after each iteration. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer block with AdaSplash attention highlighted inside (LoRA Q/K/V + splash band + entmax loop). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset cost chart: full O(N²) dashed vs splash O(N·w + N·g) (w = radius, g = global tokens) and a note "entmax adds n_iter passes but yields exact sparsity." ## Module: aegis_hybrid (attention; aliases: aegis_hybrid, aegis_hybrid_attention, aegis_hybrid_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Aegis-Hybrid Attention: three parallel branches fusing local precision and global reach. Input [B, N, D] → FactorizedLinear Q/K/V blocks (each drawn as frozen blue two-layer rank-r path: lin1 [D→r] → lin2 [r→D], plus purple LoRA lora_A/lora_B branch summed at ⊕; equivalent weight W = lin2·lin1 + lora_B·lora_A) → heads [B, H, N, Dh] with GQA [B, H_kv, N_k, Dh] → three branches: (1) Local sliding-window attention over band mask [B, 1, N, N_k]; (2) True O(N) causal linear attention using cumulative-sum trick (cumsum icon); (3) bidirectional O(N) linear attention for encoders; token-importance pruning gate selecting top tokens for the global branch; learned gating fusion node combining branch outputs (three input wires, weights via sigmoid); merge heads → factorized output projection → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: factorized projection \(W = W_2 W_1 + B A\), \(y = xW^\top\); local scores \(S^{loc} = QK^\top/\sqrt{d}\) with band mask \(|i-j|\le w\); global causal linear attention \(y_t = \frac{\sum_{j\le t}\phi(K_j)^\top V_j}{\sum_{j\le t}\phi(K_j)^\top \phi(Q_t)}\) with cumulative-sum implementation annotated "O(N) via cumsum"; bidirectional variant with full-sum denominator; gating \(g = \sigma(W_g[h; x])\), \(O = g\,O_{loc} + (1-g)\,O_{glob}\); importance pruning keeping top-m tokens. φ shown as small kernel-map block. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → FactorizedLinear (lin1: D→rank, lin2: rank→D; lora_A [r, in], lora_B [out, r]) → [B, N, D] → view(B, N, H, Dh).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, Dh]; K/V [B, H_kv, N_k, Dh]; local scores [B, H, N, N_k] masked by band_mask [B,1,N,N_k] broadcast → [B,1,1,N,N_k] note; linear branch via phi and cumsum producing numerator [B, H, Dh, Dh] and denominator [B, H, N, 1]; gate logits [B, N, H, 1] via sigmoid permute; fused [B, H, N, Dh] → transpose back → [B, N, H·Dh] → out proj → [B, N, D]. Memory-hotspot highlight moves from the [B,H,N,N] local cube (small, banded) versus dense cube ghost outline. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the gating fusion and token pruning: left, per-token gate values g drawn as a vertical heat strip over the sequence (local-heavy near recent tokens, global-heavy elsewhere); center, importance scores per token with bottom-m pruned (grayed tokens falling out of the global branch); right, the cumsum trick visualized as prefix-sum boxes replacing an N×N matrix (dense ghost matrix crossed out, replaced by a linear chain of running sums). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer stack with Aegis-Hybrid highlighted as the attention block: three mini-branches (window, cumsum-linear, gate) drawn inside. Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2 causal, Qwen/LLaMA GQA+RoPE. Inset: cost comparison O(N²) dense dashed vs hybrid O(N·w + N·(r + D)) solid; a decoder/encoder toggle icon showing the causal vs bidirectional linear-branch selection. ## Module: aegis_spectron_fp8 (attention; aliases: aegis_spectron_fp8, aegis_spectron_fp8_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Aegis-Spectron-FP8 hybrid attention. Input [B, N, D] → FactorizedLinear Q/K/V (frozen rank-r base lin1→lin2 + purple LoRA branch) → heads [B, N, h, d] → Spectron Spectral-Entropy Router: per-token entropy \(\mathcal{S}(X_t) = -\sum_d p_{t,d}\ln p_{t,d}\) computed from singular values, feeding a softmax router producing routing weights [B, N, 3] over three paths drawn as three lanes: (1) local sliding-window attention (causal, width W); (2) true O(N) low-rank global SVD linear attention (ELU+1 kernel φ(x)=ELU(x)+1, cumsum numerator [B, N, rank, D] / denominator [B, N, rank]); (3) FP8-quantized dense attention lane drawn with an FP8 badge and a small 8-bit cell grid; per-lane outputs combined by router weights; merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: spectral entropy \(\mathcal{S}(X_t) = -\sum_d p_{t,d}\ln p_{t,d}\), \(p_{t,d} = \sigma_d(X_t)/\sum\sigma\); router \(\pi_t = \mathrm{softmax}(W_r[\mathcal{S}(X_t); x_t])\in\mathbb{R}^3\); local window \(S = QK^\top/\sqrt{d}\) masked causally; global linear \(y_t = \gamma^t\,\mathrm{cumsum}(u_i\gamma^{-i})\) recurrence with decay γ; kernel φ(x)=ELU(x)+1; numerator/denominator cumsum fractions; FP8 lane note "weights/activations cast to e4m3". Entropy curve and γ exponential-decay curve drawn beside equations; routing pie per token type. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → factorized projections → [B, N, h, d]; entropy router: X → singular values → [B, N, 1] entropy → logits [B, N, 3]; local lane: attn_weights [B, h, N, N] → masked_fill(causal) → softmax → @V; global lane: φ(K) [B, N, rank] → numerator cumsum [B, N, rank, D], denominator [B, N, rank] → y [B, N, D]; FP8 lane: cast(Q), cast(K) to fp8 → matmul → cast back; weighted sum with π [B, N, 3] → [B, N, h, d] → merge → [B, N, D]. Router pie icons above the three lanes sized by average routing mass. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: aegis_v1 (attention; aliases: aegis_v1, aegis_v1_module, aegisattentionv1)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of AEGIS-Attention V1 (Adaptive Enhanced Global-Local Integrated Sparse attention). Input [B, N, D] → FactorisedLinear Q/K/V: frozen blue SVD-factorized base (energy-based rank truncation: up·down product with rank r chosen from singular-value energy) + trainable purple LoRA adapters (lora_A [rank,in], lora_B [out,rank]); fused QKV weight extraction from pretrained (fused in_proj shown split into three, repeated weights averaged via view(out, rep, −1).mean) → heads [B, N, H, D] → two branches: split-head local sliding-window attention with smooth activation on scores; global linear kernel attention using random Fourier features (RFF block rff_proj, einsum "bhnd,hdf->bhnf") with adaptive gating σ fusion; causal and token-valid masks (−10000); fp32 upcast on scores; merge → out projection → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: energy rank selection \(\min r:\ \sum_{i\le r}\sigma_i^2 \ge E\sum_i\sigma_i^2\); base \(W = W_{up}W_{down} + BA\); local scores \(S = QK^\top/\sqrt{d}\) with window mask; RFF kernel \(\phi(x) = \sqrt{2/D_f}\cos(x R^\top + b)\), global \(y = \frac{\phi(Q)(\phi(K)^\top V)}{\phi(Q)\sum\phi(K)^\top}\); gate \(g=\sigma(\cdot)\), \(O = g\,O_{loc}+(1-g)\,O_{glob}\). Mini-graphics: spectrum bars with energy cut, RFF random-projection grid, gate dial. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: fused W [3D, D] → view(out, rep, −1).mean(dim=1) → Q/K/V weights; [B, N, H·D] → view [B, N, H, D] → per-branch split; local: scores = matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2,-1)) × scale [B, H, N, N_k] → masked_fill(~mask, −10000) and token_valid [B,1,1,N] broadcast; global: Q_norm [B,H,N,d] → einsum "bhnd,hdf->bhnf" → [B,H,N,f] clamped; outputs fused per head → merge [B, N, H·D] → out proj [B, N, D]. fp32 precision nodes marked on the score path. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the RFF global-kernel path: input head vector x → random Fourier projection matrix R (frozen random grid) → cos/sin feature bank → feature map φ; the induced kernel approximation shown as a small N×N dense attention ghost crossed out and replaced by φ(Q)·(φ(K)ᵀV) chain of thin matrices; clamp marker on feature magnitudes for stability. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with AEGIS V1 highlighted (SVD-factorized projections + local window + RFF global + gate). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(N²) dashed vs O(N·w + N·f) (w window, f RFF features) solid; trainable-parameter pie showing tiny LoRA share vs frozen SVD base. ## Module: asvd (attention; aliases: asvd, asvd_attention) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of ASVD — Activation-Aware SVD Attention (arXiv 2307.07382). Left-to-right: input [B, N, D] → ASVDLinear blocks for query/key/value/output: inside each, a frozen rank-r factorization with explicit singular-value scaling, \(y = ((xV^\top)\odot\Sigma)U^\top + (xA^\top)B^\top + b\), drawn as blue V strip → elementwise Σ dial → blue U strip, plus a purple parallel LoRA branch (A [lora_rank, in], B [out, lora_rank], scaling factor noted), both summed at ⊕ with bias; above the projections, an "activation-aware calibration" sub-panel: activation covariance \(C_X = \frac{1}{T}\sum_t x_t x_t^\top\) computed from calibration tokens, feeding the weighted SVD \(W_{weighted} = W C_X^{1/2}\) whose singular vectors become U, V. Heads → [B, N, H, d] with GQA (H query heads over H_kv KV heads) and RoPE on Q/K; scores [B, H, N, N] ×scale in fp32; causal/padding masks at −10000; softmax; @V; merge; factorized output projection → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: activation-aware decomposition \(W C_X^{1/2} = U \Sigma V^\top\), absorbed factors \(U_r = U_{:,:r}\sqrt{\Sigma_r}\), \(V_r = \sqrt{\Sigma_r}V_{:r,:}\); forward \(y = ((xV_r^\top)\odot\Sigma_r)U_r^\top + (xA^\top)B^\top + b\); attention \(S = QK^\top/\sqrt{d_k}\), \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(S)V\). Visual annotations: covariance heatmap grid, singular spectrum bars with energy cut at rank r, the ⊙Σ elementwise dial, LoRA parallel path, lock icon on SVD base and flame on LoRA. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → ASVDLinear: xVᵀ [B, N, r] → ⊙Σ [B, N, r] → ·Uᵀ [B, N, D]; LoRA xAᵀ [B, N, lora_rank] → ·Bᵀ; sum + bias → [B, N, D] → query view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; key/value → [B, H_kv, N, d] (GQA); q.float() @ k.float().transpose(-2,-1) × scale → [B, H, N, N]; extended_mask masked_fill(−10000); softmax → @V → [B, H, N, d] → merge → out ASVDLinear → [B, N, D]. ## Module: block_transformer_hiba (attention; aliases: block_transformer_hiba)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of HIBA — Block Transformer with Hierarchical Intra-Block and Inter-Block Attention. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear Q/K/V (frozen base + purple rank-r branch) → sequence split into blocks of size block_size drawn as a row of boxes; two-level hierarchy: (1) Intra-block attention inside each box (full attention over block_size tokens, cheap); (2) Inter-block attention: each block's summary query attends to its top_k_inter neighboring blocks (selected via scores, sparse_mask built per block, rectification_interval refresh note) — drawn as a second, smaller N/block_size × N/block_size attention grid with only neighbor cells colored; pad masks at −inf; softmax; weighted sums at both levels; merge → [B, N, D]. Hierarchy arrows connecting level 1 boxes to level 2 grid. ## Module: chela_slay (attention; aliases: chela_slay, chela_slay_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of CHELA-SLAY: Spherical Linearized Attention with Yat-Kernel. Left-to-right: [B, L, D] → LoRALinear Q/K/V projections (frozen blue base + thin purple LoRA rank-r branch; fused pretrained weights averaged when repeated) → GQA head split: query heads [B, H, L, d] over H_kv KV heads, repeat-interleave expansion shown as KV wires fanning out (hidden_states[:, :, None].expand(batch, num_kv_heads, n_rep, ...) → reshape) → spherical normalization block: each projection L2-normalized onto the unit hypersphere, drawn as arrows clipped to a circle outline → trigonometric random Fourier feature map (Yat-Kernel): x → random_proj matrix → cos/sin feature bank φ(x), feature_dim labeled → linear attention via associative accumulation (encoder) or cumulative-sum causally (decoder), with denominator normalization; a smooth distillation gate (gate_init_bias dial, sigmoid) blends the linear branch with a ghost dense-attention path inherited from the pretrained weights; merge heads → output projection → [B, L, D]. Frobenius-norm verification badge (≥ 1e-4) on the weight-transfer node. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: spherical projection \(\hat{x} = x/\|x\|_2\) onto the unit hypersphere (unit circle graphic with arrows clipped to the rim); Yat-kernel feature map \(\phi(\hat{x}) = [\cos(\hat{x}R^\top + b), \sin(\hat{x}R^\top + b)]\) with random projection R grid; linear attention \(y_t = \frac{\phi(q_t)^\top \sum_{j\le t}\phi(k_j) v_j^\top}{\phi(q_t)^\top \sum_{j\le t}\phi(k_j)}\) (cumsum icon for causal); gating \(O = g\,O_{lin} + (1-g)\,O_{dense}\), \(g=\sigma(\cdot+b_0)\); distillation note "gate initialized to smoothly inherit pretrained attention". Each term annotated with mini-graphics: hypersphere, RFF bank, cumsum chain, gate dial. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → W_q/W_k/W_v → view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; KV GQA expansion node [B, H_kv, S, d] → expand → reshape → [B, H, S, d]; dense reference scores q.float() @ k.float().transpose(−2,−1) × head_dim^−0.5 [B, H, L, S] drawn dashed (used only for gating/distillation); spherical norm per vector; random feature proj [B, H, L, feature_dim]; linear-attention accumulators [B, H, d, d] and denominators [B, H, L, 1]; gated mix → [B, H, L, d] → merge [B, L, H·d] → out proj [B, L, D]. Score cube ghost marked "not materialized in the linear path". ## Module: compressed_attention (attention; aliases: compressed_attention, compressed_attention_layer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Compressed Multi-Head Attention via truncated SVD. [B, N, D] → a single joint factorized QKV projection block: input multiplied by Vh_qkvᵀ (thin blue [rank_qkv, D] strip) scaled by S_qkv_sqrt (diagonal dial [rank_qkv]), then by (U_qkv·S_qkv_sqrt)ᵀ producing fused qkv [B, N, qkv_out]; torch.split node cutting it into q [all_head_size], k [kv_head_size], v [kv_head_size]; each part → view/transpose → heads [B, H, N, head_dim], [B, H_kv, N, head_dim] (GQA); RoPE on Q/K; scores [N, N] ×scale; causal/padding masks; softmax; @V; merge → second factorized output projection (U_out·S_out_sqrt / Vh_out·S_out_sqrt with bias) → [B, N, D]. LoRA purple residual adapters on both factorized projections; energy-threshold rank note. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: symmetric factor reconstruction \(W = (U \cdot \sqrt{S})\,(V_h \cdot \sqrt{S})^\top\) drawn as two half-Σ strips recombining into the full weight grid; factorized forward \(qkv = ((x V_h^\top)\odot\sqrt{S})\,(U\sqrt{S})^\top + b\); truncation rule: fixed rank or singular-energy threshold; LoRA \(+\ B(Ax)\). Annotate shapes: U_qkv [qkv_out, rank_qkv], Vh_qkv [rank_qkv, D], S_qkv [rank_qkv]; lock icon on factorized base, flame on LoRA. ## Module: compressed_mha (attention; aliases: compressed_mha, compressed_mha_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Compressed MHA via Joint Truncated SVD (DistilBERT-first, multi-arch). [B, N, D] → joint factorized QKV block with non-symmetric factor placement: proj = (x·Vh_qkvᵀ)·S_qkv (singular values on the low-rank side only) → qkv_comp = proj·U_qkvᵀ → split into q/k/v → GQA heads [B, H, N, head_dim]/[B, H_kv, N, head_dim] → RoPE → scores [N, N] ×scale → causal/padding masks → softmax → @V → joint-factorized output projection (Vh_out·S_out → U_out) → [B, N, D]. Frozen base lock icons + purple LoRA adapters on both projections; a DistilBERT flat-MHA silhouette behind the block (q_lin/k_lin/v_lin merged into one W_qkv). ## Module: cosformer (attention; aliases: cosformer, cosformer_attention) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of Cosformer — rethinking softmax with linear complexity. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear Q/K/V (frozen base + purple rank-r residual) → GQA head split [B, H, N, d] over [B, H_kv, kv_len, d] with repeat-interleave expansion node → non-negative feature-map block: ReLU-rectified projections ψ(q), ψ(k) (clipped-at-zero curves drawn) → positional re-weighting panel: cos_weight = cos(angles) and sin_weight = sin(angles) viewed as [1, 1, seq_len, 1] positional modulators multiplying features per position (drawn as a sine/cosine ribbon running along the sequence axis) → dual-path linear attention: encoder path = associative accumulation (matmul φ(K)ᵀV then φ(Q)·, all positions at once) drawn as a compact matrix-product chain; decoder path = cumulative-sum causal scan (running-sum chain with causality arrow) → denominator normalization (ε node) → merge heads → output projection → [B, N, D]. A dashed ghost N×N score matrix crossed out, labeled "never formed". Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: feature map ψ(x) = ReLU(x) (non-negativity plot); positional modulation \(\tilde\psi(q_i) = \psi(q_i)\cos(i\theta)\), \(\tilde\psi(k_j) = \psi(k_j)\cos(j\theta)\) (sine ribbon graphic); linear attention \(y_i = \frac{\sum_j \tilde\psi(q_i)^\top \tilde\psi(k_j) v_j}{\sum_j \tilde\psi(q_i)^\top \tilde\psi(k_j) + \epsilon}\); causal variant via cumsum \(\sum_{j\le i}\); complexity O(N·d). Mini-graphics: ReLU curve at zero, cos/sin ribbon, cumsum chain, ε guard. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_proj view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, kv_len, d] → GQA expand/reshape → [B, H, kv_len, d]; ψ features [B, H, N, d]; cos_weight/sin_weight [1, 1, seq_len, 1] broadcast node; encoder: KV' = ψ(K)ᵀV [B, H, d, d] → y = ψ(Q)·KV' [B, H, N, d]; decoder: cumsum scan over positions; denominator [B, H, N, 1] + ε; merge → [B, N, D]. Annotate "largest intermediate is [B, H, d, d], independent of N" with the [B,H,N,N] ghost crossed out. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: decay_linear_attn (attention; aliases: decay_linear_attention, decay_linear_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Decay Linear Attention with data-dependent exponential decay gating. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v/z (z = denominator feature) projections (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, L, H, d] with repeat-interleave expansion → decay-gate panel: per-token, per-head λ_t = sigmoid/softplus gate from x_t drawn as an exponentially decaying ruler along the sequence ([B, H, L] heat strip) → recurrent matrix-memory scan over time: state update node \(S_t = \lambda_t S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\) drawn as a [B, H, d, d] state box updated step-by-step (causal) or via bidirectional accumulation (encoder icon); readout node num = Sᵀ q_t, denom = zᵀ q_t + 1e-5 → normalized output; merge heads → output projection → [B, L, D]. GQA KV-fan and partial-RoPE wires shown. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: decay gate \(\lambda_t = \exp(-\mathrm{softplus}(g(x_t))) \in (0,1)\) with decay curve; state recurrence \(S_t = \lambda_t S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\) (matrix state box); readout \(y_t = \frac{S_t^\top q_t}{z_t^\top q_t + 10^{-5}}\); ELU+1 feature-map inset φ(x)=ELU(x)+1 ≥ 0 keeping the denominator positive; bidirectional vs causal variants diagram. Memory-norm note: "decay bounds ‖S_t‖ against unbounded associative-memory drift." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v/z similarly with H_kv expansion ([B, H, 1, 1] λ broadcast node); scan: S [B, H, d, d] updated per step (unrolled 4-step mini-diagram), num = matmul(S.transpose(−1,−2), q_t) → [B, H, d, 1], denom = matmul(z.transpose(−1,−2), q_t) + 1e-5 → [B, H, 1, 1]; y = num/denom → [B, H, L, d] → merge → [B, L, D]. State-box recurrence emphasized as replacing the N×N ghost. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: expert_choice_moe (attention; aliases: expert_choice_moe, expert_choice_moe_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Expert-Choice MoE Attention (Zhou et al. 2022 — experts pick tokens). [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads ([B, H, L, d], repeat-interleave node) → ExpertChoiceMoE routing panel: affinity logits [B·L, E] computed token×expert; softmax over the TOKEN axis (dim=0 — the inversion is drawn as a swapped-axis icon); each of E experts selects its top-k tokens by affinity (capacity_factor setting the k = capacity·L/E); expert blocks process their selected tokens in parallel (E rounded blocks with gated inputs via moe_init_gate, noise_std dial on logits); outputs scattered back to token positions (unselected tokens keep identity path) → attention scores [B, H, L, S] ×scale, causal mask, softmax, @V; merge → [B, L, D]. Load-balance badge: "perfect balance by construction." ## Module: fasa (attention; aliases: fasa, fasa_attention, mixattention_moska)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MixAttention-MoSKA (FASA) — Frequency/Variance-Aware Sparse Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear W_q/W_k/W_v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused weights averaged via w_oriented.view(out_d, rep, −1).mean) → GQA heads via permute(0,2,1,3) → [B, H, L, d] → KV importance panel: per-KV-token variance-based importance scores computed over keys/values → importance masked (padding/2-D valid mask, −10000) and CLS token (index 0) force-protected (shield icon on the first token) → keep top fasa_keep_ratio fraction of KV tokens (topk) → gather via idx.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(−1, H, −1, D_h) → pruned KV [B, H, L_kv′, d]; attention of all queries against the kept KV: scores ×scale → causal mask (decoder) → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: flash3 (attention; aliases: flash3, flash_attention3)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of FlashAttention-3 integration — exact attention with asynchronous hardware overlap. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, L, Dh]/[B, H_kv, kv_len, Dh] (repeat-interleave node) → kernel dispatch panel with three lanes: (1) FlashAttention-3 kernel lane: Q/K/V tiles streamed Hopper-style — TMA async copy blocks (double-buffered tiles drawn as overlapping rounded tiles), warp-specialized GEMM + softmax units, running softmax (online max/sum icons m_i, l_i) with rescaling; (2) PyTorch SDPA fallback lane (dashed, labeled "graceful fallback"); (3) eager lane (rare path); lane selector based on hardware; outputs merged → transpose(1,2).contiguous() layout node → merge heads → out projection → [B, L, D]. Caption: "exact attention — no approximation; O(N) memory via tiling." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: exact softmax attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt{d} + M)V\); online-softmax recurrence \(m^{new} = \max(m, \mathrm{rowmax}(S_{tile}))\), \(l^{new} = e^{m - m^{new}} l + \mathrm{rowsum}(e^{S_{tile} - m^{new}})\), \(O \leftarrow e^{m - m^{new}} O + P_{tile} V_{tile}\) drawn as a 3-step unrolled tile loop; note "never materializes [N, N]"; GQA expansion formula annotation. Tile blocks and running-max/l_sum bars visualized. ## Module: fms (attention; aliases: factorised_matrix_state, fms)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Factorised Matrix State (FMS) attention — recurrent linear attention with a factorized readout. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v/z projections (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads ([B, L, H, d], repeat-interleave node) → matrix-state scan: learnable per-head decay d_val = decay_f.view(1, H, 1, 1) ([H, 1, 1] parameter dial) scaling the recurrent state update \(S_t = d\,S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\) drawn as a [B, H, d, d] state box per head (causal unrolled steps; bidirectional variant icon) → readout numerator num = Sᵀ q_t [B, H, d, 1], denominator denom = zᵀ q_t + 1e-5 → y = num/denom → the output projection is itself low-rank factorized (proj_rank parameter: W_out = A_out·B_out thin strips) → merge heads → [B, L, D]. Frobenius weight-verification badge (≥ 1e-4). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: state update \(S_t = d\,S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\) with decay dial \(d \in (0,1)\) per head; readout \(y_t = \frac{S_t^\top q_t}{z_t^\top q_t + 10^{-5}}\); factorized output \(W_{out} = W_1 W_2\) with proj_rank annotation; feature-map note ELU+1 for positive denominators. Graphics: matrix-state box, decay curve, factorized output strips (low-rank), z-denominator guard. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v/z with H_kv expansion; decay_f [H, 1, 1] → d_val [1, H, 1, 1]; scan: S [B, H, d, d] ← d·S + matmul(k_t, v_t.transpose(−1,−2)) per step; num = matmul(S.transpose(−1,−2), q_t) [B, H, d, 1]; denom = matmul(z.transpose(−1,−2), q_t) + 1e-5 [B, H, 1, 1]; y [B, H, L, d] → merge → factorized out (proj_rank) → [B, L, D]. Constant-size state vs growing-KV ghost drawn. **Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the factorized matrix memory: the [d × d] associative state matrix per head drawn as a heatmap evolving over 4 time steps with decay multiplying it down each step (opacity fading) while new k vᵀ outer products (two thin vectors forming a rank-1 sheet) are added; the learnable decay dials per head set to different values; the low-rank output projection as two thin strips reconstructing the full matrix. ## Module: fused_svd_kernel (attention; aliases: fused_svd_kernel)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Fused SVD-Linear layer with SRAM-resident RMSNorm and dual projection — a Triton-kernel-level diagram. Left: input X [M, K_in] as a tile grid. One fused kernel box (GPU SRAM chip outline) contains three sequential steps drawn inside: (1) down-projection Z = X·Vᵀ with V [rank, K_in] thin blue strip, Z [BLOCK_M, R] tile; (2) in-SRAM RMSNorm Z_norm = Z·rsqrt(mean(Z²)+ε)·weight drawn with the mean/variance node and per-channel weight dial; (3) up-projection Y = Z_norm·Uᵀ + bias with U [K_out, rank] strip. An HBM arrow is deliberately crossed out — caption "Z never leaves on-chip SRAM; no HBM roundtrip." Below, a small autograd panel (custom _FusedSVDLinearAutogradFunction) showing analytical gradients dU = gᵀ·z_norm, dz_norm = g·U, dV = dzᵀ·x, dx = dz·V with shapes [K_out, R], [M, R], [R, K_in]. Fallback node: pure-PyTorch path when Triton/CUDA unavailable. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: low-rank projection \(Z = X V^\top\), \(V\in\mathbb{R}^{R\times K_{in}}\); in-SRAM RMSNorm \(Z_{norm} = Z \odot \mathrm{rsqrt}(\tfrac{1}{R}\sum z_i^2 + \epsilon)\odot \gamma\); up-projection \(Y = Z_{norm} U^\top + b\), \(U\in\mathbb{R}^{K_{out}\times R}\); composite \(y = \|xV^\top\|_{norm}\,U^\top + b\). Each equation annotated: V and U as thin strips, the norm as a per-row gauge, ε guard; a bandwidth panel comparing 3 HBM passes (unfused, crossed) vs 1 (fused) with arithmetic-intensity ×2.5 note. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: x [B, …, K_in] → reshape(−1, K_in).contiguous() → x_2d [M, K_in] → fused kernel: Z = X·Vᵀ [M, rank] (SRAM), RMSNorm in-register, Y = Z_norm·Uᵀ + bias [M, K_out] → view(*orig_shape[:-1], K_out). Backward panel: grad_output [M, K_out] → grad_U = gᵀz_norm [K_out, R]; grad_z_norm = g·U [M, R]; grad_z (through norm Jacobian); grad_V = dzᵀx [R, K_in]; grad_x = dz·V [M, K_in]. Parameter strip: V [rank, in], U [out, rank], norm weight [rank], bias [out]. ## Module: fusion_attn (attention; aliases: fusion_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of FUSIONATTN — Multi-Paradigm Hybrid Sparse Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear Q/K/V (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d] (repeat-interleave node) → block-gated sparse panel: sequence padded and reshaped into blocks q_blocks [B, H, num_blocks, block_size, D]; per-block gating scores (learned, sigmoid dial) select the top_k_blocks most relevant key-blocks per query-block; gather node idx_exp → k_sel [B, H, top_k·block_size, D]; attention computed only query-block × selected-blocks (sparse block-diagonal pattern drawn over the N×N ghost) → subspace-estimation side path: low-rank (rank) global summary attention for long-range context; short-sequence fast-path switch (N ≤ threshold → plain full attention, drawn as a bypass arrow) → combine → merge heads → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: block gating \(g_b = \sigma(W_g\,\bar{q}_b)\), selection \(\mathcal{B}(b) = \mathrm{TopK}_{k}(g)\); block attention \(O_b = \mathrm{softmax}(Q_b K_{\mathcal{B}(b)}^\top/\sqrt{d})V_{\mathcal{B}(b)}\); subspace path \(O^{sub} = \mathrm{softmax}(Q P_c^\top) P_c V\)-style rank-r summary; fast-path rule N ≤ N₀ → dense; fusion \(O = O^{blk} + \lambda O^{sub}\). Visuals: N×N ghost matrix with colored block-rows (k blocks per query block), low-rank summary strip, bypass switch icon. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: galerkin (attention; aliases: galerkin, galerkin_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Galerkin Attention — Petrov-Galerkin projection attention. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, N, d] (repeat-interleave node) → operator-form panel: the sequence treated as a discretized continuous function; an orthonormal basis (discrete cosine basis, drawn as a stack of cosine waveforms of increasing frequency) projects Q/K/V onto basis coefficients; attention computed as a non-local integral operator in coefficient space (smaller low-dim score matrix [B, H, N, N] reduced to basis size) with softmax over coefficients; inverse transform back to token space; decoder fallback switch: standard causal attention lane (dashed) when is_decoder; masks (padding) applied; fp32 score path; merge heads → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: operator view \((\mathcal{T}u)(x) = \int k(x,y)u(y)\,dy\) discretized; Petrov-Galerkin projection \(\tilde{Q} = \Phi^\top Q\), \(\tilde{K} = \Phi^\top K\), \(\tilde{V} = \Phi^\top V\) with orthonormal basis Φ (cosine modes drawn); coefficient attention \(\tilde{P} = \mathrm{softmax}(\tilde{Q}\tilde{K}^\top/\sqrt{d})\); reconstruction \(O = \Phi\tilde{P}\tilde{V}\); trial/test space note (different bases allowed). Complexity O(N·m), m = basis size. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → query view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; basis projection Φᵀ: [N, N] → [m, N] reducing each of Q/K/V to [B, H, m, d]; scores [B, H, m, m] (small grid) fp32 × scale → softmax → @Ṽ [B, H, m, d] → inverse basis transform → [B, H, N, d] → merge → [B, N, D]; decoder fallback: scores [B, H, N, N] + causal tril → softmax → @V (dashed lane). Padding masks on both lanes. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the spectral bottleneck: a signal (token sequence curve) decomposed into DCT basis modes (bars = coefficient magnitudes, most energy in the first m modes); attention performed only on retained modes (frequency-domain score matrix m×m); reconstruction curve overlapping the original; a cut-frequency dial showing what truncation discards. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer block with Galerkin attention highlighted (basis → operator → inverse). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(N·m) vs O(N²) dashed; encoder/decoder switch icon showing the exact causal fallback lane. ## Module: gated_delta_net (attention; aliases: gated_delta_net) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of Gated DeltaNet — Delta-Rule linear recurrent attention with input-dependent gating. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen base + purple rank-r LoRA branch; fused pretrained weights averaged) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d] ([B, H_kv, L, d] with repeat-interleave expand node) → recurrent core drawn as an unrolled 4-step chain: per step t, error-correction node e_t = v_t − S_{t−1}k_t (feedback loop from state S to a subtract node), gated state update \(S_t = b_t S_{t-1} + a_t\, e_t k_t^\top\) with two per-step gates: decay b_t (forget dial, [B, H, L] strip) and correction strength a_t (write dial); the state S is a [B, H, d, d] matrix box; readout y_t = S_t q_t; forward scan lane and a parallel backward scan lane (bidirectional variant) merged for encoders; masks (padding [B, 1, L]); merge heads → out projection → [B, L, D]. Caption: "O(L) — associative memory that overwrites, not just accumulates." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: DeltaNet recurrence with gating \(S_t = \beta_t S_{t-1} + \alpha_t (v_t - S_{t-1}k_t)k_t^\top\); error correction \(e_t = v_t - S_{t-1}k_t\) (prediction-minus-actual feedback loop drawn); readout \(y_t = S_t q_t\); gates \(\beta_t = \mathrm{decay}(x_t)\in(0,1)\), \(\alpha_t = \sigma(\cdot)\) input-dependent dials; GQA note \(H_{KV} \le H\). A small rank-1 outer-product graphic shows each step writing \(e_t k_t^\top\) (a corrective sheet) into the matrix state; decay curve for β. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand → reshape; per step: pred_v = matmul(S_fwd, k_t) [B, H, d, 1]; e_t = v_t − pred_v; S_fwd = b_t·S_fwd + a_t·matmul(e_t, k_t.transpose(−1,−2)) [B, H, d, d]; y_t = matmul(S_fwd, q_t).squeeze(-1) [B, H, d]; gate tensors [B, H, L] and [B, 1, L] broadcast nodes; backward scan analog (S_bwd) for bidirectional mode; concat/merge [B, L, H·d] → out proj → [B, L, D]. The state box [B, H, d, d] drawn as constant-size vs the L×L ghost crossed out. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of delta-rule error correction vs plain accumulation: two state heatmaps evolving over the same 3 keys — top lane (accumulate-only, like linear attention) shows old values smearing; bottom lane (delta rule) shows the new key-value pair overwriting the stale entry (before/after cells flip), with the error vector e_t drawn as the difference arrow; gate dials β (forget) and α (correct) shown per step; a note "remembers what changed, forgets what's stale." ## Module: gated_linear_attn (attention; aliases: gated_linear_attention, gated_linear_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Gated Linear Attention (GLA). [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused-weight averaging node w.view(out_d, rep, −1).mean) → GQA heads [B, L, H, d]/[B, L_kv, H_kv, d] (repeat-interleave) → data-dependent decay gate panel: decay_gate projection → decay_logits [B, H, L] → λ_t = exp(−softplus(·)) decay ruler along the sequence → recurrent matrix-memory scan \(S_t = \lambda_t S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\) ([B, H, d, d] state box; causal unrolled steps; bidirectional icon for encoders) → multiplicative SiLU output gating: out = (scan result) ⊙ silu(gate_proj(x)) drawn as a second purple gate wire multiplying elementwise → denominator normalization node (z-feature readout + ε) → merge heads → out proj → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: decay \(\lambda_t = \exp(-\mathrm{softplus}(W_f x_t))\) with decay curve; state \(S_t = \lambda_t S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\); output gating \(y_t = \mathrm{readout}(S_t, q_t) \odot \mathrm{SiLU}(W_g x_t)\) (SiLU curve inset); normalization \(y_t \leftarrow y_t / (z_t^\top q_t + \epsilon)\). Annotate: bounded state norm under decay; SiLU multiplicative gate as a second pathway. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L_kv, d] → GQA expand; decay_logits [B, H, L] → λ [B, H, L, 1] broadcast; scan: S [B, H, d, d] ← λ·S + k vᵀ per step; reference dense view attn_scores [B, H, L, L] (ghost, not materialized); SiLU gate [B, H, L, d] ⊙ readout; normalize → merge → [B, L, D]. The dense ghost crossed out; state box constant. ## Module: goldfinch (attention; aliases: goldfinch, goldfinch_ahn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of GoldFinch-AHN — Adaptive Hybrid Network. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear W_q/W_k/W_v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → two parallel memory branches: (1) short-term multi-head sliding-window attention: scores q @ kᵀ × head_dim^−0.5 masked to a window (band mask over [B, H, L, L]), GQA heads ([B, H_kv, L, d], expansion node), pos_ids + RoPE node; (2) long-term GatedDeltaNetLayer recurrent memory: delta-rule matrix state with input-dependent gating (state box [B, H, d, d]) → adaptive dynamic gate combining branches: learned gate from input x ([B, L, 1]) initialized via gate_init_bias = −3.0 → sigmoid ≈ 0.047, drawn as a dial at "≈95% window passthrough at init" (preservation gate) → weighted sum of branch outputs → merge → out proj → [B, L, D]. spectral_norm icon on the gate. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: window branch \(O^{w} = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{QK^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M_{window})V\); recurrent branch \(S_t = \beta_t S_{t-1} + \alpha_t (v_t - S_{t-1}k_t)k_t^\top\), readout via q; fusion gate \(g = \sigma(W_g x + b_0)\), \(b_0 = -3\) annotation "σ(−3) ≈ 0.047 → ~95% passthrough at init"; output \(O = (1-g)O^{w} + g\,O^{gdn}\) (or the complementary form — gate trained to admit memory gradually). Window band graphic, delta-rule state box, gate initialization curve. ## Module: gqa (attention; aliases: gqa, grouped_query_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) — the KV-sharing baseline. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch): the query projection is wide (H heads) while key/value projections are narrow (num_kv_heads ≤ H) — drawn as one thick wire splitting into H query heads and H_kv shared KV wires; repeat-interleave expansion node hidden_states[:, :, None].expand(batch, num_kv_heads, n_rep, slen, head_dim) → reshape fanning KV to all query groups → scores = Q·Kᵀ × scale [B, H, N_q, N_k] → padding + causal tril masks (−10000) → softmax fp32 with nan_to_num → @V → head_mask [1, −1, 1, 1] → merge → out proj → [B, N, D]. Groups drawn explicitly: G = H/H_kv query heads per KV head. **Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: grouping \(H_{KV} = G \le H_Q\), each group shares one K/V head; scores \(S = QK^\top/\sqrt{d_k}\); masking and softmax; \(O = PV\). The MHA→GQA→MQA spectrum drawn as three small head-diagrams: all pairs shared (MHA), groups (GQA, highlighted), single shared KV (MQA); KV-cache size bar H → H_kv reduction. ## Module: granite_hybrid (attention; aliases: granite_hybrid, granite_hybrid_block)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Granite Hybrid Block — Mamba-2 SSM + MHA + Top-k MoE feed-forward. [B, L, D] → three-lane block: (1) Mamba2 SSM lane: x_proj chunked into (x_ssm, z); depthwise conv1d over the sequence; selective scan with discretization dA = clamp(einsum("bli,in->blin", dt, A), −30, 10) and input stepping dBX; state h update; output gated by SiLU(z) — drawn as a diagonal state chain with per-step gates; (2) dense MHA lane: q/k/v projections [B, L_q, H, Dh] → [B, H, L, d], scores, causal mask, softmax, @V; (3) Top-k Token-Choice MoE feed-forward: router softmax over E experts, top_k active per token, expert bars; lane-mixing ratio (ratio param) combining SSM and MHA outputs → shared MoE FFN → LoRA adapters on projections (purple branches) → [B, L, D]. ## Module: griffin_ssd (attention; aliases: griffin_ssd, griffin_ssd_block)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Griffin SSD — local sliding-window attention fused with global State-Space Duality recurrence. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear W_q/W_k/W_v + ssd_proj (frozen + purple rank-r LoRA branches) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d]/[B, H_kv, L, d] → two-branch core: (1) local branch: window-masked attention scores q @ kᵀ/√d [B, H, L, L] with window_mask band ([B, 1, 1, L] per-head window sizes via parameter [num_heads, 1, 1] dial); (2) global branch: SSD linear recurrence — q_ssd projected features drive a matrix-state scan with decay ([B, H, d, d] state box, O(L) chain) → per-head fusion gate (learned, [B, H, 1, 1]) blending branches → masks → merge → [B, L, D]. Griffin-style recurrence/attention interleave icon. ## Module: halo_regla (attention; aliases: halo_regla, halo_regla_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of HALO-ReGLA — Hardware-Aware Linear Optics & Rectified Gated Linear Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute(0,2,1,3) head layout) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d]/[B, H_kv, L, d] → RoPE rotary node on Q/K ("linear optics" stage drawn as rotation dials) → rectified kernel panel: feature map with rectification (clipped-at-zero curve) ensuring non-negative keys/values for a valid denominator; dual-path: (1) dense reference lane (dashed): dense_scores = q.float() @ k.float().ᵀ × scale → softmax → dense_out (used for distillation only); (2) linear lane: kv = k_linᵀ @ v_masked (matrix accumulator [B, H, d, d]) → num = q_lin @ kv → denominator normalization ([B, 1, L, 1] guard) → hybrid_ratio dial mixing the paths (initialized toward dense for stable distillation transfer, gate_init_bias) → merge heads → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: hamming (attention; aliases: hamming, hamming_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Hamming Attention Distillation (HAD) — sign-binarized inner products. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, L_q, Dh]/[B, H_kv, L_k, Dh] (repeat-interleave) → binarization panel: q_b = tanh(5.0·Q·q_scale) and k_b = tanh(5.0·K·k_scale) drawn as soft-sign quantizers (values squashed toward ±1, per-head scale dials [1, H, 1, 1]) → Hamming-distance scores: scores = temp·(q_b @ k_bᵀ − Dh)/(2√Dh) — drawn as a sign-vector XNOR/popcount sketch (matching signs add, differing cancel) with learned temperature dial and distill_temperature calibration → padding + causal tril masks (−10000) → softmax → @V (values unbinarized) → merge → [B, L, D]. Note "distilled to approximate dense attention." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: soft binarization \(\hat{q} = \tanh(5\,s_q Q)\) (tanh saturation curve inset); Hamming surrogate \(\hat{S}_{ij} = \tau\,\frac{\hat{q}_i^\top \hat{k}_j - D_h}{2\sqrt{D_h}}\) (equivalence: inner product of sign vectors ↔ D_h − 2·Hamming); attention \(P = \mathrm{softmax}(\hat{S} + M)\), \(O = PV\); calibrated temperature τ dial. Graphics: sign grids (+/− cells), popcount counter, dense-vs-Hamming score scatter showing correlation. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L_q, D] → q_proj view(B, L_q, H, Dh).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L_q, Dh]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L_k, Dh] → GQA expand; binarize: tanh(5·Q·q_scale [1,H,1,1]) → q_b [B, H, L_q, Dh] (same for k_b); scores = temp·(matmul(q_b, k_b.transpose(−2,−1)) − Dh)/(2√Dh) → [B, H, L_q, L_k]; masked_fill(valid/causal, −10000); softmax → @V [B, H, L_q, Dh] → merge → [B, L_q, D]. V path un-binarized (full precision wire). ## Module: hgrn2 (attention; aliases: hgrn2, hgrn2_layer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of HGRN-2 — Hierarchically Gated Recurrent Network v2 (element-wise linear recurrence). [B, L, D] → fused input projection chunked into three parts (in_w.chunk(3): input, forget, output gates) → log-space forget gating panel: f_t = exp(−softplus(gate)) with bias −3.0 initialization note ("softplus(−3) ≈ 0.048 → f_t ≈ 0.95") drawn as a gentle per-channel decay ruler along the sequence → recurrence via cumulative sum in log space: C = cumsum(log_f, dim=1) [B, D, L]; causal matrix D_causal built from exp(C_i − C_j) (lower-triangular decay mask); h_fwd = matmul(D_causal, u) with u = iv (input-modulated values) [B, D, L, 1]; bidirectional variant with D_anti (anti-triangular) summed for encoders; residual: v = v + LoRA branch (v_lora_A/v_lora_B × lora_scaling); output gating combine → [B, L, D]. res_clip stability badge. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: element-wise recurrence \(h_t = f_t \odot h_{t-1} + i_t \odot x_t\) with \(f_t = \exp(-\mathrm{softplus}(W_f x_t + b_f)) \in (0,1)\); closed form via log-space cumsum \(h_t = \sum_{j\le t} e^{C_t - C_j} i_j x_j\), \(C_t = \sum_{s\le t}\log f_s\) (cumsum icon replacing an L×L decay matrix); bias init −3.0 → f ≈ 0.95; bidirectional sum of forward/backward passes. Small L×L decay ghost crossed out next to the cumsum chain. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → fused proj → chunk(3) → i/v/f gates; log_f [B, L, D] → cumsum → C [B, D, L] (permute note); u = iv.permute(0,2,1).unsqueeze(-1) → [B, D, L, 1]; D_causal from C differences → h_fwd = matmul(D_causal, u).squeeze(-1).permute(0,2,1) → [B, L, D] (fp32 compute node → cast back); h_bwd via D_anti; LoRA: v + (v·Aᵀ)·Bᵀ × scaling; gated output → [B, L, D]. The [B, D, L, L] intermediate marked as the memory-aware construction (built from C, not stored). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of log-space forgetting: per-channel forget values f_t drawn as bars slightly below 1.0 (0.95 default from the −3.0 bias); the log-domain trick — multiplying many f's becomes summing log f's (a running sum chain) — with the exponentiated difference e^{C_t−C_j} shown as the relative decay between two positions; numeric-stability note (sum in log space, exponentiate once). ## Module: hgrn2_outer (attention; aliases: hgrn2_outer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of HGRN-2 Outer-Product Recurrent Linear Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads ([B, L, H, d] views; reference dense path attn_scores = q_f @ k_fᵀ [B, H, L, L] drawn ghosted, never materialized) → bounded decay gate: f = exp(−softplus(W_f(x))) [B, H, L] drawn as per-head decay rulers → outer-product associative memory scan: state update \(S_t = f_t\,S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\) with the rank-1 outer product k_t v_tᵀ drawn as a sheet added to the [B, H, d, d] state box (causal unrolled chain; bidirectional icon) → readout with denominator normalization (z/feature-sum guard + ε) → merge heads → out proj → [B, L, D].
