**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: decay gate \(f_t = \exp(-\mathrm{softplus}(W_f x_t)) \in (0,1)\) (softplus curve inset); outer-product recurrence \(S_t = f_t S_{t-1} + k_t v_t^\top\); readout \(y_t = \frac{S_t^\top q_t}{z_t^\top q_t + \epsilon}\); bounded-decay note "log-space bounded: f never reaches 0 or overflows." ## Module: hla (attention; aliases: hla)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Hadamard Linear Attention (HLA). [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen base + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, L, H, Dh]/[B, L, H_kv, Dh] with repeat-interleave expansion → Hadamard associative kernelization panel: q, k, v pass through fixed Hadamard rotation matrices (butterfly diagrams drawn as crossing wire pairs) before the associative scan — "kernelization" badge → multi-head matrix-state recurrence S_t ∈ R^{B×H×d_k×d_k}: per step, masked update S_t ← S_{t−1} + m_t·(k_t v_tᵀ) with mask scalar m_t = mask[:, t].view(B,1,1,1) drawn as a per-step validity dial; causal (lower-triangular chain) and anti-causal (reverse chain) lanes summed for the bidirectional mode; readout with z-denominator: denom = (zᵀq + 1e-5) [B, H, 1, 1] guard → merge heads → out projection → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: Hadamard kernelization \(\tilde{q} = H q,\ \tilde{k} = H k\) (butterfly network graphic, log-depth wire crossings); associative state \(S_t = S_{t-1} + m_t\, \tilde{k}_t \tilde{v}_t^\top\); readout \(y_t = \frac{S_t^\top \tilde{q}_t}{z_t^\top \tilde{q}_t + 10^{-5}}\); learnable decay gating \(S_t \to \lambda_h S_t\) with per-head λ dial [num_heads, 1, 1]; bidirectional = causal + anti-causal sum. Hadamard property note: "HᵀH = I, rotations preserve norms." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, Dh).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, Dh]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L, Dh] → GQA expand; Hadamard transform in-place per head; scan: S [B, H, Dh, Dh] updated per step with m_t [B, 1, 1, 1]; readout: (Sᵀ @ q_t) → [B, H, Dh, 1]; denom = (z.transpose(−1,−2) @ q_t.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1) + 1e-5; y [B, H, L, Dh] → merge [B, L, H·Dh] → out proj → [B, L, D]. head_mask view nodes shown. ## Module: hymba_transx (attention; aliases: hymba_transx)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of HymbaTransX — hybrid Differential Transformer + SSM block. [B, L, D] → two parallel attention sub-lanes with paired projections W_q1/W_k1/W_v1 and W_q2/W_k2/W_v2 (LoRALinear, frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused weights reshaped via w.reshape(w.size(0), −1)): lane 1 and lane 2 each compute GQA heads [B, L, H, head_dim]/[B, L, H_kv, head_dim]; HeadRoPE modules (per-head rotary dials, freqs = einsum("i,j->ij", t, inv_freq) table drawn) applied to both lanes; differential core: the two lanes' score maps subtracted (S₁ − S₂) — the "differential" cancellation of common-mode noise drawn as two attention heatmaps with their shared background canceling, highlighting the difference signal; softmax → @V₁ (− V₂-weighted) → SSM branch: a compact state-space scan lane (state chain) mixed at a 0.5-init gate (parameter tensor(0.5) dial) → merge → out projections → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: differential attention \(A_{diff} = \mathrm{softmax}(S_1) - \lambda\,\mathrm{softmax}(S_2)\), λ init 0.5, drawn as two matrices subtracting (shared low-frequency pattern canceling); RoPE \(q'_m = R_m q\) with frequency table \(\theta_j = base^{-2j/d}\) (freqs = t·inv_freq grid); SSM branch \(h_t = \bar{A}h_{t-1} + \bar{B}_t x_t\); block output \(O = (1-g)\,O_{diff} + g\,O_{ssm}\), g init 0.5. Noise-cancellation inset: two overlapping spectra minus common mode. **Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → W_q1/W_k1/W_v1 → view(B, L, H, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, head_dim] (lane 1), same for lane 2 with W_q2/W_k2; K lanes [B, H_kv, L, head_dim] → GQA expand; HeadRoPE: freqs [L, head_dim/2] → apply per lane; per-lane scores [B, H, L, L]; differential combine → softmax → @V → [B, H, L, head_dim]; SSM lane state chain [B, H, d_state]; gate mix → merge [B, L, H·head_dim] → W_o ×2 lanes → [B, L, D]. ## Module: infini (attention; aliases: infini, infini_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Infini-Attention with compressive memory and sliding window. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, L, Dh]/[B, H_kv, L, Dh] (repeat-interleave) → two-branch core: (1) local sliding-window attention over the recent local_window tokens (band mask over [B, H, L, L]); (2) compressive memory branch: a learned memory tensor [1, num_heads, compress_dim, head_dim] (expand to [B, −1, −1, −1]) acting as a small set of global memory slots — mem_scores = q @ memᵀ/√Dh [B, H, L, compress_dim] (tiny grid), softmax, mem_out = mem_attn @ mem; gating node combining local and memory outputs (learned sigmoid gate) → merge heads → out proj → [B, L, D]. A sequence broken into segments with memory passing between segments (memory carried across the segment boundary arrow) — "unbounded context via bounded memory." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: local attention \(O^{loc} = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{QK^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M_{window})V\); memory attention \(O^{mem} = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q\,M^\top}{\sqrt{d}})M\) with \(M \in \mathbb{R}^{c \times d}\) (c = compress_dim slots drawn as a small bank of summary vectors); gating \(O = g\,O^{mem} + (1-g)O^{loc}\); segmental recurrence note "memory updated/passed between segments → effectively unbounded context." Visuals: window band, memory-slot bank, gate dial, segment chain with memory arrows crossing boundaries. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, Dh).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, Dh]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, Dh] → GQA expand; local branch: scores [B, H, L, L] band-masked → softmax → @V; memory branch: memory [1, H, compress_dim, head_dim] → expand(B, −1, −1, −1); mem_scores = q.float() @ mem.float().transpose(−2,−1)/√Dh → [B, H, L, compress_dim]; softmax → mem_out [B, H, L, Dh]; gate → fused [B, H, L, Dh] → merge → [B, L, D]. Memory bank drawn c ≪ L. ## Module: inject_rala (attention; aliases: inject_rala)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of RALA — Rank-Augmented Linear Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute(0,2,1,3) head layout) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d]/[B, H_kv, L, d] → linear-attention core with a rank-augmentation twist: besides the standard associative accumulator S ← S + k_cᵀ v_c (chunked scan drawn as chunk boxes — "true linear-time recurrence, O(chunk·d²) memory, no O(N·d²) allocation" note), a static learned head-interaction matrix is added: rank_mat = einsum("h d r, h e r -> h d e", U, V) building a per-head low-rank [d × d] correction (U, V thin strips per head, rank r_aug); intra-chunk attention lane: intra_attn = q_c k_cᵀ ⊙ causal_mask within each chunk (small block-diagonal pattern) → inter-chunk readout: inter_out = q_c @ (S + rank_mat) → outputs combined → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: chunked linear attention \(S_c = S_{c-1} + K_c^\top V_c\), \(O_{inter} = Q_c (S_{c-1} + R)\); rank augmentation \(R_h = U_h V_h^\top\), \(U_h\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r_a}, V_h\in\mathbb{R}^{e\times r_a}\) per head (einsum shown); intra-chunk \(O_{intra} = \mathrm{softmax?}(Q_cK_c^\top \odot M_{causal})V_c\) (masked chunk-local); combined \(O = O_{intra} + O_{inter}\). Memory note: chunked scan bounds peak memory at chunk·d². Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj permute → [B, H, L, d]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand; chunk into C chunks [B, H, C, c, d]; accumulator S [B, H, d, d] ← Σ K_cᵀV_c; rank_mat = einsum("h d r, h e r -> h d e") [H, d, e]; intra: q_c @ k_cᵀ ⊙ causal_mask [B, H, C, c, c] → @v_c; inter: q_c @ (S + rank_mat) → [B, H, L, d] → merge → [B, L, D]. Chunk boxes drawn along the sequence with block-diagonal intra mask. ## Module: inject_regla (attention; aliases: inject_regla)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ReGLA — Refined Gated Linear Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v/gate (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute head layout) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d]/[B, H_kv, L, d] → element-wise key gating: g = gate_proj(x) per KV channel multiplying keys (k_gated = k ⊙ σ(g) or similar, drawn as channel dials on the key wires) → normalized linear attention: kv = k_gatedᵀ @ v [B, H, d, d] accumulator; num = q @ kv; den = (q @ zᵀ).clamp(min=1e-5) with a separate z/sum-feature denominator wire ([B, 1, L, 1] guard icon) → local refinement lane: short-range depthwise 1D convolution (kernel_size dial, drawn as a small sliding window over the sequence with per-channel taps) adding local inductive bias to the global associative output → combine → head_mask view node → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: key gating \(\tilde{k}_t = k_t \odot \gamma(x_t)\); linear attention \(y_t = \frac{q_t^\top \sum_{j\le t} \tilde{k}_j v_j^\top}{q_t^\top z_t + 10^{-5}}\) (cumsum icon; clamped denominator); depthwise conv refinement \(y_t \leftarrow y_t + \mathrm{DWConv}_w(y)_t\) with kernel size K; per-head structure. Local-vs-global receptive field sketch: conv window (short arrows) + associative memory (whole-sequence arrows). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v/g [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand; gate → k_gated; kv = matmul(k_gated.transpose(−2,−1), v) → [B, H, d, d]; num = matmul(q, kv) [B, H, L, d]; den = matmul(q, z.transpose(−2,−1)).clamp(min=1e-5) [B, H, L, 1]; out = num/den; + depthwise conv lane [B, D, L] (transpose note); × head_mask view(1, H, 1, 1); merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: inject_rpc (attention; aliases: inject_rpc)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of RPC — Random Projection Compressed Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear W_q/W_k/W_v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d]/[B, H_kv, L, d] (repeat-interleave) → sketching panel: fixed per-head Gaussian random projection buffers proj [H, d, r] (drawn as frozen random grids — no training, a snowflake icon); q_sketched = einsum("b h l d, h d r -> b h l r", q, proj) and same for k → compressed score space: scores = q_sketched @ k_sketchedᵀ × scale in [B, H, L, L] but computed via r-dimensional inner products (cost note: r ≪ d); padding + causal masks; softmax; probs × head_mask; @V (values full precision) → merge → [B, L, D]. Johnson-Lindenstrauss note: "distances preserved in expectation." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: sketch \(\tilde{q} = q P_h\), \(\tilde{k} = k P_h\), \(P_h \in \mathbb{R}^{d\times r}\) i.i.d. Gaussian frozen; approximate scores \(\tilde{S}_{ij} = \tilde{q}_i^\top \tilde{k}_j \approx q_i^\top k_j\) (JL lemma: relative error ≤ ε with r = O(log N/ε²) dial); attention \(P = \mathrm{softmax}(\tilde{S}/\sqrt{d} + M)\), \(O = PV\). Graphics: high-dim point cloud projected to a low-dim plane with pairwise distances preserved (a few chords with labels), frozen-projection snowflake, error-vs-r curve. ## Module: inject_salt_attention (attention; aliases: inject_salt_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of SALT — Singular Value Adaptation with Low-Rank Tuning. [B, L, D] → SALTLinear projections (q/k/v/out): inside each, the pretrained weight is SVD-decomposed once; buffers U [out, r], Vt [r, in], S [r] stored frozen (snowflake icon, register_buffer note) with a trainable singular-value modulation dial γ (per singular value, init ones — "rescale σᵢ by γᵢ") plus an additive low-rank adapter branch B [out, r], A [r, in] (init zeros/randn, purple) — forward \(y = x\,(\gamma \odot S)\)-weighted \((U, V_t)\) + \(x(A^\top B^\top)\)-style path + original bias (cloned) → GQA heads [B, L, H, Dh]/[B, H_kv, L_kv, Dh] (fused-weight averaging node w_oriented.view(out_dim, rep, −1).mean) → dense attention scores [B, H, L, L_kv] with causal/padding masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Caption: "attention math unchanged; the projection weights are SVD-modulated." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: SVD of pretrained \(W = U\Sigma V^\top\), truncated to rank r; modulation \(W' = U(\gamma \odot \Sigma)V^\top\) with γ trainable (spectrum bars with per-bar purple dials); residual \(W'' = W' + B A\); forward \(y = xW'' + b\); orthogonality-preservation note "singular vectors frozen — only magnitudes adapt." Attention equations standard. Visuals: spectrum with dials, frozen U/V strips, additive BA path. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → SALTLinear: x·Vtᵀ [B, L, r] → ×(γ⊙S) [B, L, r] → ·Uᵀ [B, L, D]; + x·Aᵀ [B, L, r] → ·Bᵀ; + bias → [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, Dh).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, Dh]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L_kv, Dh] → GQA expand; scores [B, H, L, L_kv] → masks → softmax → @V → merge → out SALTLinear → [B, L, D]. Parameter strip: U [out, r] frozen, S [r] + γ [r] trainable, A/B adapters. ## Module: inject_spectron (attention; aliases: inject_spectron, inject_spectron_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of SpectronAttention — importance-aware low-rank + sparse attention. [B, L, D] → LowRankSparseLinear projections (q/k/v/out): each drawn as \(y = (x W_{down}^\top) W_{up}^\top + x S^\top + b\) — blue down/up rank-r strips (per-projection ranks chosen non-uniformly from a global budget) plus a trainable sparse residual branch S (mostly-zero matrix with a few purple nonzero cells); a Lagrangian budget panel above: singular-entropy objective allocating larger ranks to projections whose spectra have higher entropy (importance-aware allocation — a bar chart of per-projection ranks under a fixed total budget); a SparsePruningCallback clock icon (periodic magnitude pruning of the sparse residual toward final_sparsity); GQA heads ([B, L, H, Dh], fused-weight averaging and KV-head rep shown via w_k.view(num_kv_heads, rep, head_dim, −1)), RoPE, causal masks → dense attention on the low-rank+sparse projections → merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: inject_squeezing_heads (attention; aliases: inject_squeezing_heads, inject_squeezing_heads_distill)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Squeezing Heads Attention Distillation layer — head-count compression with knowledge distillation. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → head-squeezing panel: the original teacher attention had H heads; the student squeezes to fewer query heads (H′ < H) and H_kv KV heads — the pretrained KV weights folded via w_k.view(num_kv_heads, rep, head_dim, −1).view(kv_head_size, −1) and bias averaging b_k.view(...).mean(dim=1) (drawn as H teacher head wires converging into H_kv student KV wires with mean-funnel nodes) → student attention [B, H′, L, S] (smaller grid next to the teacher's [B, H, L, S] ghost) → distillation lane: attention-map and output matching losses against the frozen teacher (dashed arrows to a teacher silhouette, loss dial) → merge → out proj (similarly squeezed) → [B, L, D]. ## Module: inject_svd_linear_attention (attention; aliases: inject_svd_linear_attention, inject_svd_sequence_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of SVD-Linear Attention — sequence-dimension compression. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused KV weights folded across reps w.view(num_kv_heads, rep, head_dim, −1).view(kv_head_size, −1), bias mean) → GQA heads [B, H, N, d] → the defining step drawn prominently: an SVD panel computes the top-r left-singular subspace of the key/value sequence representation and projects K and V along the SEQUENCE axis from N tokens to r summary positions (N → r funnel on the K and V wires, labeled "sequence dim projected to top-r left singular vectors"), while Q stays full length → attention scores [B, H, N, r] (thin rectangle vs the N×N ghost crossed out) → causal alignment: query token i attends only to compressed keys whose original position ≤ i (a small tril-over-r mask icon) → softmax → @V_compressed → merge → out projection → [B, N, D]. ## Module: inject_token_compressed_attn (attention; aliases: inject_token_compressed_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Token-Compressed Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; q_lin naming) → GQA heads [B, L, H, Dh]/[B, L, H_kv, Dh] → token-importance panel: per-KV-token importance from energy/norm scores (bar chart over tokens), masked to −10000 outside valid positions, [CLS] (index 0) force-preserved (shield icon on the first token); top selection keeps top ⌈compression_ratio·L⌉ tokens → gather via top_idx_exp expand(−1, H_kv, −1, Dh) → compressed K/V [B, H_kv, L′, Dh]; attention: full-length queries against compressed KV [B, H, L, L′]; causal constraint drawn explicitly: "query token i attends only to compressed keys whose original position ≤ i" (position-preserving mask icon); softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: inject_unified_rope (attention; aliases: inject_unified_rope)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of UnifiedRoPE — universal rotary position embeddings across architectures. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, L, H, head_dim]/[B, L, H_kv, head_dim] (repeat-interleave) → unified RoPE panel: frequency table freqs = einsum("i,j->ij", t, inv_freq) with inv_freq = base^(−2j/d) (drawn as a table of rotation angles per dimension pair); rotation applied to Q and K via per-dimension dials (q′ = R(θ_m)q); partial-RoPE badge for models where only a prefix of dimensions rotates (first d_r dims wrapped, rest straight wires) → dense scores q.float() @ k.float().ᵀ × scale → padding/causal masks → softmax → head_mask → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Caption: "one RoPE implementation for every backbone." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(q'_m = R_m q\) with \(R_m\) block-diagonal rotation by angles \(m\theta_j\), \(\theta_j = base^{-2j/d}\) (frequency spectrum bar with base dial); relative-position property \((R_m q)^\top(R_n k) = q^\top R_{n-m} k\) (distance-only dependence inset); partial-RoPE: rotation on first d_r dims, identity on the rest (wire diagram with a wrapped segment); attention equations standard. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, head_dim]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, head_dim] → GQA expand; RoPE: t = arange(L) → freqs [L, head_dim/2] → cos/sin tables → rotate q, k (fp32 node for precision); scores = matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(−1,−2)) × scale → [B, H, L, L]; masks → softmax → head_mask view nodes → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: inject_upi_mamba (attention; aliases: inject_upi_mamba)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of UPI-Stuffed Mamba — Uncertainty-Penalized Interpolated Mamba. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear projections (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → Mamba core: in_proj chunked (x_in, z); depthwise conv1d over the sequence (kernel window drawn); x_dbl chunked into dt_raw and gamma_raw — the gamma lane drawn as an uncertainty estimator producing per-token confidence dials; interpolation panel: the selective scan interpolates between a fast (decayed) and a slow (persistent) state path weighted by gamma, with the uncertainty penalty shrinking the interpolation toward the reliable path when confidence is low (two state chains merging through a gamma-weighted valve); mask scalars m_t = mask[:, t].view(B, 1, 1) gating each step; state initialization from log(arange(1, d_state+1)) (a structured decaying init spectrum drawn); SiLU(z) output gate → out proj → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: SSM \(h_t = \bar{A}_t h_{t-1} + \bar{B}_t x_t\); interpolation \(h_t = \gamma_t h^{fast}_t + (1-\gamma_t)h^{slow}_t\) with uncertainty penalty \(\gamma_t \to \gamma_t(1 - U_t)\), U_t estimated from x (gamma_raw lane); step gate \(m_t\) from the attention mask; Δt from dt_raw (selective timing); output \(y_t = C_t h_t \cdot \mathrm{SiLU}(z_t)\). Visuals: two state chains (fast/slow) merging at a valve, uncertainty dial U_t, SiLU curve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → in_proj → xz chunk → x_in [B, L, inner]; conv1d(x_in.transpose(1,2))[:, :, :L].transpose(1,2) → x_conv; x_dbl → dt_raw, gamma_raw [B, L, …] chunks; per-step scan with m_t = mask[:, t].view(B,1,1); state h [B, inner, d_state]; init from log(arange(1, d_state+1)) spectrum; out × SiLU(z) → out_proj → [B, L, D]. The gamma valve drawn between the two state paths with shapes marked. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of uncertainty-penalized interpolation: a token stream where ambiguous tokens (low confidence, drawn with question-mark dials) shift the state update toward the stable slow path, while confident tokens allow the fast adaptive path; the gamma valve position over the sequence (a trajectory plot); the structured state init spectrum log(1..d_state) drawn as descending bars. Inset: O(L·inner·d_state) vs O(L²) dashed; stability note: interpolation penalization bounds state drift on ambiguous inputs. ## Module: inject_vig (attention; aliases: inject_vig)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ViG Attention — Vision-GNN-inspired 2D grid linear attention with gated fusion. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → pad to N_pad → reshape to a 2D grid [B, Hh, H, W, Dh] (H×W grid drawn with rows/columns marked) → two directional linear-attention lanes: (1) row-wise attention: each grid row attends across its W columns (row scans drawn as horizontal arrows); (2) column-wise attention: each column across H rows (vertical arrows); both with GQA heads ([B, H_kv, …] shared KV) → gated fusion: gates = sigmoid(gate_net(x)).view(B, N_pad, 2, Hh, 1) — a per-token two-way dial choosing row vs column evidence → merge grid back to sequence → out proj → [B, N, D]. Small inset: the same mechanism as a grid graph (ViG heritage: tokens as nodes, row/column edges). Visuals: H×W grid with row/col arrows, per-token fusion dial, kernel-mapped attention formula. ## Module: inject_vspec_attn (attention; aliases: inject_vspec_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of VSpec++ — Variational Spectral Token-Adaptive Attention. [B, N, D] → four coordinated sub-modules drawn in pipeline: (1) VariationalSpectralGate: input-dependent variational gate over truncated singular values — stats head emits μ and log σ (chunk(2) node) forming a distribution over rank usage, masked pooling over valid tokens; (2) HeadGate: learned per-example head importance scalars (H dials, masked-pooled); (3) TokenRouter: saliency-score top-k token selection with guaranteed [CLS] retention (shield on token 0; gather via idx.expand(−1, −1, D)) → routed tokens [B, k_len, D]; (4) DynamicLowRankProjector: SVD-initialized low-rank projection z = x·V, out = z·Uᵀ whose effective rank is scaled by the variational gate (rank dials turning with μ±σ) plus purple LoRA adapters → attention over routed tokens [B, H, k_len, Dh] with valid/causal masks (−10000) → outputs scattered back; head gates weight the merged heads → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: variational rank gate \(r_{eff} = \mathrm{diag}(\mu + \sigma\epsilon)\), ε~N(0,1) over the spectrum (spectrum bars with fuzzy dial bands); router saliency \(s_t = w^\top \tilde{x}_t\), \(\mathcal{S} = \mathrm{TopK}_{\rho N}(s)\), token 0 forced in; dynamic projection \(y = x\,\Gamma(\mu,\sigma) V^\top U^\top\)-form with gate-scaled factors; head weighting \(O = \sum_h \alpha_h O_h\), α learned per example. Visuals: μ±σ band on the spectrum, saliency bars with cut, shield, head dials. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → spectral gate: stats.chunk(2) → μ, log σ [B, rank]; head gate [B, H]; router: saliency [B, N] → masked_fill(~valid, −10000) → topk idx → gather routed [B, k_len, D]; projector: z = matmul(x_routed, V) [B, k_len, rank] → ×gate → out = matmul(z, U.t()); Q/K views [B, H, k_len, Dh]/[B, H_kv, k_len, Dh] → GQA expand → scores → masked_fill(~valid_mask) and (~causal_mask[0]) → softmax → @V → scatter back to [B, N, D] → ×head gates → out proj. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the variational spectral gate: a singular spectrum where each bar carries a μ±σ confidence band (drawn as fuzzy dial ranges) — the effective rank sampled per input; three example inputs (easy → low effective rank, hard → high) with their sampled cuts; the token router's saliency map with the CLS shield; per-example head gates differing between the same three inputs. ## Module: inject_wgqa (attention; aliases: inject_wgqa)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of WGQA — Weighted Grouped-Query Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → robust GQA pooling panel: teacher KV heads folded into H_kv groups via w_k_or.view(num_kv_heads, rep, head_dim, −1).view(kv_head_size, −1) with per-group bias averaging (mean funnels drawn over head wires) → learnable per-KV-head importance weights (parameter [num_kv_heads, 1, 1] dials, init ones — one weight dial per group drawn on each KV wire) multiplying each group's contribution → GQA head groups (H query heads per KV group) → RoPE with fallback badge (falls back gracefully when positions unavailable) → scores [B, H, L, L_kv] ×scale → padding + causal masks → softmax → @V (group weights applied to K/V or outputs — shown at the group wires) → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: group folding \(W_k^{(g)} = \mathrm{mean}_{rep}(\mathrm{reshape}(W_k))\); group weights \(\beta_g\) (learnable, init 1); weighted attention \(O = \sum_g \beta_g\, \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q K_g^\top}{\sqrt{d}}) V_g\) over G groups; RoPE fallback note. Visuals: H teacher wires → G weighted group wires; β dials; a learned importance bar chart over groups (some groups down-weighted after training). ## Module: jamba (attention; aliases: jamba, jamba2)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Jamba-2 Hybrid Block — SVD-Mamba + MHA + Expert-Choice MoE. [B, L, D] → three branches: (1) SVD-Mamba lane: SVD-factorized in/out projections (U·diag(S)·Vᵀ strips + purple LoRA), causal conv, selective SSM state chain; (2) MHA lane: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin → GQA heads [B, L, H, head_dim] ([B, H_kv, …] shared KV), pos_ids arange(L) RoPE node, scores q.float() @ k.float().ᵀ × scale → causal_mask and valid_mask masked_fills (−1e4) → softmax → @V; (3) Expert-Choice MoE lane: experts pick their top-k tokens (column-wise routing icon), expert outputs scattered back; layer-type ratio dial mixing the lanes (Jamba-style interleaving: most layers SSM+MoE, a few attention) → out projections → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: SSM \(h_t = \bar{A}_t h_{t-1} + \bar{B}_t x_t\) with SVD-factorized projections; attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{QK^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M)V\) with GQA grouping; expert-choice routing \(E \ni t\) top-k per expert; block composition \(x' = x + \mathrm{MHA/SSM}(\mathrm{LN}(x))\), \(x'' = x' + \mathrm{MoE}(\mathrm{LN}(x'))\). Visuals: state chain, triangular ghost, column-wise expert selection, interleave pattern strip (S,S,A,S,S,A…). ## Module: jamba_info (attention; aliases: jamba_info)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Information-Bottlenecked Jamba block — differential attention + concept-bottlenecked SSM, dual modes. [B, L, D] → differential attention sub-block: paired projections W_q1/W_k1 and W_q2/W_k2 (LoRALinear, frozen + purple branch; weights reshaped via w.reshape(w.size(0), −1)) producing two score maps whose difference cancels common-mode noise (two heatmaps minus shared haze → crisp difference); SSM branch through InfoMambaConceptBottleneck: the state is squeezed through a bottleneck_dim concept layer — drawn as a narrow conceptual strip between wide state tensors, with a sparsity dial enforcing sparse concept usage; mode switch: two operational modes (mode A: precision-oriented with stronger attention lane; mode B: compression-oriented with stronger bottlenecked SSM) drawn as a two-position switch; RoPE, causal masks → merge → [B, L, D]. Visuals: subtraction of spectra, narrow bottleneck strip, sparse concept bars, mode switch. ## Module: kda (attention; aliases: kda, kda_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of KDA — Kernelized Decay Attention / recurrent linear attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused weights averaged) → GQA heads ([B, L, H, d]; dense reference lane attn_scores = q.float() @ k.float().ᵀ [B, H, L, L] drawn ghosted) → kernelized decay core: per-head learned decay dials ([num_heads, 1, 1] parameters) and spherical normalization (q, k projected onto the unit sphere — arrows clipped to a circle) → chunked recurrence: within each chunk, intra attention attn_intra = (qc @ kcᵀ) ⊙ D_intra with the decay matrix D_intra (distance-weighted mask drawn inside the chunk); across chunks, decayed associative states: chunk_S = k_decayedᵀ @ vc (state box per chunk boundary) and chunk_Z = k_decayedᵀ @ 1 (denominator tracker box) → readout num/den normalization → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: decayed kernel attention \(S_{ij} \propto \phi(q_i)^\top \phi(k_j)\, \lambda^{i-j}\) with learned per-head λ (decay curves of different steepness per head); spherical normalization \(\phi(x) = x/\|x\|\); chunked form: intra \(O_{intra} = (Q_c K_c^\top) \odot D_{intra}\, V_c\); inter \(S_c = (\Lambda K_c)^\top V_c\), \(Z_c = (\Lambda K_c)^\top \mathbf{1}\); readout \(y = \frac{q(S + \ldots)}{q(Z + \ldots) + \epsilon}\). Visuals: sphere, λ dials, chunk boundary states, denominator box. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand → normalize to sphere; chunk loop: D_intra [c, c] decay matrix; attn_intra [B, H, c, c]; chunk_S [B, H, d, d] and chunk_Z [B, H, d, 1] accumulated across chunk boundaries with λ-decayed keys (k_decayed); reference dense scores [B, H, L, L] ghost; readout + normalize → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of kernelized decay: distance-decay curves λ^(i−j) drawn per head (flat for persistent heads, steep for recency heads); the decay matrix D_intra inside a chunk (banded fading mask); the decayed key trick — keys pre-multiplied by their remaining decay before entering the cross-chunk state (k_decayed arrows dimming with distance); sphere-clipped feature arrows. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: leka_v1 (attention; aliases: leka_v1)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LEKAv1 — Linear-time Efficient Kernel Attention. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch; GQA H_kv) → RFF kernel panel: features = einsum("bhnd,hdf->bhnf", x_norm, proj) — L2-normalized inputs projected by a fixed random Fourier matrix into f features (cos/sin bank drawn; Gaussian-RBF-kernel approximation badge, γ = 2.0 dial); linear attention via cumulative associative memory: kv_outer = einsum("bhnf,bhnd->bhnfd", K_features, v) accumulated causally (KV_cum running sum drawn as a prefix-sum chain), num = einsum("bhnf,bhnfd->bhnd", Q_features, KV_cum), denom = einsum("bhnf,bhnf->bhn", Q_features, z_cum).unsqueeze(-1).clamp(min=1e-5) (denominator tracker lane + ε guard) → merge → out proj → [B, N, D]. bert_mode switch icon (causal/bidirectional). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: RBF kernel approximation \(e^{\gamma q^\top k} \approx \phi_\gamma(q)^\top \phi_\gamma(k)\) with RFF features \(\phi(x) = \sqrt{2/f}\,[\cos(x r_i + b_i)]_i\) (cosine bank graphic; γ = 2.0 sharpness dial); normalized linear attention \(y_t = \frac{\phi(q_t)^\top \sum_{j\le t}\phi(k_j) v_j^\top}{\phi(q_t)^\top \sum_{j\le t}\phi(k_j) + 10^{-5}}\) via cumsum; complexity O(N·f). Visuals: kernel bump vs its RFF approximation overlay, cumsum chain, clamp guard. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → query view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; key/value [B, H_kv, N, d] → GQA expand; normalize → features [B, H, N, f] via fixed proj [H, d, f]; kv_outer = einsum("bhnf,bhnd->bhnfd") → [B, H, N, f, d] running cumulative (causal mask on the scan); num = einsum("bhnf,bhnfd->bhnd") → [B, H, N, d]; denom = einsum("bhnf,bhnf->bhn").unsqueeze(-1).clamp(min=1e-5) → [B, H, N, 1]; y = num/denom → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the RFF kernel approximation: the exact Gaussian kernel surface e^{γqᵀk} drawn as a smooth bump; its RFF reconstruction (sum of f random cosine bumps) overlaid — converging as f grows (three inset overlays for f = 32/64/128); the normalization trick converting unnormalized kernel attention into a valid average (numerator/denominator lanes); the clamp guard at 1e-5. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with LEKAv1 highlighted (normalize → RFF → cumsum attention). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(N·f) vs O(N²) dashed; kernel-approximation quality-vs-f curve; bert_mode switch. ## Module: leka_v2 (attention; aliases: leka_v2, leka_v2_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LEKAv2 — Linear-time Efficient Kernel Attention with Landmark Distillation. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch; GQA H_kv) → two projection lanes proj1 = x·W1 and proj2 = x·W2 feeding the kernel map (dual random-feature lanes) → landmark distillation panel: a small set of landmark tokens selected by top-k saliency (topk over scores; [1, 1, N, 1] saliency strip drawn along the sequence); their features gathered via K_land = gather(K_features, 2, idx…) and V_land = gather(V, 2, idx…) → attention splits: queries attend to (a) landmarks (tiny N×m score grid, m landmarks ≪ N) and (b) a local window lane ([1, H, 1, 1] window dial); landmark outputs distilled back to non-landmark tokens (dashed distillation arrows) → merge → out proj → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: kernel features \(\phi(x) = \mathrm{proj}_2(\mathrm{proj}_1(x))\)-lane composition (two matmul lanes drawn); landmarks \(\mathcal{L} = \mathrm{TopK}_m(s)\) by saliency s; attention \(O_t = \sum_{l \in \mathcal{L}} \alpha_{tl} V_l + O^{window}_t\) with \(\alpha = \mathrm{softmax}(\phi(q)^\top \phi(K_\mathcal{L})/\sqrt{d})\); distillation \(L_{distill} = \|\,O^{teacher}_{non\text{-}landmark} - g\,O^{landmark}_{broadcast}\|\) (dashed arrows). Cost O(N·m + N·w). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → query view(B, N, H, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, head_dim]; k/v [B, H_kv, N, head_dim] → GQA expand; proj1/proj2 → features [B, H, N, feat_dim]; saliency [1, 1, N, 1] → topk idx [B, H, m] → gather K_land [B, H, m, feat_dim], V_land [B, H, m, head_dim]; landmark scores [B, H, N, m]; window lane scores [B, H, 1, L]-style band; combine → [B, H, N, head_dim] → merge → [B, N, D]. Landmark gather drawn as m spotlights along the sequence. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of landmark distillation: a sequence where a few tokens are stamped as landmarks (purple flags); every non-landmark token's attention is distilled from its nearest landmarks (dashed teaching arrows from landmark outputs); the teacher (full attention ghost) guiding landmark placement via saliency; a coverage diagram: m landmarks + window w covers the sequence with O(N·m) cost. ## Module: leka_v3 (attention; aliases: leka_v3, leka_v3_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LEKAv3 — Linear-time Efficient Kernel Attention with Value-Scored Landmarks. Same skeleton as LEKAv2 (LoRALinear q/k/v with GQA, dual projection lanes f1 = x·proj1 into kernel features, landmark gather K_land/V_land via idx.expand, window lane, distillation back) with the V3 delta drawn prominently: landmark selection uses VALUE scores — the saliency strip [B, 1, N] is computed from value magnitudes (which tokens carry useful content) rather than query-key saliency; pad scores masked at −1e4 before selection; [1, H, 1, 1] per-head window dials retained. ## Module: linformer (attention; aliases: linformer, linformer_attention) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of Linformer Attention. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen base + purple rank-r LoRA branch; three-branch config handling note; fused weights averaged across head reps) → GQA heads [B, H, N, d] ([B, H_kv, N, d] shared KV) → the defining projection: two learned projection matrices E [k, N] and F [k, N] (per head, drawn as short wide matrices) project the SEQUENCE dimension of K and V from N down to k projection dimensions: K_proj = E·K → [B, H, k, d], V_proj = F·V → [B, H, k, d] (N → k funnel on the K/V wires with the [k, N] matrices drawn squeezing the token axis) → scores = q @ K_projᵀ × scale → [B, H, N, k] thin strip (the N×N ghost crossed out) → causal/position handling (pos_ids + RoPE node; causal fallback for decoders) → softmax → @V_proj → merge → out projection → [B, N, D]. Preservation-gate note: "gate init σ(−3) ≈ 0.047 → ~95% passthrough at init." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: sequence projections \( \tilde{K} = E K\), \( \tilde{V} = F V\) with \(E, F \in \mathbb{R}^{k\times N}\), k ≪ N (two small wide matrices squeezing the token axis drawn); attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q\tilde{K}^\top}{\sqrt{d}})\tilde{V}\) — O(N·k) vs O(N²); per-head E/F (shared or head-wise toggle); preservation gate \(h_{init} = \sigma(-3) \approx 0.047\) keeping outputs near pretrained activations at init. Spectrum-style inset: E/F as learned low-rank summaries of token similarity. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → query view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; key/value → [B, H_kv, N, d] → GQA expand; E_act [k, N] @ k [B, H, N, d] → K_proj [B, H, k, d]; F_act @ v → V_proj [B, H, k, d]; scores = q @ K_projᵀ × scale → [B, H, N, k]; masks/RoPE; softmax → @V_proj → [B, H, N, d] → merge → [B, N, D]. Parameter strip: E/F [k_proj_dim, max_seq_len] per head, LoRA A/B, folded Q/K/V weights. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the E/F sequence projections: N token vectors drawn as a row; E projecting them onto k learned "summary slots" (arrows fanning from many tokens into few slots, weights shown as a small heatmap of E); the causal caveat drawn honestly — a decoder variant using causal-chunked projections (blocked arrows) since global E breaks causality; the preservation gate dial at init. ## Module: lola (attention; aliases: lola, lola_attention, lola_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LoLA — Low-Rank Linear Attention with Sparse Caching. [B, N, D] → LowRankLinear q/k/v projections (frozen SVD base W = U·V + purple LoRA·scaling branch) → GQA heads [B, H, N_q, d]/[B, H_kv, N_k, d] → two-tier memory system drawn as two lanes: (1) sparse recent cache: the most recent tokens' exact K/V (a short thick wire, "recent_k/recent_v" boxes); (2) low-rank summary cache: older context compressed via LowRankCache — a projection to low_rank_k basis, orthogonalized (q_orth node) and stored as low_rank_k_buf = q_orth(q_orthᵀ rk) and low_rank_v_buf (two thin buffer strips representing the compressed past); attention: queries hit the rank-r summary of old tokens + exact recent tokens (y = rk·q_proj readout lane shown) → combine → masks/RoPE → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: projections \(W = UV + s\,BA\); summary \(\tilde{K}_{old} = P K_{old}\), \(\tilde{V}_{old} = P V_{old}\) with P an orthogonalized low-rank projector (q_orth(q_orthᵀ·) drawn as a projection onto a subspace); attention \(O_t = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q[\tilde{K}_{old}; K_{recent}]^\top}{\sqrt{d}})[\tilde{V}_{old}; V_{recent}]\); memory budget note: old context costs O(rank) instead of O(length). Visuals: recent exact strip + thin summary strip, orthogonal projector icon, combined KV timeline. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_proj (U·V+LoRA) → view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N_q, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, N_k, d] → GQA expand; cache build: rk/rv → q_orth projections → low_rank_k_buf [B, H, rank, d], low_rank_v_buf; scores vs [summary; recent] → [B, H, N_q, rank + w_recent]; masks → softmax → @V-combined → merge → [B, N, D]. The KV timeline drawn as: thick recent segment + thin rank-r summary segment for the past. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the two-tier cache: a KV timeline where the boundary between "recent exact" and "old compressed" slides with each step (the oldest exact entries fall off into the low-rank summary — an arrow from the exact strip into the thin buffer); the orthogonal projector ensuring the summary is the best rank-r approximation of the evicted block (projection onto subspace icon); memory-growth curve flat vs linear cache. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with LoLA highlighted (factorized projections → two-tier cache → attention). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: memory O(rank + w_recent) vs O(N) KV cache; quality-vs-rank curve; streaming-context note. ## Module: lora_attn (attention; aliases: lora_attention, lora_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LoRA Attention — parameter-efficient fine-tuning baseline. [B, N, D] → four LoRALinear projections (q/k/v/out), each drawn as a frozen pretrained base W (big blue block, snowflake) plus a trainable low-rank residual branch: down A [lora_rank, in] and up B [out, lora_rank] (thin purple strips, alpha/r scaling noted) — y = Wx + (α/r)B(Ax) → GQA heads [B, H, N_q, d] ([B, H_kv, N_k, d] shared KV, repeat-interleave node) → standard dense attention: scores Q·Kᵀ × scale [B, H, N_q, N_k] → padding valid-mask + causal tril masked_fill(−10000) → softmax fp32 with nan_to_num → @V → head_mask view → merge → out LoRALinear → [B, N, D]. Caption: "exact dense attention; only the rank-r adapters train." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(y = W_0 x + \frac{\alpha}{r} B A x\) with W₀ frozen, A/B trainable (A init random, B init zeros → ΔW = 0 at start, drawn as an initially-zero contribution gauge); attention equations standard with masking. Trainable-parameter note: r(in+out) per projection vs in·out frozen. Visuals: frozen block, thin A/B strips, scaling dial α/r, zero-at-init ΔW gauge. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N_q, D] → q_proj: W₀x + (α/r)·x·Aᵀ·Bᵀ → view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N_q, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, N_k, d] → GQA expand; scores matmul × scale → [B, H, N_q, N_k]; masked_fill nodes; softmax(fp32, nan_to_num) → @V → [B, H, N_q, d] → merge → out proj → [B, N_q, D]. Parameter strip: W₀ [out, in] frozen; A [r, in], B [out, r] trainable. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the LoRA branch anatomy: the base weight heatmap with a thin low-rank update sheet sliding alongside; A initialized random (colored noise strip) and B zeroed (empty strip) so the initial update is exactly zero (a ΔW gauge at 0); the α/r scaling dial; a training trajectory showing ΔW growing inside the rank-r subspace; trainable-parameter counter (e.g., 0.5% of base). ## Module: lsh (attention; aliases: lsh, lsh_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LSH Attention (Reformer-style). [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, N_q, d]/[B, H_kv, N_k, d] → hashing panel: angular random hyperplane projections — dots = matmul(norm, proj) with a fixed random hyperplane grid (snowflake icon); each token's sign pattern determines its hash bucket (tokens drawn as dots falling into labeled buckets, similar tokens sharing buckets); rounds dial (multiple hash rounds drawn as r_hash parallel bucketings, an object votes if it collides in any round) → bucketed attention: queries attend only to keys in the same bucket (small per-bucket blocks over the N×N ghost) with a fallback-context lane for unmatched tokens (dashed arrows to a local window) → padding/causal masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: hash \(h(x) = \mathrm{sign}(R x)\) with random R (hyperplane cuts through a point cloud drawn — points on the same side share a bit); bucket attention within collisions across rounds; complexity O(N·N/num_buckets); fallback context for singleton buckets. Visuals: hyperplane random grid, bucket histogram, multi-round voting icon, per-bucket mini attention blocks. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N_q, D] → query view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N_q, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, N_k, d] → GQA expand; hash: norm → matmul(·, proj) → signs → bucket ids [B, N] per round; sort/group by bucket → block-diagonal score pattern [B, H, N, N] (computed per bucket, not dense); masks → softmax(fp32, nan_to_num) → @V → merge → [B, N_q, D]. Bucket histogram drawn above the sequence. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of angular LSH hashing: a 2-D point cloud of token embeddings; three random hyperplanes slicing it into 2³ = 8 buckets (sign-pattern labels per region); similar tokens landing in the same bucket (two close dots highlighted); a second-round grid slightly rotated — a token's neighborhood unioned across rounds (two shaded regions overlapping); the per-bucket attention block for one bucket. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with LSH attention highlighted (hash → bucket attention + fallback). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(N²/buckets) vs O(N²) dashed; recall-vs-buckets curve; multi-round cost note. ## Module: mamba (attention; aliases: mamba) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of Mamba — gated selective state-space model as a drop-in attention replacement. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear in-projection → chunk into x and gate z [B, L, D] each → depthwise causal conv1d over the sequence (kernel window drawn sliding) → selective SSM core drawn as an unrolled 4-step chain: per step, input-dependent Δt dials (selectivity — the step size changes with content), state update h_t = Ā_t h_{t−1} + B̄_t x_t with a state box per channel ([D, d_state]), output C_t h_t; decay built from A = −exp(A_log) ≤ 0 with discrete γ = sigmoid(·) ∈ (0,1) (decay rulers per channel drawn); a distance-based causal decay matrix (distance = i − j for i ≥ j) shown as the closed-form view [L, L, D] with strictly lower-triangular support; SiLU(z) multiplicative gate on the output → out projection → [B, L, D]. Caption: "strictly O(L) time and space." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: continuous SSM \(h'(t) = Ah(t) + Bx(t)\), \(y = Ch\); discretization \(\bar{A} = \exp(\Delta t\, A)\) with \(A = -\exp(A_{log}) \le 0\) (decay guaranteed); selective mechanism: Δt = s_\Delta(x) input-dependent (per-token step-size dials); gated output \(y_t = C_t h_t \cdot \mathrm{SiLU}(z_t)\) (SiLU curve); closed-form causal decay view \(w_{ij} = \gamma^{i-j}\) for i ≥ j. Visuals: decay exponentials per channel, selectivity dials varying along the sequence, SiLU gate, triangular decay ghost vs linear state chain. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → in_proj → [B, L, 2D] → chunk → x, z [B, L, D]; x → conv1d (causal, [B, D, L] layout note) → proj chunk p1, p2; weights = softmax(log_weights.masked_fill(~causal_mask, −inf), dim=1) → [L_q, L_k, D] decay-attention view; p1_causal = einsum("ijd,bjd->bid", weights, p1) → [B, L, D]; × SiLU(z); out view(B, L, H·Dh) handling → out_proj → [B, L, D]. The [L, L, D] decay tensor drawn as structured (distance-based), not dense-learned. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of selectivity: three token types along a sequence — a filler token (small Δt dial: state barely updates), a key fact (large Δt: state writes strongly), a repeated token (negative-style gating forgetting the stale copy); the per-channel state heatmap evolving with these writes; the A = −exp(A_log) decay spectrum (all negative, drawn on a log axis) guaranteeing stability; LoRA adapters on projections. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer block with Mamba replacing attention entirely (residual + MLP unchanged). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA (pretrained attention weights transferred into in/out projections — funnel icon). Inset: O(L) time/memory curves vs O(L²) dashed; throughput-vs-context chart. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: mamba_svd_flash (attention; aliases: mamba_svd_flash)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MambaSVD-Flash — hybrid bidirectional SSM with online-SVD state transitions plus a FlashAttention path. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear projections (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → hybrid core with two coupled lanes: (1) SSM-SVD lane: state h evolves through per-step SVD-factorized transitions — U = proj_u(x).view(B, L, D, rank) and V = proj_v(x).view(B, L, rank, D) (two thin per-step strips); recurrence v_{t−1} = V_tᵀ h_{t−1} → s_t = b_t·s_{t−1} (decay b_t from mask m_t = mask[:, t].view(b,1)) → h_next = U_t s_{t−1} + b_t x_t drawn as an unrolled chain with low-rank transition matrices; (2) FlashAttention lane: standard Q/K/V GQA heads [B, L, H, head_dim] running a tiled flash kernel (online softmax tiles) for exact local interactions; per-step or per-layer gating fusing lanes → merge → out proj → [B, L, D]. RoPE on the attention lane; causal/bidirectional switch. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: low-rank state transition \(h_t = U_t s_{t-1} + b_t x_t\) with \(s_t = b_t s_{t-1}\), gates b_t ∈ (0,1) from input; projection back \(v_{t-1} = V_t^\top h_{t-1}\) (rank-r bottleneck drawn); Flash lane \(O^{fa} = \mathrm{FlashAttn}(Q, K, V)\) (online softmax tile note); fusion \(O = g\,O^{ssm} + (1-g)O^{fa}\). Visuals: two thin per-step factor strips replacing a dense transition matrix, decay rulers, tile grid, fusion dial. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → per step t: v_t_1 = bmm(V_t, h.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1) → [B, rank]; h_next = bmm(U_t, s_t_1.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1) + b_t·x_t → [B, D]; m_t = mask[:, t].view(b, 1) gate; U/V shapes [B, L, D, rank], [B, L, rank, D]; attention lane: Q = q_proj.view(B, L, num_heads, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d] → flash kernel → [B, H, L, d]; fuse → merge → [B, L, D]. Both lanes drawn with shapes at each arrow. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of online SVD transitions: at each step, the transition operator drawn not as a dense [D × D] matrix but as two thin factor strips U_t, V_t computed from the current input (input-dependent, low-rank — an adaptive operator); the state passing through the rank-r bottleneck s; the mask-derived gate b_t gating writes; beside it the flash tiles of the exact-attention lane; a capacity note: rank-r transitions × O(L) steps. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer block with MambaSVD-Flash highlighted (SSM-SVD lane + flash lane + gate). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(L·D·rank + flash-local) vs O(L²) dashed; bidirectional mode icon (parallel forward/backward scans). ## Module: minimal_aurora (attention; aliases: minimal_aurora)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MinimalAURORA — Energy-Preserving SVD-Factorized Attention. [B, N, D] → FactorisedLinear q/k/v (frozen SVD base up·down with energy-based rank selection + purple LoRA lora_rank branch; fused weights averaged across reps W.view(out, rep, −1).mean) → GQA heads [B, N, H, D]/[B, N_kv, H_kv] → "energy-preserving" emphasis: an energy gauge per projection showing the retained singular-energy fraction at the chosen rank (a dial pinned at the energy threshold); dense attention scores Q·Kᵀ × scale fp32 [B, H, N, N_kv] → masks → softmax → @V → merge → factorized out projection → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(W = W_{up}W_{down} + BA\) with rank from energy \(\sum_{i\le r}\sigma_i^2 \ge E \sum_i \sigma_i^2\); energy-preservation note: factors scaled so the retained subspace's response energy matches the original (norm-preservation gauge); attention \(P = \mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt{d} + M)V\). Visuals: spectrum with energy cut, norm gauge, LoRA strips. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_proj: x·downᵀ [B, N, rank] → ·upᵀ (+LoRA) → view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, D]; k/v folded across reps → [B, H_kv, N_kv, D] → GQA expand; scores [B, H, N, N_kv] fp32 × scale → masks → softmax → @V → merge → out FactorisedLinear → [B, N, D]. Energy gauge node on each factorization. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

[B, N, D] → FactorisedLinear q/k/v/out blocks, each drawn in full detail: pretrained weight W SVD-decomposed → frozen up/down strips (W = W_up·W_down, rank from the energy threshold with a spectrum-cut graphic) + trainable purple LoRA residual branch (A [lora_rank, in], B [out, lora_rank]); fused multi-head pretrained weights averaged across reps (W.view(out, rep, −1).mean(dim=1) funnel drawn once) → GQA heads [B, H, N, D] over [B, H_kv, N_kv, D] (repeat-interleave node) → dense attention: scores = Q·Kᵀ × scale in fp32 [B, H, N, N_kv] → padding + causal masks (−10000) → softmax → @V → merge → factorized out projection → [B, N, D]. Caption: "the reference template all minimal-family variants derive from."
