## Module: mla (attention; aliases: mla, mla_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MLA — Multi-Head Latent Attention (DeepSeek-V2, arXiv 2405.04434). [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q_proj (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → latent compression core: keys and values are first projected DOWN into a shared low-dimensional latent c_KV (kv_dim ≪ D — drawn as a bottleneck strip), cached in KV memory as the latent only; at attention time the latent is projected back UP via k_up/v_up into per-head K/V ([B, kv_len, H_kv, head_dim] each; teacher KV weights folded w_k_f.view(kv_dim, rep, −1).mean) → GQA-style head expansion → scores q·kᵀ × scale fp32 → masks → softmax → @V → head_mask → merge → out proj → [B, L, D]. Caption: "cache the latent, not the heads — KV memory shrinks to kv_dim." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: latent \(c^{KV}_t = W^{DKV} x_t \in \mathbb{R}^{d_c}\) (bottleneck strip); up-projection \(k_t = W^{UK} c^{KV}_t\), \(v_t = W^{UV} c^{KV}_t\); attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{QK^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M)V\); cache note: store c only → KV memory ∝ d_c, not H_kv·d_h (two cache bars). Decomposition inset: W^{UK}·W^{DKV} factor view (up·down strips) showing the low-rank structure. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj → [B, H, L, head_dim]; latent: x → W_DKV → c [B, L, kv_dim] (this is what the cache stores — cache icon on the strip); per use: k = k_up(c) view(B, kv_len, H_kv, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H_kv, kv_len, head_dim]; v = v_up(c) same; GQA expand → scores [B, H, L, kv_len] × scale → masks → softmax → @V → head_mask view → merge → out proj → [B, L, D]. The up/down factor pair drawn on the KV path. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of latent KV caching: a KV timeline where each step stores only the thin latent vector c (small purple squares) instead of full K/V head blocks (large ghost squares crossed out); at read time, the up-projections reconstruct per-head K/V from the latent (fan-out from one thin wire to H_kv wires); memory bars: full KV vs latent-only at several context lengths; a quality note on reconstruction fidelity. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with MLA highlighted (down → cache latent → up → attention). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: KV-memory compression d_c/(H_kv·d_h) bar; compute unchanged O(N²) — a memory-focused module. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: moba (attention; aliases: moba, moba_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MoBA — Mixture of Block Attention for long contexts. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused weights averaged across reps) → GQA heads [B, L, H, d]/[B, L_kv, H_kv, d] → block-mixture panel: the sequence split into blocks of block_size (a row of boxes); the N×N attention ghost replaced by a block-level grid [nb, nb]; per query block: (1) a guaranteed causal current-block lane (dense within the diagonal block — drawn as the tril diagonal cells) and (2) a learned gate selecting top_k past blocks (gate scores over past blocks [nb]-strip → top-k purple cells in the query's grid row); attention runs only query-block × selected-blocks ([B, H, nb, nb] block-score view) → softmax → @V gathered from selected blocks → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: block scores \(S_B = Q_{blk} K_{blk}^\top/\sqrt{d}\) at block granularity; selection \(\mathcal{B}(i) = \mathrm{TopK}_k(g_i)\ \cup\ \{\text{current block}\}\) with gate g learned per query block; token attention within selected blocks \(O_i = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q_i K_{\mathcal{B}(i)}^\top}{\sqrt{d}}) V_{\mathcal{B}(i)}\); cost O(L·k·B). Visuals: block grid with diagonal + k purple cells per row, gate bars per block, causal safety of the current block. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L_kv, d] → GQA expand; reshape into blocks [B, H, nb, B_s, d]; block-level gate scores → top_k selection [B, H, nb, k]; gather selected K/V blocks; token-level scores within selection → masks (current-block causal) → softmax → @V → scatter to [B, H, L, d] → merge → [B, L, D]. Block grid drawn to scale vs the dense ghost. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the mixture-of-blocks router: a query block's gate bar chart over all past blocks; top-k blocks highlighted (content-relevant ones — e.g., for a question block, the blocks containing its answer); the current block always attended (causal diagonal); a hop-analysis note: any token reachable in one hop if selected, else via later blocks — coverage diagram across three consecutive query blocks. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with MoBA highlighted (block router + selective block attention). Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. ## Module: moe_deltakv_paged (attention; aliases: moe_deltakv_paged)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MoE-DeltaKV-Paged hybrid attention. [B, N, D] → q/k/v projections → heads [B, H, N, d] → MoBA-style block routing panel (query blocks attend to their top-k selected KV blocks; block grid with purple selected cells) over a Paged Delta-KV cache: the KV cache organized into pages (a paged memory array of blocks drawn); each page stores base KV plus a low-rank delta correction (thin strips per page); the deltas are quantized to 4-bit uniform or SVD-compressed via a Straight-Through Estimator node (STE icon: dashed gradient arrow bypassing the quantize step) so the 4-bit rounding stays differentiable; attention reads k_hat (base + dequantized delta) → scores q @ k_hatᵀ × scale → pad mask (−10000) → softmax → @V → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: paged cache with base + delta \(KV = KV_{base} + \Delta\); quantized delta \(\hat\Delta = \mathrm{STE}(Q_4(\Delta))\) (4-bit grid + STE bypass arrow drawn); SVD delta \(\hat\Delta = U_rV_r\); block selection \(\mathrm{TopK}_k\) per query block; attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q\hat{K}^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M)V\). ## Module: nasc (attention; aliases: nasc, nasc_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of NASC — NEXUS-AEGIS Spectral Cascade Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused weights rep-averaged) → GQA heads [B, H, L, d] → cascade of three sparsity scales drawn as a waterfall: (1) local sliding-window lane (window_size band over the score ghost); (2) inter-block top-k lane: blocks of block_size, per-query-block top_k block selection (block grid with purple cells); (3) global RFF lane: random Fourier features (proj [head_dim, rff_features] fixed grid) giving O(L) global coverage; routing logits [B, 1, 1, 3] — a softmax dial over the three scales per token; masks (mask, pad_k at −10000) → scale-fused softmax outputs combined → merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: nexus (attention; aliases: nexus)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of NEXUS — Neural Efficient X-scoring Unified Sparse Attention (4 components). [B, L, D] → LoRALinear projections → GQA heads [B, H, L, D] → component 1, XAttentionScorer (training-free): antidiagonal sub-sampling — Q and K sampled at stride σ, attn_sample = Q_sample @ K_sampleᵀ × scale evaluated along block-antidiagonal positions to score block importance cheaply (a block grid with an antidiagonal line of sampled cells highlighted); component 2, SeerGating: learnable block gates from low-rank pooled query-key features (q_pool/k_pool → rank-gate_rank matmul → head-wise sparsity budgets, temperature dial); component 3, DualStageStripeAttention: intra-block dense attention + top-k inter-block attention with stripe granularity (stripe_mask cells over the [B, H, L, L] score matrix, masked_fill −10000); component 4, SpectraformerRectify: RFF global linear attention for full coverage → combined output → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: antidiagonal scoring \(S_{antidiag} = \mathrm{sample}_\sigma(Q) \mathrm{sample}_\sigma(K)^\top\) (training-free — snowflake badge); Seer gate \(g = \frac{(W_q^{pool}Q)(W_k^{pool}K)}{\tau}\) → head-wise sparsity budget; stripe attention: intra dense + TopK inter blocks; RFF rectification \(\phi(x)R\)-based global linear lane; fusion of the dual-stage and global outputs. Visuals: antidiagonal sampling line over a block grid, gate network stripes, RFF ribbon. ## Module: nystrom (attention; aliases: nystrom, nystrom_attention) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of Nyströmformer attention. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads → flatten to [B·H, d, N] transposed views → landmark selection: m landmarks (segment-means or learned) drawn as m summary columns alongside the N token columns → three matrix products of the Nyström approximation drawn as a triad: F-type products Q·Û (tokens→landmarks), Û·(landmarks→landmarks pseudoinverse), and (landmarks)·Kᵀ (landmarks→tokens); iterative pseudoinverse panel: V = αAᵀ; V ← V(2I − AV) Schulz iterations (pinv_iterations loop drawn as a circular arrow with a convergence gauge) → assembled low-rank attention \(O = \tilde{Q}(\tilde{K}^+\tilde{V})\) without forming N×N → merge → [B, N, D]. Caption: "O(N·m·d) associative linear multiplication." Visuals: three thin landmark matrices, the pseudoinverse loop, the N×N ghost crossed out. ## Module: orion (attention; aliases: orion, orion_attention, orion_module)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ORION — Optimized Rectified Input-aware Orthogonal Network. [B, L, D] → FactorisedLinear projections (zero-shot SVD energy-ranked up·down base, min_rank/max_rank bounds + purple LoRA) → GQA heads → head-wise bifurcation: heads split into two populations: (a) linear-kernel heads: normalized Q/K → RFF features via einsum("bhnd,hdf->bhnf", Q_norm, proj).clamp(max=11.0) (stability clamp badge) → causal linear attention via cumsum KV_outer accumulators with denominator clamp(min=1e-5); (b) rectified Softpick full-attention heads: full softmax-style attention with a rectified (Softpick) activation replacing softmax (clipped-below-zero curve drawn) → head outputs concatenated → merge → out projection → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: factorized \(W = W_{up}W_{down} + BA\) with rank in [min_rank, max_rank]; RFF lane \(\phi(x) = \mathrm{clamp}(x^\top R, 11.0)\) with normalization; linear attention \(y_t = \frac{\phi(q)^\top \mathrm{cumsum}(\phi(k)v^\top)}{\phi(q)^\top \mathrm{cumsum}(\phi(k)) + 10^{-5}}\); Softpick lane \(P = \mathrm{Softpick}(S)\) (rectified activation curve inset — positive outputs with exact zeros); head bifurcation note. Visuals: clamp box, cumsum chain, Softpick curve vs softmax. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → projections → [B, H, L, Dh]; linear heads: Q_proj/K_proj [B, H, L, f] clamped → kv_outer einsum "bhnf,bhnd->bhnfd" cumsum → num "bhnf,bhnfd->bhnd" → [B, H, L, d]; Softpick heads: scores [B, H, L, L] → rectified activation → @V; concat head populations → merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: padre (attention; aliases: padre, padre_attention; registered under compression_engine)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of PADRe — Polynomial Attention with Dropout Regularization. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads → score computation S = q·kᵀ × scale [B, H, N, N] → the polynomial core: instead of softmax, the centered stable polynomial (1 + S/order)^order drawn as a curve replacing the exponential (order dial); invalid positions masked via masked_fill for max computation and zeroed after weighting (dropout regularization nodes on the attention weights — small drop icons); head_mask views → weighted sum with V (unnormalized-style kernel weighting, normalization discussed in the math panel) → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: polynomial kernel \(P(S) = (1 + S/n)^n \to e^{S}\) as n = order grows (curve family: n = 2, 4, 8 converging to exp); stability centering of S; dropout regularization \(P \leftarrow P \cdot \mathrm{mask}/(1-p)\) (drop icons); masking \(P_{invalid} = 0\); output \(O = P V\) with normalization as needed. Visuals: polynomial curves approaching exp, dropout mask speckle, order dial. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_proj view(B, N, H, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; k/v [B, H_kv, N, d] → GQA expand; scores [B, H, N, N] × scale; masked_for_max = scores.masked_fill(invalid_mask, −1e9) → polynomial evaluation (1 + S/n)^n; attn_weights.masked_fill(invalid_mask, 0.0); dropout nodes; × head_mask views → @V → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: performer (attention; aliases: performer, performer_attention) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of Performer — FAVOR+ positive orthogonal random features. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear query/key/value (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, N, d] with Q/K pre-scaled by √softmax-stability factor (× scale before features) → FAVOR+ feature panel: random matrix ω (positive orthogonal random features — drawn as a structured random grid, snowflake; orthogonal rows keeping features diverse); features φ(q) = exp(ωᵀq − |q|²/2)-style positive maps drawn as a cos-based bank with exponential positivity badge → linear attention: kv_outer = einsum("bhnm,bhnd->bhnmd", K_feat, V) — a [m, d] outer-product state per token accumulated via cumsum (causal) or sum (bidirectional); readout num/denominator lanes with ε guard (eps) → merge → out projection → [B, N, D]. Caption: "softmax kernel approximated by positive random features; O(N) memory, never forms N×N." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: softmax kernel \(e^{q^\top k}\) unbiasedly approximated by \(\hat{k}(q,k) = \mathbb{E}_\omega[h(q,\omega)h(k,\omega)]\) with positive features \(h(x,\omega) = \frac{e^{\omega^\top x}}{\sqrt{m}\,e^{\|x\|^2/2}}\) (positivity → denominators valid); orthogonal random features (rows of ω orthogonal → lower variance); linear form \(O = \frac{\phi(Q)(\phi(K)^\top V)}{\phi(Q)\phi(K)^\top\mathbf{1}}\); causal via cumsum. Visuals: kernel bump vs feature-average reconstruction (m growing), orthogonal random grid, cumsum accumulator. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → query view(B, N, H, d).transpose(1,2) × scale → [B, H, N, d]; proj = x @ ωᵀ → features [B, H, N, m]; kv_outer = einsum("bhnm,bhnd->bhnmd", K_feat, V) → [B, H, N, m, d] cumsum accumulators; numerator/denominator contractions → [B, H, N, d] and [B, H, N, 1] (+eps); output → merge → [B, N, D]. The [B, H, N, m, d] accumulator drawn as the largest intermediate (independent of pairwise N²). ## Module: phantom (attention; aliases: phantom, phantom_module)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of PHANTOM — Parameter-Hashed Attention with Token-wise Orthogonal Mapping. [B, L, D] → FactorisedLinear_CUR projections: weights compressed via leverage-score CUR decomposition (leverage scores computed from empirical statistics, drawn as per-row/column importance bars selecting actual rows/columns C and R of W — "CUR picks real rows/columns, not abstract factors" caption) + purple LoRA branch → GQA heads → multi-round angular LSH sparse attention: hq = (Q_norm·hash_proj > 0) @ bit_shifts — normalized tokens hashed by random hyperplane sign patterns into integer bucket ids ([B, H, N] id strips per round); scores_base masked to bucket matches per round (match cells kept, others −1e4), rounds unioned; val_mask and causal masks (−1e4) → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Rectified Softpick activation badge on the attention weights. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: CUR \(W \approx C U R\) with columns/rows chosen by leverage scores \(\ell_i = \|U_{i,:}\|^2\) (bars selecting rows of the actual matrix — interpretability note); hash \(h(x) = \mathrm{bitsign}(x^\top P) \cdot 2^j\) (bit-shift integer ids); bucket attention over matching ids across rounds; Softpick rectified weighting. Visuals: leverage bars over a weight grid picking columns, hash bits forming an integer, multi-round bucket union. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → CUR projections → heads [B, H, L, d]; hash: Q_norm @ hash_proj → signs → @ bit_shifts → hq [B, H, N] (same hk); per round: match = hq == hk → scores_round = scores_base.masked_fill(~match, −1e4); union rounds + causal + val masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: primal (attention; aliases: primal, primal_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Primal Attention — asymmetric kernel SVD in primal representation. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads → segment-pooling landmarks: Q_land = Q.reshape(B, H, m, seg, d).mean(dim=3) — the sequence divided into m segments, each pooled to one landmark (mean funnels over token groups) → primal factorization panel: F1 = Q·K_landᵀ [L×m], F2 = Q_land·K_landᵀ [m×m], F3 = Q_land·Kᵀ [m×L] (three thin products drawn as the Nyström-style triad in primal form, no softmax on F2); iterative Schulz refinement of the F2 pseudoinverse: X_{t+1} = X_t(2I − F2·X_t) with pinv_iters loop icon → assembled output O = F1·pinv(F2)·F3·V → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: landmark pooling \(\tilde{Q} = \mathrm{segmean}(Q)\), \(\tilde{K} = \mathrm{segmean}(K)\); primal factors \(F_1 = Q\tilde{K}^\top/\sqrt{d}\), \(F_2 = \tilde{Q}\tilde{K}^\top/\sqrt{d}\), \(F_3 = \tilde{Q}K^\top/\sqrt{d}\); Schulz refinement \(X_{t+1} = X_t(2I - F_2 X_t)\); output \(O = F_1 X F_3 V\). Asymmetric-kernel note (Q and K treated asymmetrically in primal space). Visuals: segment-mean funnels, three thin matrices, iteration loop with residual gauge. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → query permute → [B, H, L, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand; Q_land = reshape(B, H, m, seg, d).mean(3) → [B, H, m, d]; F1 [B, H, L, m], F2 [B, H, m, m], F3 [B, H, m, L]; Schulz loop on F2; O = F1·X·F3 @ V → [B, H, L, d] → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: ptp (attention; aliases: progressive_token_pruning, ptp)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of PTP — Progressive Token Pruning. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q_lin/k_lin/v_lin (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → learnable pruning gate panel: a scorer computes per-token importance; keep_ratio dial keeps top ⌈ρ·L⌉ tokens with guaranteed [CLS] retention (shield on token 0); pruned tokens bypass via an identity lane (dashed wire carrying them unchanged) → attention over the kept subset: q vs k_pruned (gathered via top_idx_exp expand(−1, H, −1, head_dim)) → attn_scores [B, H, L, k_tokens] × scale → position-aware causal mask (q_pos = arange(L) compared against kept-token positions, masked_fill −1e4) → softmax → @V_pruned → outputs reassembled with the bypassed tokens → merge → [B, L, D]. **Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: importance \(s_t = w_p^\top x_t\) (learnable); selection \(\mathcal{S} = \mathrm{TopK}_{\rho L}(s) \cup \{0\}\); causal consistency \(M_{ij} = -\infty\) if \(\mathrm{pos}_j > i\) using kept-token original positions; attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{QK_\mathcal{S}^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M)V_\mathcal{S}\); bypass \(O_t = x_t\) for t ∉ S. Visuals: importance bars, cut line, shield, position-labeled kept tokens, bypass lane. ## Module: qcqa (attention; aliases: qcqa, qcqa_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of QCQA — Quality and Capacity-Aware Grouped Query Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q_proj (full H heads) / k_proj, v_proj (compressed H_kv heads) → RoPE on q/k (rotate then permute lanes shown) → continuous routing panel: instead of hard GQA group sharing, each query head synthesizes its own KV as a learned convex combination of the compressed KV heads: route_logits per query head → route_weights = softmax(route_logits, dim=-1) (a [H × H_kv] routing matrix drawn, each row a distribution — soft blending of KV heads rather than repeat-interleave) → per-head K_h = Σ_g w_hg K_g, V_h = Σ_g w_hg V_g → attention per head with its synthesized KV [B, L, L] scores → causal masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. router_temp dial on the softmax temperature (capacity/quality trade). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: routing \(w_h = \mathrm{softmax}(r_h/\tau)\) over KV heads (τ = router_temp dial); synthesized KV \(K^{(h)} = \sum_g w_{hg} K^{(g)}\), \(V^{(h)} = \sum_g w_{hg}V^{(g)}\) (convex combination — interpolation between sharing patterns); attention \(O^{(h)} = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q^{(h)}K^{(h)\top}}{\sqrt{d}} + M)V^{(h)}\); limits: τ → 0 recovers hard GQA, τ → ∞ averages all (diagram of both extremes). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj view(B, L, H, head_dim) → permute → [B, H, L, d]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, d] → RoPE (q_rot/k_rot) → route_weights [H, H_kv] → per-head blend K_h/V_h [B, H, L, d]; scores [B, H, L, L] → masks → softmax → @V_h → merge → [B, L, D]. Routing matrix drawn as a small heatmap beside the blend node. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of continuous KV routing: the routing heatmap [H × H_kv] with soft rows (each query head a distribution over KV heads); an example head blending 70% of KV head 1 and 30% of KV head 3 (arrows with weights); the temperature dial sweeping between hard GQA (one-hot rows) and full averaging (uniform rows); learned routing matrix after training showing structured blocks. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with QCQA highlighted. Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: KV-cache at H_kv heads (GQA-level savings) with near-MHA quality; routing-temperature quality curve; compute remains O(N²) — memory-focused. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.


## Module: rala (attention; aliases: rala, rala_attention, rala_linear_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of RALA — Rank-Augmented Linear Attention. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute head layout) → GQA heads [B, H, N, d]/[B, H_kv, N, d] → chunked linear scan with a rank-augmented state: sequence split into chunks; intra-chunk lane: intra_attn = q_c k_cᵀ ⊙ causal_mask (small block-diagonal grids); inter-chunk lane: recurrent accumulator S ← S + k_cᵀ v_c at chunk boundaries plus a static learned head-interaction matrix rank_mat = einsum("h d r, h e r -> h d e", U, V) (per-head U, V thin strips forming a low-rank prior over head interactions) added at readout: inter_out = q_c @ (S + rank_mat) → combined → merge → [B, N, D]. Caption: "true linear-time recurrence — O(chunk·d²) memory, no O(N·d²) allocation." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: chunk recurrence \(S_c = S_{c-1} + K_c^\top V_c\); rank augmentation \(R_h = U_h V_h^\top\) per head (einsum shown); intra-chunk masked scores \(S^{intra} = (Q_cK_c^\top)\odot M_{causal}\); output \(O = Q_c(S_{c-1} + R) + O^{intra}\); memory bound O(chunk·d²). Visuals: chunk boxes with block-diagonal masks, U/V strips forming R, memory ceiling marker. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_proj permute → [B, H, N, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, N, d] → GQA expand; chunk loop: q_c/k_c/v_c [B, H, c, d]; intra scores [B, H, c, c]; S [B, H, d, d] per chunk boundary; rank_mat [H, d, e] broadcast-add; readout [B, H, N, d] → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the rank-augmented head prior: the accumulated S heatmap with the learned low-rank sheet R = UVᵀ sliding beneath it (per-head strips); effect: a trainable "prior" over which head interactions matter, unavailable to pure accumulation; the chunked-scan memory timeline with the O(N·d²) allocation crossed out. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with RALA highlighted. Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(N·d + C·d²) vs O(N²) dashed; peak-memory bars chunked vs naive. ## Module: reap_moe (attention; aliases: reap_moe)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of REAP-MoE — Robust Expert Allocation and Pruning MoE attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q_lin/k_lin/v_lin (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads → REAPMoE panel: router logits [B·L, E] + noise (noise_std dial) → softmax → top-k selection (topk_weights softmax-renormalized; topk_indices expand to gather expert outputs); experts as SVDLinear blocks (down/up rank-r bottleneck strips each, use_svd toggle); load-balancing regularizer: density = expert_mask mean [E] pulled toward router mean (springs icon); robust pruning note — persistently low-mass experts pruned (grayed expert with X) → attention scores fp32 × scale → masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: router \(g = \mathrm{softmax}(\mathrm{TopK}_k(W_rx + \epsilon))\), ε~N(0, σ²); SVD experts \(E_e(x) = (xW^\top_{down,e})W_{up,e}\); balance loss \(L_{aux} \propto E\sum_e \overline{density}_e\bar p_e\); pruning rule for starved experts; attention \(O = \mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt{d}+M)V\). Visuals: rank-r expert bottlenecks, top-k cells, balance springs, pruned expert. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_lin permute → [B, H, L, head_dim]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, head_dim] → GQA expand; MoE: logits [B·L, E] → router_probs → density [E]; topk_indices [B, L, k] → topk_expanded [B, L, k, D] → gather → weighted sum [B, L, D]; scores [B, H, L, L] fp32 × scale → masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. ## Module: regla (attention; aliases: regla, regla_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ReGLA — Refined Gated Linear Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v/gate (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads → element-wise key gating: g = gate_proj(x) multiplying keys per channel (dials on key wires) → linear attention: kv = k_gatedᵀ @ v [B, H, d, d] accumulator; num = q @ kv; den = (q @ zᵀ).clamp(min=1e-5) (denominator wire with floor guard); local refinement: short-range depthwise 1D conv (kernel_size dial) over the output adding local inductive bias; head_mask multiply → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: gated keys \(\tilde k = k \odot \gamma(x)\); \(y_t = \frac{q_t^\top \sum_{j\le t}\tilde k_j v_j^\top}{q_t^\top z_t + 10^{-5}}\); DWConv refinement \(y \leftarrow y + \mathrm{DWConv}_K(y)\); head-mask weighting. Visuals: channel dials, cumsum chain, clamp floor, conv slider. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj permute → [B, H, L, d]; k/v/g [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand; kv = matmul(k_gated.transpose(−2,−1), v) → [B, H, d, d]; num = matmul(q, kv) [B, H, L, d]; den [B, H, L, 1] clamp(1e-5); conv lane [B, D, L]; × head_mask view → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the refinement trio: key-gate dials (which key channels enter memory), the clamped denominator floor, and the depthwise conv slider restoring local sharpness to the globally-smoothed output (before/after curves); receptive-field sketch (global associative arrows + conv window). ## Module: res_svd (attention; aliases: res_svd, spectral_residual_svd)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Res-SVD — Spectral Residual Low-Rank SVD Attention. [B, N, D] → per projection (q/k/v/out): W = U_base V_baseᵀ + α·B·A drawn as a frozen truncated-SVD base (blue strips at base_rank) PLUS a trainable residual adapter BA initialized directly from the top-k residual singular directions of (W − W_base) — a spectral-init funnel drawn: the discarded tail's strongest directions seed the adapter (purple strips "born from the residual spectrum"); scaling α dial; factorize_base / freeze_base toggles → GQA heads (permute layout) → dense attention scores fp32 × scale → softmax → head_mask multiplies → @V → merge → [B, N, D]. Caption: "the adapter recovers exactly what truncation threw away." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(W = U_{base}V_{base}^\top + \alpha\,BA\) with \(BA\) initialized from top-k singular directions of \(W - U_{base}V_{base}^\top\) (residual spectrum feeding the adapter — arrow from the cut tail into the BA branch); α scaling; frozen base lock, trainable adapter flame; attention equations standard. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → projection: x·V_baseᵀ [B, N, base_rank] → ·U_baseᵀ; residual: x·Aᵀ [B, N, residual_rank] → ·Bᵀ × α; sum → [B, N, out]; q permute → [B, H, N, D]; k/v [B, H_kv, N, D] → GQA expand; scores [B, H, N, N] → softmax → head_mask views → @V → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of spectral residual initialization: the weight's singular spectrum with the base cut at base_rank; the discarded tail re-decomposed and its top residual_rank directions drawn flowing into the A/B adapter parameters (init arrows) — so step-0 reconstruction error ≈ tail beyond base+residual, dramatically smaller than base-only; error-bar comparison base-only vs base+residual adapter. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: routing (attention; aliases: routing, routing_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Routing Attention — content-based clustering for sparse attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads → online k-means panel: queries and keys partitioned into num_clusters content clusters over n_iters refinement iterations (token scatter with star centroids and assignment lines; centroid updates via einsum("bhlk,bhld->bhkd", one_hot, Q)) → exact intra-cluster attention: attention confined to same-cluster neighborhoods (block-sparse pattern over the [B, H, L, L] ghost — colored cluster blocks on the diagonal and off); exact masking node (total_mask, −1e4) preserving causality and padding; softmax fp32 → @V → head_mask → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: online k-means assignment \(a_t = \arg\min_k \|q_t - c_k\|^2\), centroid drift update; intra-cluster attention \(O_t = \mathrm{softmax}(\frac{Q_tK_{C(t)}^\top}{\sqrt{d}} + M)V_{C(t)}\) with exact masking (causal ∩ cluster); cost O(Σ|C|²) ≈ O(L²/K). Visuals: scatter with clusters, block-sparse pattern, mask intersection diagram. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → q_proj permute → [B, H, L, d]; k/v [B, H_kv, L, d] → GQA expand; one_hot assignments [B, H, L, K] → centroids einsum → [B, H, K, d]; total_mask (cluster ∧ causal ∧ valid) → scores.masked_fill(~total_mask, −1e4) → softmax → @V → head_mask → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: rpc (attention; aliases: rpc, rpc_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of RPC — Random Projection Compressed Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear W_q/W_k/W_v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads → sketching panel: fixed head-wise Gaussian random projections proj [H, d, r] (snowflake buffers); q_sketched = einsum("b h l d, h d r -> b h l r", q, proj) and k_sketched likewise (d → r funnels on Q/K wires) → compressed scores q_sketched @ k_sketchedᵀ × scale fp32 [B, H, L, L] computed with r-dim inner products → masks → softmax → head_mask → @V (full precision values) → merge → [B, L, D]. Johnson-Lindenstrauss badge: "pairwise similarities preserved in expectation." Visuals: point-cloud projection with preserved distances, frozen grid snowflake, error-vs-r curve. Funnel glyphs on the d → r step. ## Module: salt (attention; aliases: salt, salt_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of SALT — Singular Value Adaptation with Low-Rank Tuning. [B, L, D] → SALTLinear projections (q/k/v/out): pretrained W SVD'd once; frozen buffers U [out, r], Vt [r, in], S [r] (register_buffer snowflakes); trainable singular-value modulation γ [r] (init ones) rescaling σᵢ plus additive low-rank adapters B [out, r], A [r, in] (purple, zeros/randn init); original bias cloned → forward y = x·(γ⊙S)-weighted (U, Vt) + x·AᵀBᵀ + b → GQA heads (fused-weight rep-averaging funnel) → dense attention with masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Caption: "directions frozen — only singular magnitudes adapt." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(W = U\Sigma V^\top\) truncated to r; \(W' = U(\gamma\odot\Sigma)V^\top + BA\) (γ per singular value — spectrum bars with purple dials); forward \(y = xW'^\top + b\); orthogonality-preservation note (U/V frozen keeps the subspace aligned). ## Module: simple_ssm (attention; aliases: simple_ssm)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of SimpleSSM — lightweight selective state-space block. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear projections → expand → symmetric depthwise 1D conv over the sequence (d_conv window) → selective discretization: input-dependent Δt dials; diagonal state transitions A (log_dA = −dt·A [B, L, D] drawn per-channel decay exponents) → parallel scan via kernel matrices: forward K_fwd and backward K_bwd built from pairwise log-differences C_diff[b,d,i,j] = C[b,i,d] − C[b,j,d] (a [B, D, L, L] lower/upper-triangular decay-kernel pair drawn) → h_fwd/h_bwd = K @ u → × D skip → bidirectional sum (or causal-only) → multiplicative gating → out projection → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: discretization \(\bar A_t = e^{-\Delta t A}\) (diagonal A, [B, L, D]); kernel form \(h_{t} = \sum_{j\le t} e^{-(C_i - C_j)A}u_j\) with C = cumsum(Δt) (pairwise log-difference matrix graphic); readout \(y = h\odot D + \mathrm{gate}\); symmetric conv prefilter; bidirectional sum for encoders. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → conv → x_ssm; dt [B, L, D]; log_dA = −dt·A.view(1,1,D); C = cumsum → [B, D, L]; C_diff [B, D, L, L]; K_fwd/K_bwd; u_tr [B, D, L, 1]; h = matmul(K, u_tr).squeeze(−1).transpose(1,2) → [B, L, D]; ×D → gate → out → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the kernel-matrix scan: the [L × L] lower-triangular decay kernel built from exponentiated log-differences (cell (i,j) colored by e^{−(C_i−C_j)A}); its backward twin; input-dependent Δt making kernel cell values content-adaptive; the symmetric conv prefilter icon. ## Module: spectraformer (attention; registered under compression_engine; aliases: spectraformer, spectraformer_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Spectraformer — random-feature linear attention with flexible kernel approximation. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads [B, H, N, d] → kernelization panel: Q_proj = ReLU(q·R)·scale and K_proj = ReLU(k·R)·scale with R a random orthogonal projection (snowflake grid; ReLU making features non-negative for a valid denominator) → recurrent linear scan: state ← state + kᵀ_t v_t ([B, H, r, d] state box) and z_state ← z_state + kᵀ_t ([B, H, r, 1] denominator tracker), readout num_t = q_t·state normalized by z (causal unrolled chain; bidirectional full-sum variant icon) → merge → out projection → [B, N, D]. Caption: "exact linear-time O(N·r·d) computation." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: kernel map \(\phi(x) = \mathrm{ReLU}(xR)\cdot s\) (orthogonal random R — variance-controlled features); linear attention \(y_t = \frac{\phi(q_t)^\top S_t}{\phi(q_t)^\top z_t}\), \(S_t = S_{t-1} + \phi(k_t)v_t^\top\), \(z_t = z_{t-1} + \phi(k_t)\); complexity O(N·r·d); flexible-kernel note (R's structure controls the induced kernel family). Visuals: ReLU curve, random grid, state + denominator boxes, cumsum chain. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_proj view(B, N, H, head_dim).transpose(1,2) → [B, H, N, d]; k/v → [B, H_kv, N, d] → GQA expand; features [B, H, N, r]; scan: state [B, H, r, d], z_state [B, H, r, 1]; num_t [B, H, 1, d] per step → collected [B, H, N, d] → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of orthogonal random features: feature directions drawn as spread orthogonal axes (vs correlated iid ghost); ReLU positivity ensuring a non-negative denominator; the induced kernel surface compared with the softmax kernel (approximation quality inset vs r). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** Transformer layer with Spectraformer highlighted. Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: O(N·r·d) vs O(N²) dashed; state memory constant in N. ## Module: spectral_mamba (attention; aliases: spectral_mamba)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Spectral-Mamba attention module — pretrained attention fused with a spectrally-gated SSM. [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q_lin/k_lin/v_lin (frozen + purple rank-r branch; permute layout) → GQA heads → two branches: (1) multi-head attention lane: scores fp32 × scale → softmax → head_mask multiplies → @V (pretrained weights carried — copy funnel); (2) SpectralMamba lane: in_proj chunked (x1, x2), selective SSM state scan (d_state) with SiLU gating, output modulated by learned per-channel spectral gates ([B, N, 1] broadcast) → adaptive learned gating fusing the branches per token → merge → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: attention lane \(O^a = \mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt d + M)V\); SSM lane \(h_t = \bar A h_{t-1} + \bar B x_t\), \(y = (C h)\odot G_{spec}\odot\mathrm{SiLU}(x_2)\); fusion \(O = g\,O^a + (1-g)\,O^{ssm}\), g = σ(W_g x). Visuals: dense ghost, state chain, spectrum dials, gate curve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, N, D] → q_lin permute → [B, H, N, head_dim]; k/v [B, H_kv, N, head_dim] → GQA expand; attn scores [B, H, N, N] → softmax → head_mask views → @V; SSM: in_proj.chunk(2) → scan → spectral gate [B, N, 1]; fuse → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of spectral channel modulation: SSM output channels as a bar spectrum shaped by a learned attenuation curve (long-range structure boosted, noise trimmed); fusion gate trajectory (attention-heavy locally, SSM-heavy on long-range drift). ## Module: spectron (attention; aliases: spectron, spectron_attention, spectron_attn)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Spectron — importance-aware low-rank + sparse attention. [B, L, D] → LowRankSparseLinear projections: \(y = (x W_{down}^\top)W_{up}^\top + x S^\top + b\) — blue down/up strips with per-projection ranks from a Lagrangian spectral-entropy budget (allocation bar chart) plus a trainable sparse residual S (few purple nonzero cells); SparsePruningCallback clock (prune_freq dial; kthvalue threshold scissors on magnitude histograms toward final_sparsity); GQA heads with fused-weight rep-folding; RoPE, causal masks → dense attention → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(y = (xW_{down}^\top)W_{up}^\top + xS^\top + b\); Lagrangian budget \(\max_{\{r_p\}}\sum_p H(\Sigma_p) \text{ s.t. } \sum r_p = R\) (entropy bars → rank bars); magnitude pruning keep top-k via kthvalue threshold (schedule dial). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → x·W_downᵀ [B, L, r_p] → ·W_upᵀ + x·Sᵀ + b → [B, L, out]; q view → [B, H, L, Dh]; k/v rep-folded [B, H_kv, L_kv, Dh] → GQA expand; scores → masks → softmax → @V → merge → [B, L, D]. Rank bars r_q/r_k/r_v differ. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of entropy-ranked allocation + sparse residuals: four spectra feeding an uneven budget split (highest-entropy projection gets the largest rank); the sparse-residual lifecycle (dense-ish init → periodic thresholding → few surviving purple entries compensating the low-rank cut); error bars uniform vs entropy allocation. ## Module: squeezing_heads (attention; aliases: squeezing_heads, squeezing_heads_distill, squeezing_heads_distiller)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Squeezing Heads distillation layer — head-count compression via KD. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → head-squeeze funnel: pretrained H-head K/V weights folded to H_kv heads (w_k.view(num_kv_heads, rep, head_dim, −1).view(kv_head_size, −1), bias rep-averaged) — mean-funnels over grouped head wires; student attention with fewer heads → distillation lane: attention-map KL + output matching losses against the frozen teacher (teacher silhouette, dashed arrows, loss dial) → merge → out proj → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: head folding \(W^{sq}_k = \mathrm{mean}_{rep}(\mathrm{reshape}(W_k))\); student attention standard; \(L = \alpha\,\mathrm{KL}(P^{tea}\|P^{stu}) + \beta\|O^{tea} - O^{stu}\|^2\). Visuals: H→H′ funnel, teacher/student heatmap pair with KL bracket, savings bar. ## Module: svd (attention; aliases: svd, svd_sequence, svd_sequence_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the SVD sequence-compressed attention implementation (svd_attention.py). [B, N, D] → LoRALinear q/k/v (frozen + purple rank-r branch; fused KV rep-folding) → GQA heads → sequence-subspace SVD: top-r left singular vectors of the KV sequence compress K/V from N tokens to r summary positions (N → r funnel) → thin attention scores [B, H, N, r]/√d → softmax → @V_compressed → merge → [B, N, D]. O(N·r) badge; N×N ghost crossed out. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

Visuals: N→r squeeze, spectrum cut. ## Module: svd_linear (attention; aliases: svd_linear, svd_linear_attention, svd_linear_attn) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of SVD-Linear Attention — the framework's namesake module: SVD-factorized projections + kernelized linear attention with exponential decay gating. [B, N, D] → SVDLinear projections (q/k/v): frozen \(y = (xV^\top)U^\top + b\) with U [out, rank], V [rank, in] strips and Σ absorbed, plus LoRA \(\frac{\alpha}{r}B(Ax)\) purple branch; optional fused Triton kernel lane (use_fused_kernel badge — down-proj → in-SRAM RMSNorm → up-proj drawn as a chip); orthogonality regularizers: U_orth = UᵀU − I, V_orth = VVᵀ − I drawn as penalty gauges keeping factors orthonormal → heads → two attention modes (mode dial): (1) kernelized linear attention with exponential decay gating: φ features, pairwise log-decay matrix log_M[t, j] = log γ_t − log γ_j (j ≤ t) — a data-dependent decay mask; affinity A = φ_q φ_kᵀ normalized; (2) decay-gated dense fallback; causal/bidirectional switch → merge → out projection → [B, N, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: factorized layer \(y = (xV^\top)U^\top + b + \frac{\alpha}{r_{lora}}(xA^\top)B^\top\); orthonormality penalties \(\|U^\top U - I\|\), \(\|VV^\top - I\|\) (gauges); decay gate \(\gamma_t\) with log-decay matrix \(M_{tj} = \gamma_t/\gamma_j\) for j ≤ t; linear attention \(y_t = \frac{\sum_{j\le t}\gamma_t\gamma_j^{-1}\phi(q_t)^\top\phi(k_j)v_j}{\sum_{j\le t}\gamma_t\gamma_j^{-1}\phi(q_t)^\top\phi(k_j)}\). Visuals: U/V strips, penalty gauges, decay-ratio matrix heat sketch. ## Module: zeros (attention; aliases: zeros, zeros_attention)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ZeroS — Zero-Mean Linear Attention. [B, L, D] → LoRALinear W_q/W_k/W_v (frozen + purple rank-r branch) → GQA heads → the defining step: keys are zero-meaned (k̂ = k − mean(k) drawn as arrows re-centered at the origin; "removes the mean" badge eliminating the dominant shared component that saturates linear attention) → ReLU-based non-negative feature maps φ (clipped-at-zero curves) → normalized linear attention: kv = einsum("b h j d, b h j e -> b h j d e", k̂, V) cumulative [B, H, N_k, d, d] state; num = einsum("b h l d, b h l d e -> b h l e", q̂, kv_cum); denominator lane (z-tracking [B, H, L, 1] guard); sim = q̂k̂ᵀ reference view ([B, H, L, N_k] ghost) → RoPE priority note → merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: zero-meaning \(\hat k = k - \bar k\) (before/after vector clouds at the origin); φ = ReLU features (non-negativity plot); \(y_t = \frac{\phi(\hat q_t)^\top \sum_{j\le t}\phi(\hat k_j)v_j^\top}{\phi(\hat q_t)^\top \sum_{j\le t}\phi(\hat k_j) + \epsilon}\); rationale: removing the common mean prevents rank-1 saturation of the associative state. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: [B, L, D] → W_q transpose(1,2) → [B, H, L, Dh]; k/v [B, H_kv, N_k, Dh] → GQA expand → zero-mean → φ; kv_cum [B, H, N_k, d, d] cumulative; num [B, H, L, d]; denom [B, H, L, 1]; sim ghost [B, H, L, N_k]; merge → [B, L, D]. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: a3 (compressor; aliases: a3, a3_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the A3 Compressor — Activation-Aware Factorized SVD with Fisher/Activation-Energy weighting and LayerNorm recalibration. Pipeline left→right: (1) calibration node: streaming activations collected via forward hooks (x = inp.reshape(−1, D) from calibration_loader/texts); (2) statistics panel: online activation covariance C_X = Σ xᵀx accumulated memory-safely + empirical Fisher/activation-energy diagonal proxy (per-channel energy bars); (3) weighted decomposition node: W_weighted = W·C_X^{1/2} → SVD → symmetric factors → A3FactorizedLinear layer drawn as y = (xVᵀ)Uᵀ + ((x Aᵀ)Bᵀ)·α + b with LoRA branch and frozen base; (4) validation node: strict reconstruction check W_rec = U_rV_r vs W (error gauge vs max_error), adaptive rank-retry loop (max_retries dial); failed layers → fallback_to_original (frozen original restored, grayed); (5) LayerNorm recalibration node (recalibrate_ln toggle) re-estimating LN statistics after replacement; target_attention_only filter chip selecting q/k/v/o projections. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: streaming covariance \(C_X = \sum_b x_b^\top x_b\); Fisher proxy \(F \approx \mathrm{diag}(\mathbb{E}[x^2])\); weighted SVD \(WC_X^{1/2} = U\Sigma V^\top\); factorized forward \(y = (xV^\top)U^\top + \alpha(B(Ax)) + b\); energy rule \(\sum_{i\le r}\sigma_i^2/\sum\sigma_i^2 \ge \tau\); reconstruction gate ‖W − U_rV_r‖/‖W‖ ≤ max_error. Visuals: covariance heatmap, Fisher bars, spectrum cut, error gauge, LoRA branch. **Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N_cal, in] → C_X [in, in]; W [out, in] → W·cov_sqrt → U [out, rank], V [rank, in]; A3FactorizedLinear: x [B, …, in] → x·Vᵀ [B, …, rank] → ·Uᵀ [B, …, out]; LoRA x·Aᵀ [B, …, lora_rank] → ·Bᵀ; W_rec = matmul(U, V) + matmul(lora_B, lora_A)·scaling verification; bias copy; replaced-module wiring into the model tree (before/after subtree diagrams). ## Module: aasvd (compressor; aliases: aa_svd, aa_svd_compressor, aasvd) [FLAGSHIP]

## Module: adaptive_svd (compressor; aliases: adaptive_ranks, adaptive_svd)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Adaptive Ranks SVD Compressor. Pipeline: (1) calibration hooks collecting activations (flat_x = x.reshape(−1, in); batch_cov = xᵀx accumulated per batch); (2) activation-weighted SVD: M = W·cov_sqrt → spectra per layer; (3) dynamic rank panel: per-layer rank chosen by cumulative energy \(\frac{\sum_{i\le r}\sigma_i^2}{\sum_j\sigma_j^2} \ge \tau = 0.92\) with min_rank floor — a bar chart of chosen ranks across the model's layers (varying heights); (4) symmetric factor replacement (q/k/v fused weights chunked: w_q, w_k, w_v = W_in.chunk(3)); (5) validation W_recon = U_rV_r vs max_error with retry; fallback valve; protect_critical flag shielding embeddings/LM head; target_attention_only chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: energy rule with τ = 0.92 and min_rank floor (spectrum with cut line and floor marker); whitened SVD \(WC^{1/2} = U\Sigma V^\top\); per-layer rank allocation bar chart; reconstruction gate. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → cov [in, in]; M = matmul(W, cov_sqrt); U_r [out, r_ℓ], V_r [r_ℓ, in] with per-layer r_ℓ; fused QKV chunk(3) handling; forward through symmetric factors; W_recon check; module swap. **Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of per-layer rank adaptation: three layers' spectra with different decay speeds → different chosen ranks (τ line crossing each at a different point); the resulting model-wide rank profile bar chart; critical-layer shields. ## Module: adasvd (compressor; aliases: adasvd)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of AdaSVD — adaptive SVD with per-layer importance allocation and ALS refinement. Pipeline: (1) importance scoring: per-layer spectral importance computed (importance bars across layers); (2) global rank allocation under a target_param_ratio budget (a budget bar split across layers ∝ importance, bounded by min_rank and the reduction constraint r(in+out) ≤ 0.75·in·out); (3) factorization + iterative ALS refinement: A_0, B_0 init → alternate least-squares updates recon = A·B over num_iters (a circular refinement arrow with reconstruction-error gauge shrinking per iteration — recon_0 vs recon comparison); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; Conv1D support; LoRA preservation icon (existing PEFT adapters untouched); per-layer stats emitter. ## Module: admm (compressor; aliases: admm, admm_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the ADMM Compressor — joint pruning + quantization via Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers. Pipeline: (1) target filter (attention-only chip; Conv1D/LoRA preservation icons); (2) per-layer ADMM loop drawn as a three-way split: X (original weights) splits into Z (sparse+quantized codewords — a pruned/quantized grid) and U (scaled dual variables); the alternating updates X-step / Z-step / U-step drawn as a triangular iteration diagram (max_iter loop, rho1/rho2 penalty dials); (3) quantization panel: uniform or non-uniform codebooks (quant_bits dial; k-means-style centroid markers on a value axis); (4) structured 2:4 option (structured_24 toggle: every 4 consecutive weights exactly 2 nonzero — a 2:4 pattern strip); (5) validation: reconstruction on calibration data vs max_error; fallback valve freezing originals. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: ADMM objective \(\min \|Wx - y\|^2 + \lambda_1\|Z\|_0 + \lambda_2\,Q(Z)\ \text{s.t.}\ W = Z\); scaled dual updates W ← (XᵀX + ρI)⁻¹(XᵀY + ρ(Z − U)); Z ← prune/quantize(W + U); U ← U + W − Z (triangular update diagram); 2:4 mask constraint. Visuals: constraint manifold sketch, codebook axis, dual-variable accumulation arrow. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: weight tensor [out, in] → flat = abs().reshape(−1) threshold statistics → per-iteration X/Z/U [out, in]; quantized grid 4/8-bit cells; 2:4 mask [out, in/2, 4]; replaced ADMMMaskedLinear-style forward (mask ∘ weights) → stats (sparsity %, bit-width, block_size). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the ADMM triangle: three matrices (dense W, sparse-quantized Z, dual U) with arrows cycling X→Z→U→X each iteration; the Z-step drawing weights onto a union of {0} ∪ codebook centroids (a value axis with snapped points); rho scheduling note; convergence of objective over iterations. ## Module: admm_pruner (compressor; aliases: admm_pruner, admm_pruner_distill)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the ADMM Pruner — structured/semi-structured pruning with reconstruction distillation, STE, and dynamic rho. Pipeline: (1) calibration forward passes capturing layer inputs (X_calib); (2) ADMM loop (rho dial, dynamic_rho schedule curve, max_iter) producing a binary mask M per layer; (3) ADMMMaskedLinear layer: continuous weights W kept, forward = (W ⊙ M)x with ADMMSTEPrune autograd node — forward masked, backward gradient passed straight through (STE dashed arrow) so training can adapt surviving weights; (4) layer-wise reconstruction distillation lane: pre-computed teacher outputs Y0 = X@W0ᵀ; a distillation loss pulls the masked layer's outputs toward Y0 (dashed teacher arrows, λ_distill dial); (5) 2:4 semi-structured option (tensor-core badge); (6) validation vs max_error; fallback freeze; --export_only note for re-export runs. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: ADMM with mask constraint \(W = M\odot W\); STE: forward \(y = (M\odot W)x\), backward \(\partial y/\partial W = M\) (identity through the mask); distillation \(\mathcal{L} = \| (M\odot W)x - W_0x \|^2\) per layer weighted by λ; dynamic ρ schedule curve; 2:4 pattern per 4-block. Visuals: STE bypass arrow, mask grid, teacher-output funnel, ρ curve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: X_calib [N, in] → ADMM masks M [out, in]; ADMMMaskedLinear: x → (W ⊙ M)x → out; STE autograd graph (forward solid, backward dashed); distillation: Y0 = X@W0ᵀ [N, out] vs Ŷ → loss; res_w = W_final.t().contiguous() weight-write nodes; stats (sparsity, 2:4 eligibility). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: armor (compressor; aliases: armor, armor_compressor, armor_pruner)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ARMOR — Adaptive Resilient Matrix Orthogonal Rank pruning (2:4 sparse core + low-rank correction). Pipeline: (1) saliency panel: resilient first-order Taylor saliency per weight — handles zero-grad, NaN/Inf, and missing activations (shield icons on each failure mode; saliency heatmap); (2) 2:4 structured selection: top-2 per 4-block by saliency (a 2:4 pattern strip with purple kept cells); (3) low-rank error compensation: the residual (W − W_sparse) decomposed into a rank-r correction — ARMORLinear drawn as out = sparse_weight·sparse_mask + scaling·(lora_B·lora_A) with a decoupled gradient-flow diagram (frozen base snowflake; clean gradients flowing only to the adapter branches — decouple toggle); (4) optional LoRA scaling + LayerNorm recalibration; (5) validation W_recon = W_sparse + scaling·B·A vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: saliency \(S_{ij} = |g_{ij}\cdot W_{ij}|\) with resilient fallbacks (grad=0 → |W|, NaN → 0); 2:4 constraint; correction \(BA\) fit to the pruning residual: \(BA \approx \mathrm{SVD}_r(W - W\odot M)\); forward \(y = (M\odot W)x + s\,B(Ax)\); validation gate. Visuals: saliency heatmap, 2:4 strip, residual→BA funnel, dual-branch gradient arrows. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations reshape [N, in]; saliency [out, in] → 2:4 mask; residual → BA [out, rank]×[rank, in]; ARMORLinear forward: x → sparse branch (mask∘W) + adapter branch (s·B(Ax)) → out.view(*orig_shape[:-1], out); W_recon check; stats (sparsity, rank, corrected error vs raw error bars). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.
