## Module: ast (compressor; aliases: ast, ast_pruner)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of AST — Adaptive Semi-Structured 2:4/N:M pruning with Fisher calibration and PCA rotation. Pipeline: (1) activation hooks → covariance cov = centeredᵀ·centered/(N−1) and per-channel empirical Fisher (variance bars); (2) PCA rotation node: eigenvectors Q of the activation covariance; weights rotated into PCA space W̃ = W·Q and Fisher rotated F̃ = F·Q² (a coordinate-frame rotation glyph — pruning becomes axis-aligned and easier); (3) N:M selection: per block of M inputs keep n_keep by rotated Fisher-saliency (a 2:4 pattern strip in rotated space); (4) rotate back W = W̃_pruned·Qᵀ; (5) ASTPrunedLoRALinear layer: frozen 2:4 base (snowflake) + optional LoRA (lora_r, α) — forward through sparse weights; (6) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; protect_critical; recalibrate_ln chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: covariance \(C = X^\top X/(N{-}1)\); PCA \(C = Q\Lambda Q^\top\); rotation \(\widetilde W = WQ\), \(\widetilde F = FQ^{\odot 2}\); N:M selection \(\mathrm{TopN}_n(\widetilde F)\) per M-block; inverse rotation \(W = \widetilde W_{pruned}Q^\top\); LoRA branch. Visuals: rotated coordinate frame, Fisher bars pre/post rotation, 2:4 strip, Q↔Qᵀ round-trip arrows. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → cov [in, in] → Q [in, in]; W [out, in] → W_pca = matmul(W, Q); F_pca = matmul(F_mat.reshape(1, d_in), Q.pow(2)).expand(d_out, d_in); per-block TopN masks [out, in]; W_pruned = W̃·Qᵀ; ASTPrunedLoRALinear forward x [B, in] → sparse matmul → out [B, out]; stats (sparsity, Fisher-weighted error). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of PCA rotation for pruning: an activation scatter with correlated axes; the PCA frame rotated to align with the data's principal directions; in the rotated frame, the N:M mask picks the truly important coordinates (Fisher bars before/after rotation); rotation-back arrows preserving equivalence y = Wx exactly modulo pruned entries. ## Module: astro (compressor; aliases: astro, astro_quantizer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the AstroQuantizer — dynamic uniform range quantization with STE and Fisher-adaptive mixed-precision bit allocation. Pipeline: (1) calibration hooks collecting activations (act_concat); (2) Fisher/activation-energy panel: per-layer importance bars from Empirical Fisher Information or output activation energy; (3) mixed-precision bit allocator: a budget dial (target_avg_bits) distributing bit-widths across layers ∝ importance (a bar chart of per-layer bits from 4 to 8, higher for important layers) — per-channel bits supported (per_channel_bits broadcast view(−1, 1, …)); (4) AstroLinear fake-quantization node: STEFakeQuantize autograd function — clamp to [qmin, qmax] in integer space, de-quantize, forward quantized, backward straight-through (dashed gradient arrow crossing the staircase); symmetric/asymmetric and per-channel scale/zero-point dials; (5) weight write-back W_quant copied into the module; validation vs max_error; fallback valve; protect_critical shields. Visuals: staircase function with straight-through dashed arrow, bit-allocation bars, Fisher bars, codebook axis. ## Module: bonsai (compressor; aliases: bonsai, bonsai_pruner)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Bonsai — iterative magnitude/Taylor pruning (IMP) with recovery and CLS routing protection. Pipeline drawn as a cycle: (1) saliency scoring per weight (magnitude + Taylor |g·w| combined — a saliency heatmap); (2) prune step along a cubic sparsity schedule (schedule curve rising to target sparsity over `steps`; per-step mask update — cells falling to zero); (3) recovery step: gradient-descent fine-tuning of surviving weights for recovery_steps at recovery_lr (error curve dipping between prune steps — the IMP sawtooth); (4) BonsaiPrunedLoRALinear layer: frozen sparse base with persistent mask + trainable LoRA adapters (lora_r, α); (5) protection chips: protect_cls (CLS routing shield), skip_attention_qkv, GQA-aware protection, Conv1D W0 transpose handling; (6) validation vs max_error; fallback valve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: cubic schedule \(s_t = s_{final}(1 - (1 - t/T)^3)\); saliency \(S_{ij} = |W_{ij}|\) or \(|g_{ij}W_{ij}|\); per-step TopK pruning to s_t; recovery \(\min_W \mathcal{L}(M\odot W)\) for recovery_steps; effective forward \(y = (M\odot W)x + \alpha B(Ax)\). Visuals: cubic curve, sawtooth error recovery, mask grid, LoRA branch. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → saliency [out, in] → per-step mask M; W0 = raw_w.t() for Conv1D handling; BonsaiPrunedLoRALinear: x → (M⊙W)x + scaling·B(Ax) → out.view(*orig_shape[:-1], out); cov_X = XᵀX/X.shape[0] for structured options; stats (sparsity per step, 2:4 eligibility). ## Module: cachenotes (compressor; aliases: cachenotes, cachenotes_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of CacheNotes — task-aware KV cache compression with a learnable Compression-Planning-Token generator. Two coupled panels: (A) static weight compression: the CPTGenerator maps a task description (text chip) to continuous per-feature importance vectors (importance bars over channels); these guide a task-weighted SVD of projection weights → CacheNotesLoRALinear (frozen compressed base + optional LoRA; pure-static mode when lora_r = 0); a safe static-heuristic fallback generator drawn as a bypass switch; (B) runtime KV compression: CacheNotesKVCache — as tokens stream in, task-relevance is scored per token (importance strip along the cache axis), non-essential context tokens pruned to keep_ratio with attention-sink tokens preserved (num_sinks shielded slots at the cache head); weighted_k = k_cache·cpt_importance scoring → top selection → idx_exp gather → compacted cache. Arrows from the CPT importance vector feeding both panels ("one plan, two compressions"). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: CPT \(c = G_\theta(\text{task}) \in \mathbb{R}^D\) (importance vector); weight compression guided by c (weighted SVD / channel scaling); KV relevance \(s_t = \langle c, \phi(k_t)\rangle\) → TopK keep under keep_ratio with sink slots fixed; compacted cache \([k_{sinks}; k_{\mathcal{S}}]\). Visuals: task→importance funnel, weighted spectrum, cache strip with pruned segments and shielded sinks. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: task text → CPTGenerator (num_freqs embedding) → c [D]; weights [out, in] → task-weighted SVD → CacheNotesLoRALinear forward x [B, in] → [B, out]; KV cache [B, H, N, D] → weighted_k = k_cache·cpt_importance.view(1,1,1,D) → scores → top_indices → idx_exp gather → [B, H, N′, D]; sink slots [:1].expand cloned. ## Module: compot (compressor; aliases: compot)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of COMPOT — Compressed Orthogonal Dictionary Learning compressor. Pipeline: (1) calibration activations X; (2) basis learning: QR decomposition of activations X = Q·R gives an activation-aligned orthogonal dictionary (Q columns drawn as an orthonormal frame aligned with the data); dictionary refinement via the learning loop: D = W·Rᵀ → A_cand = V_r·R_inv_T → W_recon = B·A with B the orthogonal dictionary and A the coefficient matrix (a dictionary-learning loop with error gauge shrinking); (3) COMPOTLinear layer: y = x·Aᵀ·Bᵀ (B [rank, in] orthonormal, A [out, rank]); (4) validation W_recon vs max_error with retry; fallback valve; Conv1D support; target_attention_only chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(W \approx BA\) with B orthonormal (BᵀB = I — frame gauge) learned from activation QR; \(A = V_r R^{-\top}\) closed-form coefficients; dictionary update loop minimizing ‖W − BA‖_F; comparison note vs data-oblivious SVD (dictionary aligned to what the network actually feeds the layer). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → QR → Q [in, k], R; D = matmul(W, R.t()) [out, k]; SVD of D → V_r; A_cand = matmul(V_r, R_inv_T) [rank, in]; B [rank, in]; COMPOTLinear forward x [B, in] → x·Aᵀ [B, rank] → ·Bᵀ [B, out]; W_recon = matmul(B, A) verification. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the activation-aligned dictionary: a weight factorized in the standard basis vs in the learned orthogonal dictionary basis (both decompositions drawn; the dictionary one sparser/low-rank because the basis follows the data); the QR frame arrows; reconstruction error bars SVD vs COMPOT at equal rank. ## Module: coopq (compressor; aliases: coopq, coopq_quantizer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of CoopQ — cooperative game-theoretic bit-allocation quantizer. Pipeline: (1) calibration; (2) game-theoretic allocation panel: layers as players in a cooperative game — a coalition diagram where each layer's bit-width b_ℓ ∈ {4, 6, 8…} (min_bits ≥ 4 floor enforced) is assigned by marginal-contribution valuations under a global average-bits budget (a coalition/bargaining graphic with per-layer bit bars); (3) per-channel asymmetric calibration: per-output-channel scale and zero-point dials (asymmetric range shift drawn on a value axis); (4) CoopQLinear fake-quant node: CoopQSTEFakeQuantize — forward quantized-dequantized weights per channel, backward straight-through (dashed arrow); Conv1D weight-layout handling (w_data transpose); (5) weight write-back; validation vs max_error; fallback valve; recalibrate_ln chip. **Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: quant \( \hat w_c = s_c\cdot\mathrm{clip}(\mathrm{round}(w_c/s_c + z_c), q_{min}, q_{max}) - z_c s_c\) per channel (asymmetric zero-point); STE gradient; allocation as a cooperative game: bits distributed by marginal sensitivity valuations with floor \(b_\ell \ge \min\_bits \ge 4\) and budget \(\overline{b}\). Visuals: coalition diagram, per-channel scale dials, staircase + STE arrow, floor marker on the bit axis. ## Module: delta_kv (compressor; aliases: delta_kv, delta_kv_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of DeltaKV — residual-based KV cache compression via long-range similarity. Two panels: (A) runtime KV compression: a reference bank holds max_refs historical K/V representatives (a small memory box at the cache head); incoming tokens' keys are compared to references — sim = k·refᵀ/√D → top-k closest references (top_idx over [B, H, N, tk]); each token stored not in full but as a sparse delta residual to its references (a full-KV ghost crossed out beside thin residual strips + reference pointers); residual_threshold dial zeroing small deltas; (B) weight-side modeling: the DeltaKVCompressor decomposes projection weights into a low-rank reference component plus sparse residuals (rank + sparsity dials) mirroring the runtime scheme; validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: references R = last max_refs KV; per token: \(\hat k_t = k_t - \sum_{j\in TopK_{tk}}\alpha_{tj}R_j\), store (α, indices, residual); reconstruction \(\hat k_t + \Sigma\alpha R\); sparse residual thresholding; weight analog \(W \approx W_{ref}(\text{rank-r}) + S\) (sparse). Cache memory bar: full vs refs+deltas. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape trace:** Shape trace: KV [B, H, N, D]; ref bank [B, H, max_refs, D] (rolling window keep last); sim [B, H, N, tk] = matmul(k_4d, ref_kᵀ)/√D; top_idx [B, H, N, tk] → top_idx_exp gather refs [B, H, N, tk, D]; residuals stored sparse; read path reconstructs full K/V for attention; weight path: W [out, in] → low-rank ref + sparse S. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of reference-based deltas: a sequence where a repeated entity's later tokens point back to the earlier reference (arrows across long range) storing only the small difference (before/after value strips nearly identical → tiny delta bars); novel tokens fall back to fuller residuals (threshold slider); attention fidelity check (reconstructed vs true attention maps). ## Module: direct_svd (compressor; aliases: direct_svd, direct_svd_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Direct SVD Compressor — plain truncated SVD layer replacement, fully hardened. Pipeline: (1) target scan: Linear/Conv1D layers (PEFT base_layer unwrap, LoRA skip list lora_A/lora_B icon; last_part name matching; target_attention_only filter with non-target freezing note); (2) truncated SVD: W = UΣVᵀ → symmetric factors U_r = U√Σ, V_r = √ΣVᵀ (spectrum with rank cut; rank/min_rank/rank_ratio dials); (3) factorized layer replacement y = (xVᵀ)Uᵀ + b (true low-rank execution); (4) validation: W_recon = matmul(U_r, V_r) vs max_error with max_retries; fallback valve freezing originals; protect_critical shields. ## Module: dobi (compressor; aliases: dobi, dobi_svd)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Dobi-SVD — differentiable SVD with learnable spectral gating. Pipeline: (1) truncated SVD of pretrained weights into symmetric factors (as direct_svd); (2) the Dobi twist: a learnable per-singular-value gate vector g (init_gate_val dial, drawn as a dial on each spectrum bar) modulating the factors — forward y = x·(U·(V⊙g))ᵀ drawn as U [out, rank] and V [rank, in] with a per-component gate dial between them; (3) recovery fine-tuning: gates (and optionally factors) trained end-to-end through the differentiable product — the gradient flowing through the gate dials highlighted; init_rank_ratio/prank schedule note; (4) validation vs max_error with max_retries; fallback valve; target_attention_only freeze note; PEFT safety (base_layer/LoRA skip). ## Module: dynamic_rank (compressor; aliases: dynamic_entropy, dynamic_entropy_svd, dynamic_rank)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Dynamic Entropy SVD Compressor. Pipeline: (1) calibration hooks collecting activations per layer (x_flat); (2) entropy panel: activation covariance → singular values → normalized mass p_i = σ_i/Σσ_j → entropy \(H(\Sigma_l) = -\sum_i p_i\ln p_i\) per layer (entropy bars across the model — flat spectra = high entropy = hard layers); (3) rank allocator: budget r_l ∈ [r_min, r_max] distributed ∝ entropy under target_param_ratio/total_rank_budget (a budget bar split by entropy shares); (4) activation-aware whitened SVD: eigen-decomposition with clamping λ_i ≥ 1e-4 (stability badge) → symmetric factors → DynamicSVDLinear y = (xVᵀ)Uᵀ + bias (true low-rank); (5) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only freeze; PEFT safety. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: entropy \(H(\Sigma_l) = -\sum_i p_i\ln p_i\), \(p_i = \sigma_i/\sum_j\sigma_j\) (entropy curves for peaky vs flat spectra); allocation \(r_l \propto H_l\) within [r_min, r_max] under the budget; whitened SVD \(WC^{1/2} = U\Sigma V^\top\) with eigenvalue clamp λ ≥ 10⁻⁴; forward \(y = (xV^\top)U^\top + b\). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → cov [in, in] → eigen (clamped) → entropy H_l; budget → r_l per layer; U [out, r_l], V [r_l, in]; DynamicSVDLinear forward x [B, in] → x·Vᵀ → ·Uᵀ + bias; W_recon checks; per-layer rank table. ## Module: erc (compressor; aliases: erc, erc_svd)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ERC-SVD — Error-Controlled SVD with bounded Frobenius truncation error. Pipeline: (1) per-layer spectrum; (2) error-control loop: find the MINIMAL rank r such that relative Frobenius error ‖W − U_rV_r‖_F/‖W‖_F ≤ allowed_error — drawn as a descending-rank search (bisection arrows on the error-vs-rank curve, crossing the allowed_error line at the minimal r); (3) symmetric factors → replacement layer; (4) safety: max_error hard cap as a second gate, max_retries, Conv1D support, parameter bounds, fallback valve freezing originals; PEFT skip list; target_attention_only chip. The model-wide outcome: a per-layer rank table where hard layers got more rank, easy layers got less — "the error budget, not a fixed rank, decides." ## Module: faq (compressor; aliases: faq, faq_quantizer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of FAQ — Feature-Activation-Aware Quantization. Pipeline: (1) calibration: activation statistics collected per layer (flat_x; batch_cov = xᵀx); (2) activation-aware scaling: per-input-feature act_scale vector computed (a channel-importance strip along the input axis) — channels carrying large activations get finer quantization steps; layer.act_scale.copy_(act_scale.view(−1)[:in_f]) write node; (3) FAQLinear fake-quant node: weights quantized bit-wide (bits dial, symmetric option) with per-feature step scaling and STE backward (dashed gradient arrow); optional LoRA (lora_r, α); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip; PEFT skip list. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: activation-aware scale \(s_i = f(\mathbb{E}[x_i^2])\) (alpha-blended activation/weight statistics — α dial); quant \(\hat w_{ij} = s_j\,\mathrm{round}(w_{ij}/s_j)\) per input feature (finer grid where activations live — two grids compared: coarse uniform vs activation-shaped); STE. Visuals: activation histogram over channels, shaped quant grid, α dial. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → act_scale [in] (written to layer.act_scale); W [out, in] → per-feature scaled fake-quant [out, in]; FAQLinear forward x [B, in] → quant-matmul (STE) → [B, out]; LoRA branch; stats (bit width, error). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of activation-shaped quantization: a per-channel activation histogram (some channels hot) beside the quantization grid — uniform grid (red, coarse where it matters) vs FAQ grid (blue, finer over hot channels); the α blend dial between weight-range and activation-range statistics; error bar comparison. ## Module: flap (compressor; aliases: flap, flap_compressor) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of FLAP — Fluctuation-Based Adaptive Structured Pruning. Pipeline: (1) calibration: activation covariance XᵀX collected per layer (activations reshape [N, in]); (2) fluctuation scoring panel: per-input-neuron fluctuation scores computed from activation variance and gradient/weighted-error contributions (a score bar chart over input channels — "which inputs wobble the output most if removed"); metric_type toggle (wanda-like / FLAP fluctuation metric); (3) structured pruning decision: whole neurons/channels (columns of W) pruned by score under the sparsity target — a weight heatmap with entire purple columns removed (structured, not scattered); structured_24 option for 2:4 patterns; (4) residual/error compensation note (bias correction for the removed columns' mean activation contribution); (5) safety: LoRA/base_layer skip checklist, Conv1D support, target_modules/target_attention_only chips, validation vs max_error, fallback valve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: fluctuation metric \(s_j = \mathbb{E}[x_j^2]\cdot\|W_{:j}\|^2\)-style output-fluctuation contribution (adapting per metric_type); structured selection: prune whole columns j with smallest s_j until sparsity target; bias/mean correction \(b' = b + \bar{x}_{pruned}W_{:j,pruned}\) style compensation; validation gate. Visuals: per-channel fluctuation bars with cut line, column removal on the heatmap, correction arrow. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → scores [in] → sorted → threshold at (1−sparsity)·in → keep mask [in] → W[:, keep] [out, in′] structured slice + corrected bias; forward x [B, in] → x[:, keep] → W_kept matmul → [B, out]; stats (pruned neurons per layer). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of fluctuation-based selection: per-input fluctuation bars (high-variance inputs wobble outputs — kept; flat inputs pruned); the structured column removal on the weight heatmap (whole columns vanish, not scattered cells); the bias-correction arrow re-injecting the pruned mean contribution; magnitude-vs-fluctuation selection comparison (magnitude keeps a high-variance junk channel, FLAP removes it). ## Module: flat_llm (compressor; aliases: flat_llm, flat_llm_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of FLAT-LLM — activation-aware structured dimension/neuron pruning via eigen-decomposition. Pipeline: (1) calibration → activation covariance H = actᵀ·act/N (a covariance heatmap); (2) eigen-decomposition panel: top eigen-directions of the activation space (spectrum with keep_ratio cut — the input dimensions that carry the activations' energy); (3) structured pruning: input neurons/dimensions outside the kept eigen-subspace removed (whole input channels dropped on the heatmap — structured slices; damping dial regularizing the eigenvalues); structured_24 option; (4) downstream weight slicing: W columns aligned to pruned inputs removed (paired pruning arrows across two layers — "neuron pruning cascades"); (5) safety: LoRA/base_layer skip, Conv1D support, target chips, validation vs max_error, fallback valve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(H = X^\top X/N\); eigen-decomposition H = QΛQᵀ; keep subspace via keep_ratio on Λ (damped: λ/(λ+damping)); prune input dims with negligible eigen-energy; forward through the narrowed layer. Visuals: covariance heatmap, spectrum with cut, damped-eigenvalue curve, cascading slice arrows. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → H [in, in] → Q, Λ; keep mask [in] → W column slice [out, in′]; paired next-layer row slice; forward x [B, in] → x[:, keep] → W_kept → [B, out]; stats (kept dims per layer). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of eigen-based dimension pruning: the activation scatter's principal axes (Q columns); the low-energy directions dropped (dashed axes); the damping curve λ/(λ+δ) softening borderline decisions; the cascade — a pruned neuron's column removed in the current layer and its row in the next. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: frandi (compressor; aliases: frandi, frandi_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of FRanDI — Feature-Regression Aligned SVD. Pipeline: (1) calibration activations → covariance cov = xᵀx; (2) feature-regression alignment: a whitening transform L from the activation covariance (Cholesky/eigen factor — drawn as a coordinate stretch aligning the weight's input basis with the activation distribution); W̃ = W·L (whitened product node); (3) SVD of W̃ → factors mapped back into feature-regression-aligned bases → FRanDILinear: W ≈ B·A with B [out, rank], A [rank, in] (forward x·Aᵀ·Bᵀ; LoRA optional lora_r/α); (4) energy_threshold rank cut; (5) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv/target chips; PEFT safety. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: alignment transform L from Cov(X) (feature-regression basis); \(W̃ = WL\); SVD \(W̃ ≈ U_rΣ_rV_r^\top\); mapped factors \(B = U_rΣ_r^{1/2}\), \(A = Σ_r^{1/2}V_r^\top L\) (round-trip arrows drawn); forward \(y = xA^\top B^\top\). Note: factors aligned so regression error on real activations — not synthetic identity inputs — is minimized. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → cov [in, in] → L [in, in]; W [out, in] → W̃ = matmul(W, L) → SVD → B [out, rank], A [rank, in]; forward x [B, in] → x·Aᵀ [B, rank] → ·Bᵀ [B, out]; LoRA; W_recon = matmul(B, A) check. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of feature-regression alignment: activation covariance ellipse; the L transform stretching the input frame onto its principal axes; the weight factorized in that frame (top singular directions aligned with hot activation channels vs plain-SVD ghost); output-error bar on real calibration data, plain vs aligned. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: gfws (compressor; aliases: gfws, gfws_d)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of GFWSVD — Fisher/Covariance-weighted SVD. Pipeline: (1) calibration → per-layer batch covariance cov = xᵀx accumulated; (2) whitening node: M = W·Cov(X)^{1/2} (damping dial regularizing the covariance square root); (3) SVD of M → rank-r truncation → de-whitening candidate = M_r·Cov^{−1/2} (the round-trip drawn explicitly); (4) GFWSLoRALinear layer: frozen compressed base (from the de-whitened factors) + trainable LoRA (lora_r, α) — two-panel weight representation (frozen W base + adapter strips); (5) Fisher framing note: the covariance square-root acts as a Fisher-information metric on the input space; (6) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv/target chips. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(M = WC^{1/2}\), SVD M = UΣVᵀ, truncate, \(W_r = U_rΣ_rV_r^\top C^{-1/2}\); metric note ‖Δy‖² ≈ ΔWᵀCΔW — the covariance defines the natural geometry (Fisher view); damping for stability. Visuals: covariance ellipse, whitening round-trip, metric-space error gauge. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, in] → cov [in, in] → cov_sqrt/cov_inv_sqrt [in, in]; M = matmul(W, cov_sqrt) [out, in] → SVD → M_r; W_r = matmul(M_r, cov_inv_sqrt); GFWSLoRALinear forward x [B, in] → base + LoRA → [B, out]; error check. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the Fisher-metric view: output-error ellipses induced by weight perturbations (round under the covariance metric); SVD in Euclidean space cuts the wrong directions (ghost) vs the whitened SVD cutting directions the metric deems unimportant; damping curve on small eigenvalues. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: grecodec (compressor; aliases: grecodec, grecodec_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of GreCoDeC — Greedy Coordinate Descent low-rank compressor. Pipeline: (1) factor init (U [out, rank], V [rank, in]); (2) coordinate-descent loop drawn as alternating closed-form updates: U-step U = WVᵀ(VVᵀ)⁻¹ (given V, solved exactly — a closed-form solve node) then V-step V = (UᵀU)⁻¹UᵀW (given U); the iteration circle with reconstruction error ‖W − UV‖_F shrinking per sweep (max_iter dial); (3) GreCoDeCLinear layer y = x·Vᵀ·Uᵀ (LoRA optional); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv/target chips. Caption: "like ALS — but greedy coordinate sweeps with exact solves." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: U-step \(\min_U \|W - UV\|_F^2 \Rightarrow U = WV^\top(VV^\top)^{-1}\); V-step \(\Rightarrow V = (U^\top U)^{-1}U^\top W\); convergence gauge over max_iter sweeps; note: no SVD needed — works from any init, streaming-friendly. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in]; loop: VVt = matmul(V, V.t()) [r, r]; WV_t = matmul(W, V.t()) [out, r]; U = WV_t·VVt⁻¹; UtU/UtW → V update; GreCoDeCLinear forward x [B, in] → x·Vᵀ [B, r] → ·Uᵀ [B, out]; error per sweep. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the coordinate-descent cycle: U/V strips updating alternately (two-phase rotation diagram), the error curve descending over sweeps with the SVD one-shot error marked as the floor; a streaming note (updates never need the full W in memory at once beyond the products). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 5 — System Context & Complexity:** End-to-end pipeline. Badges: DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, Qwen/LLaMA. Inset: error vs sweeps; accuracy vs rank. ## Module: hashed_net (compressor; aliases: hashed_net, hashed_net_compressor, hashednets, hashednets_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the HashedNet Compressor — weight sharing via hash tables. Pipeline: (1) hashing node: each weight of the dense W assigned to one of n_buckets via a fixed hash of its (i, j) coordinates (a hash funnel: many weight cells → few bucket cells; compression_ratio dial); use_sign option (± sign kept outside the table); (2) HashedLinear layer drawn as: input x → hashed grouping (weights in the same bucket share one value — same-colored cells grouped) → grouped matmul → output; optionally rank-augmented (rank dial adds a low-rank path) and LoRA (lora_rank, α); freeze_hash_table toggle (snowflake); (3) validation vs max_error with max_retries; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip; PEFT skip list. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(W_{ij} = h(i,j)\)-shared: \(\hat W = S \odot \phi_H(b)\) where b is the bucket table, φ expands it by hash H, S optional ±1 signs; parameters ∝ n_buckets ≪ in·out (compression bar); collision note (hash collisions are tolerated — averaging in training). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: huffman (compressor; aliases: huffman)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Huffman Compressor — entropy coding over quantized weights and KV caches. Pipeline: (1) quantization node: continuous tensors quantized to 4/8-bit symbol grids (quantize_bits dial; a value axis with symbol cells); (2) symbol frequency modeling: histogram of quantized symbols (weights and KV values are bell-shaped — few symbols dominate); (3) Huffman tree construction: HuffmanNode tree drawn bottom-up — frequent symbols get short codes, rare symbols long codes (a binary tree with code lengths annotated beside the histogram); (4) coding simulation: HuffmanCompressor as a model-wide profiler — expected bits/symbol from the entropy H vs the fixed bit-width (savings bar); lossless note (exact reconstruction — reconstructed.reshape(shape) round-trip arrow); (5) safety: LoRA/base_layer skip, target_attention_only chip, max_error sanity, fallback valve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: symbol entropy \(H = -\sum_s p_s\log_2 p_s\) bits vs uniform log₂|alphabet| (two bars — the gap is the savings); Huffman optimality note (prefix codes within 1 bit of H); quantization as the lossy front-end + Huffman as the lossless back-end. Visuals: histogram, code tree, entropy-vs-fixed bar. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: tensor [out, in] → flat view → quantize to symbols → histogram p_s → tree build → codes → simulated packed size; reconstruction: decode → dequantize → reconstructed.reshape(shape) exact; per-tensor savings table (weights vs KV cache). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the Huffman tree: symbol histogram with a bell shape; the greedy merge steps building the tree (two smallest nodes merged repeatedly); resulting code lengths (1, 2, 3, 6 bits) vs fixed 8-bit cells; a KV-cache application panel (cache values quantized + Huffman-coded for memory). ## Module: hybrid_svd (compressor; aliases: direct_svd_encoder, hybrid_svd) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of the Hybrid SVD Layer — a single-head SVD attention replacement with positional encoding, plus a full encoder-layer replacement mode. Pipeline: (1) SVDSingleHead core: hidden_states L2-normalized (x_flat = F.normalize(reshape)) → the pretrained multi-head attention's stacked QKV space factorized by SVD: weighted_U = U[:,:k]·S[:k], output = weighted_U·Vh[:k,:] — the attention map itself approximated in a rank-k subspace (k = svd_rank; the multi-head ghost crossed out, one composite head drawn); sinusoidal positional encoding injected (a small sine-wave ribbon added to the normalized features); (2) DirectSVDEncoderLayer: the whole encoder layer with MHA swapped for SVDSingleHead + standard FFN (use_full_layer_replacement toggle) + LoRA (lora_r, α); (3) HybridSVDCompressor modes: linear-level SVD replacement OR architecture-safe full-layer replacement (mode switch); (4) validation W_recon vs max_error; fallback valve; PEFT safety; target_modules chips. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: normalized features \(\hat x = x/\|x\|_2\); attention-map approximation \(A \approx U_kΣ_kV_k^\top\) over the (H·d × H·d) joint attention subspace (weighted_U = U[:, :k]·S[:k]); positional encoding PE(m) sinusoidal added; layer output \(x' = \mathrm{SVDHead}(\mathrm{LN}(x)) + x\), FFN after. Note: compressing the attention operator itself, not just its projections. ## Module: hybridkv (compressor; aliases: hybridkv, hybridkv_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of HybridKV — hybrid KV cache compression for multimodal/long-context LLMs. Pipeline: (1) head classification node: attention heads profiled and split into static heads (stable, image-token-heavy — a shield icon) vs dynamic heads (context-sensitive) via static_heads_mask/static_ratio; (2) per-type policy panel: static heads → fixed long retention (their KV kept: anchors/sinks num_sinks + a long static span); dynamic heads → hierarchical budget: recent_window (exact recent tokens) + budget-limited older tokens selected by importance (topk) — two cache timelines drawn side by side with different fill patterns; (3) HybridKVCache manager: cache [B, H, N, D] maintained per head-type (k_cache[:1].expand clone nodes for sinks); max_cache_len cap per type; (4) note: attaches to attention layers without modifying weights (a clip-on adapter icon); (5) registry aliases ('hybridkv', 'hybridkv_compressor'). ## Module: kmeans (compressor; aliases: kmeans, kmeans_compressor, kmeans_quantize)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the KMeans Compressor — vector quantization of weights. Pipeline: (1) weight flattening (flat = tensor.view(−1)); (2) k-means clustering node: weights clustered into n_clusters centroids (a value axis with centroid markers and weight points assigned to nearest centroid — 1-D clustering sketch; bits ≈ log₂(n_clusters) label); (3) KMeansLinear layer: forward(centroids, indices, x, bias) — weights replaced by (centroid index, lookup) pairs drawn as an index map into a small codebook; KMeansSTEFunction autograd node — forward looks up centroid values by index, backward passes gradients directly to the centroids (dashed gradient arrow from all assigned weights into their centroid); optional LoRA (lora_r, α); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only/skip_attention_qkv chips. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: k-means objective \(\min_{\{c_k\},\{a_{ij}\}} \sum (W_{ij} - c_{a_{ij}})^2\); STE: forward \(\hat W = c_{a(W)}\), backward \(\partial\hat W/\partial c_k = \mathbb{1}[a(W) = k]\) (gradients of all members accumulate on the centroid); bits/symbol = log₂K; compression bar n_clusters vs in·out. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in] → flat [out·in] → k-means → centroids [K], indices [out, in]; KMeansLinear forward x [B, in] → gather(centroids, indices) → Ŵ → matmul + bias → [B, out]; STE backward: grad_W [out, in] → flat_grad_W.scatter-add into centroids [K]; stats (K, bits, error). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of centroid gradient accumulation: a value axis with K centroid markers; each weight dot connected to its nearest centroid; the STE backward drawing all member gradients into their centroid (arrows converging on each marker) so centroids drift to minimize output loss; histogram of weight values with K cell boundaries. ## Module: kronecker (compressor; aliases: kronecker, kronecker_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Kronecker Compressor — replacing Linear/Conv1D with Kronecker-product layers. Pipeline: (1) factorization: W ≈ A ⊗ B drawn as the Kronecker grid (a big out×in matrix tiled by scaled copies of a small block B, scaled by A's entries — the classic block-tiled Kronecker picture with the two small factor strips A [a1×a2], B [b1×b2] beside it); (2) KroneckerLinear forward: x reshaped and contracted through A and B separately (never materializing the full W — "matmul with A and B only" note); (3) fallback path: if the Kronecker fit error exceeds max_error, adaptive low-rank SVD fallback (a valve switching to the SVD lane); LoRA (lora_r, α) optional; (4) validation; critical layer protection chips; Conv1D support; target filters. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(W = A\otimes B\) with \(W_{(i_1i_2)(j_1j_2)} = A_{i_1j_1}B_{i_2j_2}\) (block-tiled grid graphic); parameters a1a2 + b1b2 ≪ out·in (savings bar); forward via reshaped contractions; error gate. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in] → reshape to block structure [a1, b1, a2, b2] → least-squares fit of A, B → KroneckerLinear forward x [B, in] → reshape [B, b2, a2] → contract B then A → [B, out]; W_recon = kron(A, B) check; fallback valve to SVD lane. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Kronecker-TT-LoRA — hybrid PEFT/compression adapter. ## Module: kvtc (compressor; aliases: kvtc, kvtc_quantizer)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of KVTC — KV Cache Transform Coding (PCA decorrelation + adaptive quantization + entropy coding). Two panels: (A) runtime KV store: incoming K/V flattened [N, D] → PCA decorrelation: k_trans = k·pca_basis_k (rotating the feature frame onto uncorrelated principal axes — before/after covariance ellipses: correlated ellipse → axis-aligned); adaptive per-axis quantization (scale_k dials per principal axis, bits dial) → entropy coding of the quantized symbols (SimpleEntropyCoder — Huffman-style prefix codes); reconstruct k_hat = (q·scale)·pca_basisᵀ; (B) weight-side: KVTCQuantizedLoRALinear — projection weights compressed with the same PCA-quantize-entropy chain (frozen base + LoRA lora_r/α); pca_energy dial choosing how many axes to keep; (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target chips. Paper badge: "KV Cache Transform Coding, ICLR 2026". Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: PCA \(C_K = \mathbb{E}[k k^\top] = Q\Lambda Q^\top\); transform \(z = Q^\top k\) (decorrelated); quantize \(q = \mathrm{round}(z/s)\) per axis (adaptive s); entropy code the q symbols (H(p) bits vs fixed bits); reconstruct \(\hat k = Q\,(q\odot s)\). Visuals: correlated → decorrelated ellipses, per-axis scale dials, entropy bar vs fixed bits. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: K [B, H, N, D] → k_flat [N, D] → k_trans = matmul(k, pca_basis_k) [N, D′] → q_k [N, D′] ints → entropy codes; reconstruct k_hat = matmul(q_k·scale_k, pca_basis_k.t()).reshape(orig); weight path analogous; stats (pca_energy kept, bits/symbol achieved). ## Module: liftr (compressor; aliases: liftr, liftr_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LIFTR — Linearized Function Trick compressor. Pipeline: (1) activation linearization: _LinearizedGELU replacing GELU/SiLU with f(x) ≈ α·x (the nonlinear curve vs its best linear approximation drawn, α = gelu_coefficient dial); (2) attention linearization: _LinearizedAttentionWrapper replacing quadratic softmax attention with sequence pooling — context = v.mean(dim=1) expanded (the softmax curve replaced by a flat mean pooling icon; the N×N ghost crossed out and replaced by a mean-arrow); split qkv handling for fused weights; (3) output-level calibration validation: compressed model's outputs compared against the original on calibration data (validation_threshold gauge — pass/fail valve); (4) LoRA preservation (lora_A/lora_B untouched), target_attention_only chip; fallback valve. Caption: "the extreme compressor: linearize everything." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: GELU(x) ≈ αx (tangent-line graphic with slope α); softmax attention → mean pooling \(O \approx \mathbf{1}\,\mathrm{mean}(V)\) (constant attention row graphic); validation \(\frac{1}{N}\|y_{comp} - y_{orig}\| \le \tau\). Honest-cost note: drastic simplification, validated empirically. ## Module: lillama (compressor; aliases: lillama, lillama_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LiLLaMA — Local Distillation via activation cross-covariance SVD. Pipeline: (1) hooks capturing BOTH inputs and outputs of each layer (inp_flat, out_flat); (2) cross-covariance node: C = outᵀ·inp / N — the input-to-output response matrix of the layer as actually exercised (a cross-covariance heatmap, not the weight matrix); (3) SVD of C → W_new = U[:, :r]·S[:r]·Vh[:r, :] — a distilled replacement weight aligned to the layer's empirical transfer behavior (distillation funnel icon: original layer's behavior → compressed layer); (4) replacement + optional LoRA; (5) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention/target_attention_only chips. Caption: "distill what the layer does, not what its weights look like." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: cross-covariance \(C = \frac{1}{N}\sum_t y_t x_t^\top\) (layer response operator); SVD C ≈ U_rΣ_rV_rᵀ → replacement W_new = U_rΣ_rV_rᵀ; note: for a linear layer C ∝ W in expectation, but on finite calibration data the cross-covariance SVD down-weights directions never exercised (data-driven regularization). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: inputs [N, in], outputs [N, out] → C [out, in] = outᵀ·inp/N → SVD → U [out, r], S [r], Vh [r, in] → W_new = matmul(U·S, Vh); swap; forward x [B, in] → W_new x → [B, out]; error check on calibration outputs. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: lsc (compressor; aliases: lsc, lsc_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of LSC — Low-Rank Spectral & Fourier-Domain compressor. Pipeline: (1) the weight matrix W treated as a 2-D image; 2D DFT node: W → frequency domain (a log-magnitude spectrum with energy concentrated at low frequencies — the classic spectral decay image); (2) two-domain pruning: low-rank spectral truncation (rank dial) AND Fourier-domain filtering (energy_ratio dial zeroing high-frequency coefficients — flat_mag = magnitude.reshape(−1) thresholding on |F(W)| cells); (3) LSCPrunedLoRALinear: frozen spectral-filtered base + LoRA (lora_r, α); forward via the inverse transform implicit in the stored filtered representation; (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target chips. Caption: "compress weights where their energy lives: low frequencies + top singular directions." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: 2D DFT \(\mathcal{F}(W)[u,v] = \sum_{ij}W_{ij}e^{-2\pi i(ui/m + vj/n)}\); keep top-energy coefficients by |F| (energy_ratio) and/or truncate the spectral (SVD) domain; reconstruction \(W_r = \mathcal{F}^{-1}(M \odot \mathcal{F}(W))\). Visuals: log-spectrum heatmap with kept (purple) vs zeroed (gray) cells, energy-vs-coefficient curve. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in] → fft2 → magnitude [out, in] → flat_mag → threshold keep mask → filtered spectrum (complex) → inverse fft2 → W_filtered; LSCPrunedLoRALinear forward x [B, in] → W_filtered x (+LoRA) → [B, out]; error check. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: mgie (compressor; aliases: mgie, mgie_svd)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of MGIE-SVD — Maximum Information Entropy rank selection. Pipeline: (1) per-layer spectra; (2) entropy criterion panel: for each candidate rank r, compute the information entropy of the truncated spectrum — select the rank where the retained singular distribution carries maximum information (an entropy-vs-r curve with its peak marked — the MGIE cut point; contrast markers for fixed-rank and energy-threshold cuts); min_rank floor; (3) truncated SVD → replacement layers (LoRA optional lora_r/α); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv/target chips. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(p_i^{(r)} = \sigma_i/\sum_{j\le r}\sigma_j\) over the retained spectrum; \(H(r) = -\sum_{i\le r}p_i\ln p_i\); choose \(r^* = \arg\max_r H(r)\) (peak-of-entropy cut — the retained spectrum maximally "informative"/balanced); rationale: peaky retained spectra waste rank on redundant directions. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in] → SVD → σ spectrum → H(r) scan → r* → U_r/V_r → forward through factors; W_recon check; per-layer r* table. ## Module: nmf (compressor; aliases: nmf, nmf_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the NMF Compressor — non-negative matrix factorization. Pipeline: (1) sign handling: W split into a shift plus a non-negative part (W_pos = W + shift; shift dial — drawn as the heatmap lifted into positive territory); (2) multiplicative-update NMF loop: H, V ≥ 0 iterated — HTW = HᵀW_pos, HTHV etc. (the classic MU update arrows in a cycle, max_iter dial; all factors drawn clamped ≥ 1e-10); W ≈ H·V + shift (two non-negative strips); (3) NMFLinear forward x·Vᵀ·Hᵀ + shift·x + bias (LoRA optional lora_r/α); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target chips. Caption: "parts-based, non-negative factors — unlike SVD's signed directions." ## Module: oats (compressor; aliases: oats, oats_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of OATS — Outlier-Aware Training & Saliency-Based Sparsification with low-rank decomposition. Pipeline: (1) calibration: activation second moment M = XᵀX/N (covariance heatmap); (2) scaling node: W_scaled = W·L_M where L_M = M^{1/2} (whitening-style scaling — the round-trip with L_M⁻¹ drawn); (3) outlier-aware split: the scaled weight decomposed into a low-rank part L (top singular directions, rank_ratio dial) and a sparse residual S — R = W_scaled − L; sparse entries chosen by magnitude saliency (flat = R.abs() threshold; sparsity dial; outliers drawn as tall spikes in the residual histogram surviving the threshold) — optionally structured_24; (4) de-scale: W_comp = (L + S)·L_M⁻¹; (5) OATSLoRALinear (frozen decomposed base + LoRA lora_r/α); (6) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv chip. Caption: "low-rank for the smooth mass, sparse for the outliers." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

## Module: res_svd (compressor; aliases: res_svd)

Pipeline: (1) frozen base: W_base = U_rΣ_rV_rᵀ at base_rank (spectrum with base cut, snowflake); (2) residual init funnel: the tail E_tail = W − W_base re-decomposed; its top residual_rank directions seed the trainable adapters: (B·A)·γ = E_tail — "the adapter is born from the discarded spectrum" (init arrows from the tail spectrum into A/B; γ = scaling dial); (3) ResSVDLinear forward: base = U_rV_r; res = scaling·B(Ax); y = base(x) + res; PEFT wrappers (SpectralResidualLoRA, registered variants) drawn as chips on one implementation; (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. ## Module: rotational_slicegpt (compressor; aliases: rotational_slicegpt, rotational_slicegpt_int4)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of Rotational SliceGPT INT4 — rotation + slicing + second-order INT4 quantization combined. Pipeline: (1) orthogonal rotation node: W̃ = W·Q, X̃ = QᵀX (a coordinate-frame rotation glyph — invariance of the layer function under orthogonal Q); (2) variance-based slicing: in the rotated frame, slice the lowest-variance coordinates — P_r selection matrix keeping slice_ratio of axes (spectrum of per-axis activation variance with a cut; W_sliced = W̃·P_r, X_sliced = P_rᵀX̃); (3) second-order INT4 quantization on the sliced weights (AstroQuantizer non-uniform codebook badge — 2/4/8-bit Gaussian-aware centroids; quant_bits dial); (4) RotationalSliceGPTINT4Linear: forward y = W_quant·(Q·P_r)ᵀx + bias — the fused rotation+slicing projector Q_P_r = Q·P_r drawn once (x_proj = x·Q_P_r then quant-matmul); (5) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; lambda_reg for rotation optimization. Caption: "rotate → slice → quantize: three compression levers in one pass." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

Visuals: rotation frame, variance spectrum with slice cut, codebook axis, fused projector block. ## Module: rpca (compressor; aliases: rpca)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the RPCA Compressor — Robust PCA: W = L + S. Pipeline: (1) decomposition loop: alternating updates splitting W into a low-rank L (thin spectrum strips) and a sparse outlier S (a heatmap with a few tall purple spikes; mu penalty dial, max_iter loop; the classic RPCA inexact-ALM cycle drawn as a two-way iteration between L and S estimates); (2) factorization of L: the recovered low-rank component further factorized via structural SVDLinear (true low-rank execution); (3) forward: y = L(x) + S handled at compression time (S folded into weights or kept sparse); LoRA optional (lora_r, α); (4) validation W_rec = U·V vs max_error; fallback valve; skip_attention_qkv/target chips. Caption: "smooth mass → low rank; sharp exceptions → sparse." ## Module: slicegpt (compressor; aliases: slicegpt, slicegpt_compressor) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of SliceGPT — deleting rows and columns via orthogonal rotation. Pipeline: (1) calibration: activation covariance cov = XᵀX/N collected per layer (a covariance heatmap); (2) orthogonal rotation node: eigenvectors Q of the covariance → rotate the coordinate frame: W̃ = W·Q, X̃ = QᵀX (invariance glyph: the layer computes the same function in the rotated frame — rotation arrows); (3) slicing node: delete the lowest-variance coordinates in the rotated frame — P_r keeps the top (1−sparsity) axes (variance spectrum with the cut; non_uniform option: per-layer sparsity variation); columns of W̃ and rows of the incoming activations dropped in sync (paired scissor icons on both sides of the layer — "delete rows AND columns consistently across the network"); (4) rotate back / or keep rotated: W_pruned = W_sliced·Qᵀ (round-trip arrows); (5) safety: validation vs max_error, fallback valve, target_modules/target_attention_only chips. Caption: "the network itself becomes narrower — every subsequent layer shrinks too." Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: orthogonal invariance \(Wx = (WQ)(Q^\top x)\); slice \(W_s = WQ\,P_r\) with P_r = [I_r; 0] selection; activations sliced identically \(x_s = P_r^\top Q^\top x\); variance ranking from cov(X); consistency condition across consecutive layers (the sliced width propagates). Visuals: rotation frame, spectrum cut, paired slicing arrows, propagation chain. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: activations [N, D] → cov [D, D] → Q; W [out, D] → W̃ = matmul(W, Q) → W_sliced [out, D′] (D′ = (1−sparsity)·D); propagate: next layer's input dimension now D′ (its rows sliced too); forward x [B, D] → x̃ = x·Q → x[:, :D′] → W_sliced → [B, out]; error check. ## Module: sparsegpt (compressor; aliases: sparsegpt) [FLAGSHIP]

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Publication-grade flat vector schematic of SparseGPT — one-shot layer-wise second-order pruning with OBS weight compensation. Pipeline: (1) Hessian collection: per-layer activations → empirical Hessian H = 2XᵀX (a covariance-style heatmap labeled "empirical Hessian"); percdamp diagonal loading dial (H + λI); (2) column-sweep OBS engine drawn as a left-to-right block sweep: for each block of weights (block_size), compute the inverse Hessian block, rank weight saliency by second-order loss ΔL = w²/(2[H⁻¹]_{jj}), mask the bottom (sparsity or prunen:m N:M pattern — prunen/prunem dial) and immediately UPDATE all remaining weights in the row to compensate the removed column's contribution: update = err_block·(invH_diag ⊙ H_cross) — the compensation arrows flowing left-to-right across the weight grid (weights to the right of each removed column adjusted); (3) structured_24 option for tensor-core patterns; (4) output: sparse weights + binary mask; (5) validation on calibration data vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. ## Module: spectral_residual_lora (compressor; aliases: spectral_residual_lora)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Spectral Residual LoRA alias (res_svd.py, PEFT-focused key). Pipeline: (1) frozen base \(W_{base} = U_rΣ_rV_r^\top\) at base_rank (spectrum with base cut, snowflake); (2) residual-adapter init: the tail \(E_{tail} = W − W_{base}\) re-decomposed; its top residual_rank singular directions seed the trainable adapters — \((B\cdot A)\cdot\gamma = E_{tail}\) (init arrows from the tail spectrum into A/B; γ = scaling dial); (3) ResSVDLinear forward: base = matmul(base_U, base_V); res = scaling·matmul(lora_B, lora_A); y = base-path + res-path; PEFT chips (SpectralResidualLoRA, ResSVDCompressorRegistered, SpectralResidualLoRARegistered — one implementation, multiple registered views); (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. ## Module: swift_svd (compressor; aliases: swift_svd) [FLAGSHIP]

Caption: "the fast path: one SVD, symmetric factors, strict error gate." ## Module: tomoe (compressor; aliases: tomoe, tomoe_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ToMoE — converting dense layers into Mixture-of-Experts by weight clustering. Pipeline: (1) clustering node: the dense weight's neurons clustered into num_experts groups (a weight heatmap with column groups colored by cluster — k-means-style grouping of neurons; Conv1D (nx, nf) layout handled); (2) ToMoELinear layer: each expert = the sub-block of neurons for its cluster (E expert blocks drawn as narrow strips of the original matrix); learned router: token features → topk over experts (topk_vals → softmax → topk_weights [N, top_k]; topk_idx → expanded_idx gather); (3) sparse dispatch: index_copy/scatter assembling the mixed output from selected experts' columns (dest_cols/src scatter node drawn); freeze_experts toggle; LoRA (lora_r, α) on routers/adapters; (4) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target chips. Caption: "the same parameters, re-organized so only top-k experts run per token." ## Module: tt (compressor; aliases: tt)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of the Tensor-Train Compressor — replacing Linear/Conv1D with TTLinearLayer. Pipeline: (1) reshaping node: the weight matrix W [out, in] reshaped into a multi-dimensional tensor (e.g., [d1, d2, d3] × [d1′, d2′, d3′] — a cube drawn from the matrix); (2) TT decomposition: the cube expressed as a CHAIN of small 3-way cores G1 → G2 → G3 with TT-rank wires between them (rank dial for the wire widths; parameter count Σ cores ≪ full tensor — a savings bar); (3) TTLinearLayer forward: input reshaped, contracted through the core chain left to right (the chain drawn with shapes annotated at each contraction), output reshaped back; adaptive TT-rank retry (max_retries) against the Frobenius error gate; (4) LoRA optional (lora_r, α); validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: TT format \(W_{(i_1i_2i_3)(j_1j_2j_3)} = \sum G_1[i_1,j_1,r_1]G_2[r_1,i_2,j_2,r_2]G_3[r_2,i_3,j_3]\) (chain graphic with rank wires r₁, r₂); parameters Σ s_k·r's vs Π dims; forward as sequential contraction; error gate. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in] → reshape cube → TT-cores G_k; forward x [B, in] → reshape to index grid → contract G1 → [.., r1] → G2 → [.., r2] → G3 → reshape [B, out]; W_rec check via TT reconstruction; stats (tt_rank, params). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the TT chain: the weight cube with its core chain (three small boxes and two rank wires); the rank wire widths as the compression dial (thinner wires = fewer parameters); a TT-rank sweep error curve; the reshape cube↔matrix glyph. ## Module: uniql (compressor; aliases: uniql)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of UniQL — joint low-rank + uniform quantization. Pipeline: (1) two-lever panel: truncated SVD (rank dial) AND uniform min-max quantization (bits dial) applied to the SAME weight; (2) joint search: adaptive (rank, bits) retries against the relative Frobenius error gate — drawn as a small 2-D grid of (r, b) candidates with the search arrows moving until err ≤ max_error or max_retries exhausted; best_w_rec chosen (a star on the winning cell); (3) weight assignment: w_to_assign = best_w_rec.t().contiguous() written back into the module (dequantized low-rank-quantized product; Conv1D t() layout handled); (4) fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: \(\hat W = Q_b(U_rV_r)\) (quantized low-rank reconstruction); joint objective \(\min_{r,b} \|\hat W_{r,b} - W\|_F\) under memory r(in+out)·b/8 ≤ budget; retry grid graphic. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: W [out, in] → per candidate (r, b): SVD → quantize → W_rec → error; select best; write-back with transpose handling; forward through dequantized factors; stats (chosen r/b per layer). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 4 — Unique Mechanism Close-up:** Close-up of the joint (rank, bits) grid: error surface over r and b; the search path; the winning cell starred; comparison bar: rank-only, bits-only, joint at equal memory. ## Module: zerokv (compressor; aliases: zerokv, zerokv_neo, zerokv_neo_compressor)

**Image Prompt 1 — Base Architecture Diagram:** Flat vector schematic of ZeroKV-Neo — L2-norm-based KV cache eviction with sink and heavy-hitter preservation. Pipeline: (1) scoring node: per-token KV importance from L2 norms (‖k_t‖, ‖v_t‖ bars along the cache axis); (2) budget partition: the cache split into three zones — attention sinks (first tokens, num_attention_sinks shielded slots, k_cache[:1].expand clone), heavy hitters (top heavy_hitter_ratio by L2 score, topk selection → idx_exp gather), recent window (last recent_window tokens exact); (3) ZeroKVNeoCache manager: rebuilt cache = cat([sink_k, sel_mid_k, win_k]) (the three-zone timeline drawn with shielded head, purple selected middle, dense recent tail); bounded O(1) memory note (max_cache_len cap; safe dimension handling badge); (4) weight-side pruning option preserving sinks/heavy hitters; (5) validation vs max_error; fallback valve; target_attention_only chip. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 2 — Mathematical Core:** Equation panel: importance \(I_t = \|k_t\|_2 + \|v_t\|_2\); zones \(\{sinks\} \cup \mathrm{TopK}_{HH}(I) \cup \mathrm{recent}_w\); memory \(O(s + HH + w)\) = O(1) in t; eviction of mid-band low-norm tokens. Visuals: L2 bars with three-zone cut lines, shielded sinks, compacted timeline. Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.

**Image Prompt 3 — Tensor Flow & Shape Trace:** Shape trace: KV [B, H, N, D] → norms [B, H, N] → sinks [s], heavy hitters topk [hh] (idx_exp gather), window [w]; rebuild k_cache = cat([sink_k, sel_mid_k, win_k], dim=2).contiguous() [B, H, s+hh+w, D]; sliding update as t grows (drop-from-window, re-select mid); stats (cache size flat vs N). Style: clean flat vector technical illustration, light blue (#60A5FA) and purple (#A78BFA) palette on white background with subtle light-gray engineering grid, thin precise dark-slate arrows, small legible sans-serif annotations, rounded-rectangle compute blocks, no photorealism, no heavy gradients, 16:9.
